# Product updates

> New updates and product improvements

**November 2026**

### Translate threads to English

You can now read a non-English thread in English without translating each message. The **Thread** layout translates the thread's messages and span names on your device with the built-in translation API in desktop Chrome or Edge, so translation doesn't use model credits. See [Translate message content](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#translate-message-content) for details.

### Pattern rules

Pattern rules tell Loop what to investigate in each scheduled Patterns run. A rule can narrow its scope to specific traces with a SQL filter and list problems for Loop to watch for. Loop suggests problems based on your recent traces, and it still reports other problems it finds. See [Tune a pattern rule](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/enable.md#tune-a-pattern-rule) for details.

### bt CLI releases

* [v0.31.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.31.0) - Adds [OpenCode 2 tracing](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md#set-up-tracing), which requires `@braintrust/trace-opencode` v2.2.0 or later. [`bt trace run opencode`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-run) checks the OpenCode version before launching it and runs OpenCode 2 with a private server.
* [v0.30.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.30.1) - [Muse Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/muse-code.md#what-braintrust-traces) turn spans include the images attached to a prompt, in live sessions and in sessions you import or attach to. Fixes coding-agent spans failing to upload when they have repeated tags. This release includes the changes from v0.30.0, which wasn't published.
* [v0.29.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.29.0)

  * **Optional MCP telemetry**: [`bt mcp run`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/mcp#telemetry-argument) no longer requires the `telemetry` argument, so you can call a Braintrust MCP server tool with only its own arguments.
  * **Continue a trace from a run**: [`bt trace run`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-run) accepts `--parent` with the value returned by an SDK span's `export()`, attaching the coding-agent session below that span instead of starting a new trace.
  * **Coding-agent tracing**: [Cursor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/cursor.md#what-braintrust-traces) subagent spans are nested under the turn that started them and record the task as a user message. [OpenCode](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md#what-braintrust-traces) model call spans record the selected model variant in `metadata.variant`. Hook capture stays responsive while the background tracing process recovers earlier events, preventing hook timeouts in long sessions.

### Terraform provider releases

* [v1.0.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/terraform-provider-braintrust/releases/tag/v1.0.0) - The [Braintrust Terraform provider](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/terraform.md) is generally available. `braintrust_view` accepts `patterns` as its `view_type`, so you can manage saved views for [Patterns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns.md) as code.
* [v0.0.9](https://github.com/braintrustdata/terraform-provider-braintrust/releases/tag/v0.0.9) - [AI secret and environment variable values](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/terraform.md#supply-secret-values) are write-only, so Terraform no longer stores them in state or plan files. Adds an [ephemeral `braintrust_service_token` resource](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/terraform#manage-service-tokens) that creates a service token without storing its key in state or plan files, and a [`braintrust_users` data source](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/terraform#2-look-up-members) that looks up many members in one request. **Breaking change**: Requires Terraform or OpenTofu 1.11.1 or later. **Breaking change**: `braintrust_ai_secret` requires `secret_version` with `secret`, and `braintrust_env_var` requires `value_version` with `value`. Add a version to each configured value, and increment it to write a new value, because changing only the value no longer updates it. References to `secret` or `value` return `null`. **Breaking change**: Removes the `braintrust_org_automation` resource, which the Braintrust API no longer supports. Delete its blocks from your configuration, then run `terraform state rm` on each address, because `terraform plan` fails while state still contains the resource.

### Python SDK releases

* [v0.49.3](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.49.3) - Fixed [remote evals](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/playgrounds.md#max-concurrency) ignoring the **Max concurrency** setting from playgrounds, [`Eval()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/api-reference#eval) and `EvalAsync()` failing when `data` is an `async` function or other awaitable, and [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) and [LangGraph](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/langgraph.md) spans being marked as errors when you stop reading a stream early. With [`BRAINTRUST_OTEL_COMPAT=true`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/opentelemetry/link-spans#share-context-within-a-process), a span that ends in a different async context than it started in no longer logs an error. Type checkers infer that [`wrap_mcp_client()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/mcp-sdk#manual-instrumentation-python) returns the same client type you pass to it.
* [v0.49.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.49.2) - [Temporal](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/temporal.md#what-traced-python) activity spans record the retry attempt as `temporal.activity_attempt`, cancellations are marked with `temporal.cancelled: true` instead of logged as errors, and trace context carries across continue-as-new. Spans opened in workflow code nest their activities and child workflows and aren't logged again on replay. [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-python) Responses API spans record hosted tool usage as `tool_usage`, `instructions` as a `system` message, and string `input` as a `user` message. Added `Cursor Context Summary` spans to [Cursor SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cursor-sdk.md#what-traced-python) tracing and fallback model attempts to [Pydantic AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md#what-traced-python) tracing, where agent spans now record the model that answered. Fixed [Gemini](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md#what-traced-python) continuation requests producing one span per request, streamed OpenAI Chat Completions merging multiple choices, and [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md) parallel tool spans losing their outputs.

### TypeScript SDK releases

* [v3.43.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.43.0) - Added [MCP server tracing](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/mcp-sdk.md#fastmcp-typescript) for `fastmcp` and `mcp-handler` servers, including the new `wrapFastMCP()` and `wrapMCPHandler()` functions. Tracing supports newer versions of [Google ADK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/google.md), [Cloudflare Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cloudflare-agents.md), [Cloudflare AI Chat](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cloudflare-ai-chat.md), [Cloudflare Think](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cloudflare-think.md), [Mistral](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/mistral.md), [LangGraph SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/langgraph.md), [LangSmith](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langsmith.md), and [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md), and [Gemini](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md#what-traced-typescript) interactions with `@google/genai` v2.9.0 and later. [Cursor SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cursor-sdk.md#what-traced-typescript) traces include subagent tool calls, and [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md#what-traced-typescript-auto) traces follow LiveKit's PII redaction settings. Fixed [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-typescript) Decisions spans to use the `decision` span type, duplicate tool spans in AI SDK `HarnessAgent` traces, [remote evals](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/playgrounds.md#max-concurrency) ignoring the playground's concurrency limit, and UTF-8 text in logged `ReadableStream` byte chunks.

### Go SDK releases

* [v0.20.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.20.0) - [Firebase Genkit](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/firebase-genkit.md#tracing-flows-go) flows defined with `tracegenkit.DefineFlow`, `tracegenkit.DefineStreamingFlow`, `tracegenkit.NewFlow`, or `tracegenkit.NewStreamingFlow` record their input and output on the flow span, and Orchestrion auto-instrumentation replaces the matching `genkit` calls. `context.span_origin.instrumentation.name` identifies the integration that created each span, such as `openai` or `genkit`, instead of `braintrust-go` for every span. The [`openapi` API client](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/api-reference#api-client) is regenerated from the latest Braintrust OpenAPI specification.
* [v0.19.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.19.1) - Fixes a data race on the `braintrust.org_name` and `braintrust.app_url` span attributes when multiple goroutines start spans at the same time. The recommended [Orchestrion](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/install-and-instrument.md#configure-tracing) version for auto-instrumentation is v1.14.0, which requires Go 1.26 or later. On Go 1.25, keep using v1.12.1.

### Java SDK releases

* [v0.3.28](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.28) - [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md#tracing-java) tracing supports Anthropic Java SDK 3.x, and `BraintrustAnthropic.wrap()` accepts asynchronous clients. Fixes duplicate spans with Anthropic Java SDK v2.72.0 and later, which turns on its own tracing by default. Streaming spans are marked as errors when the stream returns an error event or fails mid-read. Fixes streaming `time_to_first_token` leaving out the wait for response headers, so values are more accurate.
* [v0.3.27](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.27) - Fixed [Bedrock](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock.md#tracing-java) tracing with AWS SDK for Java v2.57.0 and later. Fixed [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md#what-traced-java) MCP connector tool calls producing a child span named `mcp_tool` with no input. Each call now produces one `tool` span named after the tool, with the call's input and the result's output. Fixed [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-java) Responses API spans dropping `instructions` and string `input`. The span input now records `instructions` as a leading `system` message and string `input` as a `user` message. [Gemini](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md#what-traced-java) spans now record thinking tokens as `completion_reasoning_tokens` and include them in `completion_tokens`, so `completion_tokens` is higher for thinking models than in earlier versions.

### C# SDK releases

* [v0.2.15](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-dotnet/releases/tag/v0.2.15) - Updated the [generated API client](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/csharp/api-reference.md#api-client) to the latest Braintrust API.

### @braintrust/pi-extension releases

* [v2.2.4](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.2.4-pi) - Fixes Oh My Pi skipping the extension without an error, which left Oh My Pi sessions untraced. In Oh My Pi, the status line shows `Braintrust: connected` without a trace link. See [Configure the tracing status display](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md#common-workflows) for details. Run `bt trace update pi` to update.
* [v2.2.3](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.2.3-pi) - The trace link appears in pi's status line as a clickable **Braintrust trace ↗** link instead of a separate widget below the editor. The status reads `Braintrust: connected` instead of `Braintrust: tracing` until the link is available. In terminals where pi doesn't detect hyperlink support, the status stays `Braintrust: connected` and doesn't show the trace URL. See [Configure the tracing status display](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md#common-workflows) for details. Run `bt trace update pi` to update.

### @braintrust/trace-opencode releases

* [v2.2.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.2.0-opencode) - Adds tracing for [OpenCode 2](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md#set-up-tracing) sessions, including tool calls, subagents, resumed sessions, and context compactions. OpenCode 1 tracing is unchanged. Tracing OpenCode 2 requires `bt` v0.31.0 or later. Run `bt trace update opencode` to update.

### Improvements

* The search dropdown's **Recent** section now includes structured filters alongside SQL filters, so you can reapply a recent filter without rebuilding it. See [Filter and search logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/filter.md) for details.
* [Patterns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns.md#how-discovery-works) (public preview) now finds problems your users notice, such as replies they correct or complain about, even when every tool call succeeds.
* [Patterns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#how-impact-is-counted) measures impact in affected users or sessions when your project logs them, so one user with many traces doesn't overstate a problem. Loop can find and save your project's [end-user ID field](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/enable.md#count-impact-by-user), and Slack alerts report the same unit. You can compare a pattern with all traces or only similar ones.
* Coding agents can now list the past runs of a scheduled [Loop automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#review-past-runs) with `list_scheduled_loop_job_runs` on the [Braintrust MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md#manage-automations-and-alerts). Each run includes a link that opens it in Braintrust.
* In human review, you can now read traces in the [new thread view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#use-the-new-thread-view) (public preview) and collapse the scores panel to give the trace more room. See [Change the trace layout](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review/score-traces.md#change-the-trace-layout) for details.
* The **Reasoning effort** dropdown in prompts and playgrounds now lists each model's supported values, including `xhigh` and `max` for newer GPT models, and starts at the model's default. See [Evaluate reasoning models](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/reasoning.md#configure-reasoning).
* Organization-level Loop threads can now push branches and open pull requests through the [GitHub integration](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/github.md#how-write-access-works) (public preview). They use the organization's default GitHub installation for reads and writes, regardless of attached projects.
* Organization owners can now see whether members on their email domain have turned on two-factor authentication. See [View member 2FA status](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#view-member-2fa-status) for details.
* Dashboard filter, time range, and grouping changes now stay unsaved until you save them, so exploring a dashboard no longer changes it for other viewers. See [Filter and group data](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards.md#filter-and-group-data) for details.
* Organization owners can now revoke members' API keys through the API with `DELETE /v1/api_key/{api_key_id}`. See [Manage API keys](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#manage-api-keys) for details.
* Removing a member from your organization now also deletes their API keys for the organization, so any application or script that uses those keys loses access. See [Remove members](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#remove-members) for details.
* Deleting a scorer now removes it from the online scoring rules that run it and permanently deletes any rule left with no scorers. See [Create scoring automation rules](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#create-scoring-automation-rules) for details.

**October 2026**

### Trace Muse Code sessions

Trace [Muse Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/muse-code.md) sessions, including turns, model calls, tool calls, and subagents. Run `bt trace enable muse` to set up tracing, which requires `bt` v0.28.0 or later and Muse Code 1.3.0 or later. See [Muse Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/muse-code.md) for details.

### MCP server tracing

The Python SDK v0.49.1 or later and TypeScript SDK v3.42.0 or later support [MCP tracing](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/mcp-sdk.md), capturing tool calls, prompt requests, resource reads, and server notifications. The Python SDK also traces outgoing MCP client requests, including standalone FastMCP, with trace context propagated between clients and servers.

See [Trace and improve your MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/mcp-servers.md) to learn how to instrument your server and use Loop and Patterns to find recurring problems in how agents use your tools.

### Async queries

SQL queries can now run asynchronously, so large exports and long-running reads don't have to finish within a single synchronous request. Braintrust runs an async query in the background and stores the results, and you fetch them in pages. Run async queries with the [API](https://docs.braintrust.dev/api-reference/query.md#async-queries) or with [`bt sql --async`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/sql#async-queries).

> [!NOTE]
>
> Async queries are only available on the [Enterprise plan](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#plans).

### Terraform provider

The [Braintrust Terraform provider](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/terraform.md) manages your organization's configuration as code, including projects, permission groups, access control, AI provider secrets, environment variables, automations, prompts, and functions. Changes go through code review and stay consistent across environments. Install it from the [Terraform Registry](https://registry.terraform.io/providers/braintrustdata/braintrust/latest/docs).

> [!WARNING]
>
> The Terraform provider is in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Thread summaries

The [new thread view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#generate-thread-summaries) can show Loop-generated headings for each turn and a summary of the whole conversation, so you can scan a long agent trace before reading it. Select a phrase in the summary to jump to the turn it describes.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Thread summaries are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Continuous Loop automations

Scheduled [Loop automations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md) can run in continuous mode. Between scheduled runs, Loop checks for new traces and, when enough arrive, analyzes them in the current run's thread. Busy projects get findings sooner without starting a new investigation each time.

### GitHub repositories in Loop

Connect [GitHub repositories to Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/github.md) to investigate failing traces alongside your source code. Loop can find the relevant implementation, explain likely causes, and suggest targeted fixes and regression tests. Access is read-only by default, but you can enable [write access](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/github.md#give-loop-write-access) to let Loop push fixes and open pull requests.

> [!WARNING]
>
> The GitHub integration is in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Reorganized settings

\*\* Settings\*\* groups organization, project, and personal settings by task in one sidebar and merges related pages. For example, \*\* Models\*\* holds organization and project AI providers and Loop's model settings, and \*\* Permissions\*\* holds permission groups, project permissions, and SCIM as tabs. Search the Settings sidebar or the command bar to jump to a setting. Links to previous settings pages redirect to the reorganized pages. See [Manage organizations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md) for details.

### Inference activity on the billing page

The [**Inference activity**](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/monitor-usage.md#view-usage) section of the billing page shows how your Braintrust Gateway token usage over the last 7 days splits across features, such as Online scoring, Topics, and Loop, and across your top projects, so you can see what drives your inference usage.

### Add rows to human review with MCP

The Braintrust MCP server can now send project log traces, experiment traces, or dataset rows to [human review](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review/score-traces.md) with the [Braintrust MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md#tools), and optionally assign them to reviewers. Loop automations can queue rows for review on a schedule when you allow the tool in their [write tool permissions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#write-tool-permissions).

### Customize pattern severity and categories

You can customize the [severity and category rubric](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/enable.md#customize-severity-and-categories) that Loop uses across a project's Patterns automations and conversations. Define your own categories and severity criteria to match what matters to your team.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Patterns is in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Pattern severity and categories

Patterns assigns [severity](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#severity) so you can prioritize findings by impact and filter the list, and [categories](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#categories) to describe the kind of finding, such as a tool failure or safety and privacy risk. You can correct either with an optional reason, and Loop preserves your corrections in later investigations.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Patterns is in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### PagerDuty notifications for automations

Send notifications from [Loop automations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#destinations) and [log, time window, and environment alerts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/alerts.md) directly to PagerDuty. Configure an integration key and incident severity for each destination.

### Coding agent insights

Coding agent insights shows what your team's coding agents cost, who uses them, and where they could work more effectively. A built-in dashboard at \*\* Dashboards\*\* > [**Coding agent insights**](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/agent-insights) shows usage by user, API key, model, tool, and branch, with cost breakdowns for all except tools. A daily Loop automation finds recurring problems and inefficiencies across your team's sessions.

To learn more, see:

* [Improving coding agents with coding agent insights](https://docs.braintrust.dev/cookbook/recipes/CodingAgentInsights.md)
* [Coding agent insights dashboard](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/coding-agent-insights.md)

> [!WARNING]
>
> Coding agent insights is in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### New thread view

The new thread view organizes a trace by conversation turn. Each turn shows the request, the work the agent did to answer it, and the final response, so you can follow a long agent trace without reading every intermediate message. See [Use the new thread view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#use-the-new-thread-view) for details.

> [!WARNING]
>
> The new thread view is in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### SQL charts

Build dashboard charts from a SQL query, and switch between the query text and a visual builder that edits the same query. Braintrust adds the dashboard's timeframe, filters, and grouping when the chart renders, so the same chart works on any dashboard you paste it into. You can also chart a query's results in the [\*\* SQL sandbox\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/sql) and save a chart to a dashboard. See [SQL charts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/sql-charts.md) and [Chart a query in the SQL sandbox](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/sql-charts.md#chart-a-query-in-the-sql-sandbox) for details.

> [!WARNING]
>
> SQL charts are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

> [!NOTE]
>
> SQL charts are only available on [Pro and Enterprise plans](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#plans).

### Loop on dashboards

Open [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md) from an individual dashboard or the chart editor to investigate data and build or refine charts in natural language. The dashboard or chart is added to your conversation's context automatically. See [Build charts with Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md#build-charts-with-loop) for details.

### Copy logs into datasets with MCP and Loop

Build datasets from existing project log rows with the [Braintrust MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md#run-evals-and-edit-datasets) or [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/capabilities.md#curate-datasets). Copied rows preserve their input, metadata, tags, and links to the source logs without passing their full contents through the assistant's context.

### Trace Cursor sessions

Trace [Cursor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/cursor.md) agent sessions from the Cursor CLI and desktop app, including turns, model calls, tool calls, and subagents. Run `bt trace enable cursor` to set up tracing, which requires `bt` v0.26.0 or later. See [Trace Cursor sessions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/cursor.md#trace-cursor-sessions) for details.

### Redesigned permission groups

You can now rename a permission group and edit its description, permissions, and members in one sheet. The permission groups list shows each group's member count, so you can check membership without opening each group. You grant access to a project group from a permission group instead of from the project group's settings. See [Edit or delete a permission group](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control/manage-permissions.md#edit-or-delete-a-permission-group) and [Set project group permissions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control/manage-permissions.md#set-project-group-permissions) for details.

> [!NOTE]
>
> Editing a permission group's name, description, and permissions is only available on the [Enterprise plan](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#plans).

### Reorganized navigation

On desktop, the left navigation groups project pages under **Observe** and **Evaluate**, and you can collapse it to an icon-only rail with  or the `[` keyboard shortcut. The organization and project switchers move to the breadcrumbs at the top of each page, the project overview is labeled **Home**, and the SQL sandbox is renamed [**Query**](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql.md#sql-sandbox).

### Duplicate prompts and scorers in bulk and across projects

Select up to 100 prompts or scorers in the **Prompts** or **Scorers** table and duplicate them in one step, or duplicate a single prompt or scorer from its header or from a saved version in its **Activity** tab. Copies can go into the current project or any project where you can create and update prompts. Each copy's **Activity** links to the source version it came from and notes whether it included unsaved editor changes. See [Duplicate prompts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/prompts/manage.md#duplicate-prompts) for details.

### Go workflow evaluations

Go evaluations (SDK v0.18.0+) can now send their tasks and scorers to asynchronous provider APIs, such as batch APIs, which providers often offer at a discount. Start a run, then resume it later by polling or from a webhook. With a Redis store, a different process can resume the run. Braintrust records the results in a single experiment. See [Workflow evaluations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/workflow-evaluations.md) for details.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Go workflow evaluations are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### TypeScript application logs

[Application logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/application-logs.md) are now available in the TypeScript SDK (v3.39.0+). Emit messages with a logger's severity methods such as `info()` and `error()`, or forward existing `console` output with `wrapConsole()`, then inspect them alongside your traces.

> [!WARNING]
>
> TypeScript application logs are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Default provider for Loop

Set the provider that new [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md) threads and automations use across your organization: Braintrust built-in models or one of your AI providers. Loop uses that provider's recommended model, and members can still pick another for any thread or automation. See [Set a default provider for Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#set-a-default-provider-for-loop).

### Account billing across organizations

Organizations that Braintrust links under one contract share a billing account. Owners, and members with the **View account billing** permission, can see the account's billing period, billing email, and linked organizations from the billing page. See [View account billing](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing.md#view-account-billing) for details.

### bt CLI releases

* [v0.28.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.28.1)

  * **Codex command errors**: [Codex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md#what-braintrust-traces) tool spans record an error when a shell command exits with a nonzero code, even if the tool call as a whole completes, and record each command's result in `metadata.command_outcomes`.
  * **Docs fetch**: [`bt docs fetch`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/docs) recognizes documentation page links on `docs.braintrust.dev` in the docs index, in addition to `www.braintrust.dev/docs`.

* [v0.28.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.28.0)

  * **MCP tools from the CLI**: [`bt mcp`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/mcp) (public preview) lists and runs the tools exposed by the [Braintrust MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md) without an MCP client, with a generated flag for each tool argument or with JSON input.
  * **[Muse Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/muse-code.md) tracing**: [`bt trace enable muse`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-enable) traces Muse Code sessions, turns, model calls, tool calls, and subagents. `bt trace run muse` traces a single invocation, and `bt trace import muse` imports past or active sessions. Requires Muse Code 1.3.0 or later.
  * **Codex imports**: [`bt trace import codex`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-import) imports sessions whose transcripts Codex has compressed into `.jsonl.zst` files. You can't follow a compressed session with `--attach`.
  * **Tracing fixes**: [OpenCode](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md#what-braintrust-traces) tool spans keep images that tools return, so they render as images in the trace. When a [pi](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) turn starts from a custom message instead of a typed prompt, the turn records that message as its input.

* [v0.27.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.27.2)

  * **Codex hook activation**: [`bt trace enable codex`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex#set-up-tracing) and `bt trace update codex` trust the Braintrust hooks in Codex, so you no longer approve them through `/hooks`.
  * **Span plugin timeouts**: [Span plugins](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace.md#transform-spans-with-javascript) have 1 second instead of 50 milliseconds per call, and `bt` retries span updates that time out twice before holding them. [`bt trace doctor`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-doctor) lists held sessions and what `bt` is waiting for before it sends their spans.
  * **Hook executable**: Set `BRAINTRUST_BT_BIN` to an absolute path to have `bt trace hook` [capture events with that `bt` executable](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#troubleshooting) instead of the one on `PATH`.
  * **Trace delivery**: The background tracing process sends spans in batches of up to 30 seconds and frees delivered span data, so long sessions use less memory. Spans are sent right away when a session ends.

* [v0.27.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.27.1)

  * **Eval fixes**: [`bt eval`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval) no longer requires a Go toolchain to run a [prebuilt Go eval binary](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval.md#runtime-configuration) passed with `--runner`, and reports the actual cause when Go eval discovery fails.
  * **TLS trust update**: `bt` distrusts certificates affected by the [eMudhra (emSign) certificate authority compromise](https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/).

* [v0.27.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.27.0)

  * **Topics settings in observability templates**: [`bt observability template`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/observability) templates bundle each facet's Topics automation settings, such as scope, filter, sampling, and model, so a push recreates them in the destination project. With `--force`, a push replaces a facet's topic map binding only in the selected Topics automation.
  * **Reliable coding-agent trace delivery**: `bt` saves coding-agent spans to disk and retries failed uploads, including after a restart. When a [span plugin](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace.md#transform-spans-with-javascript) fails, `bt` holds the affected spans until you fix the plugin instead of dropping them.
  * **Richer coding-agent traces**: Every span records the plugin and agent versions in `context.span_origin`. [Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md#what-braintrust-traces) and [Codex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md#what-braintrust-traces) turns record how they ended in `metadata.outcome`, and interruptions are no longer recorded as errors. Claude Code also records the effort level and compactions, [Cursor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/cursor.md) subagent spans nest under the tool call that started them, and [pi](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) sessions record the working directory and model.
  * **Eval fixes**: [`bt eval`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval) finds Go evals from any folder in a Go module, rejects Go packages that don't import `evalrunner`, shows runner errors when output isn't a terminal, such as in CI, and fixes the automatic `vite-node` retry for TypeScript evals.
  * **Tracing fixes**: Fixes missing Cursor traces on Windows, shows the arguments a Claude Code tool actually ran with, and ends Codex tool spans that never receive output.
  * **Claude Code changes**: `AskUserQuestion` spans are task spans instead of tool spans. Update queries that select them as tool spans. Resuming a session that already ended starts a new trace with the same `session_id`.

* [v0.26.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.26.0)

  * **Preprocessors from the CLI**: [`bt preprocessors`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/preprocessors) bootstraps, previews, and pushes preprocessors written in TypeScript or JavaScript with `customPreprocessor()` from `braintrust/preprocessors`. You can also use it to list, view, invoke, and delete published preprocessors. Preview runs a local preprocessor against a real trace without publishing it.
  * **Scorer preprocessors**: [`bt scorers create --preprocessor`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/scorers#preprocessors) selects the preprocessor that builds a prompt-based scorer's trace template variables. It accepts a pushed preprocessor's slug, a built-in preprocessor, a local file, or `none`.
  * **Environment variable names**: Command-specific environment variables use the `BRAINTRUST_` prefix, matching the Braintrust SDKs. For example, `BT_EVAL_WATCH` is now `BRAINTRUST_EVAL_WATCH`, and coding-agent tracing's `BT_DAEMON_CONFIG` is now `BRAINTRUST_DAEMON_CONFIG`. The `BT_` names still work as deprecated aliases. See the [v0.26.0 migration instructions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate.md#v0-26-0).
  * **[Cursor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/cursor.md) tracing**: [`bt trace enable cursor`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-enable) adds capture hooks to Cursor's `hooks.json`, so sessions are traced even when Cursor doesn't load the local plugin.
  * **Coding-agent tracing**: [Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md), [Codex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md), and [pi](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) session spans record the final response of the last completed turn, and pi turn spans record each turn's final response. Codex traces keep images in prompts and tool results, so they render as images in the trace.

* [v0.25.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.25.0)

  * **Turn attribution**: Coding-agent traces record `metadata.turn_span_id` on each turn span and every span under it, including subagent spans, so you can filter or aggregate a turn's spans without walking the trace tree.
  * **Span origin versions**: Coding-agent session and turn spans record the plugin, `bt`, and coding agent versions in `context.span_origin`, captured when each span is created. Unknown versions are `null`. See the integration page for your agent, such as [Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md#what-braintrust-traces).

* [v0.24.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.24.0)

  * **Plugin updates**: [`bt update`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/update) now updates your installed tracing plugins too, even if `bt` itself is already up to date. Restart your coding agents to pick up the new plugin versions.
  * **Windows hooks**: Fixes hooks that could hang when starting the background tracing process.

* [v0.23.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.23.0)

  * **[pi](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) tracing**: Nests pi 1.0 codemode tool calls under the calling tool, and records virtual model selections, request settings, and tool definitions on model call spans. **Breaking change**: On pi model call spans, `reasoning_tokens` is now `completion_reasoning_tokens`, `cost` is now `estimated_cost`, `metadata.provider_request` is replaced by top-level fields such as `temperature` and `tools`, and skill loads use `tool_name: "read"` with `tool_kind: "skill"`. Update queries that use the earlier fields.
  * **Long session IDs**: Fixes missing traces for sessions with very long IDs.
  * **Span metadata**: **Breaking change**: Spans no longer include `metadata.bt_daemon_version`.

* [v0.22.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.22.2)

  * **Windows credential storage**: On Windows, `bt` stores profiles and credentials in one DPAPI-encrypted file, `%USERPROFILE%\.braintrust\auth\credentials.dpapi`, so commands run from a terminal and from packaged (MSIX) desktop apps share the same logins. The first command that reads saved logins after upgrading migrates them automatically. After downgrading to an earlier `bt` version, log in again. See [Authentication and profiles](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/overview.md#authentication-and-profiles) for details.
  * **Project groups in `bt functions push`**: When `bt functions push` creates a missing project, it places it in the [project group](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control/manage-permissions.md#manage-project-groups) set on the project with `projectGroupName` (TypeScript SDK v3.36.0+) or `project_group_name` (Python SDK v0.44.0+). Existing projects are not moved. See [`bt functions push`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/functions#bt-functions-push) for details.
  * **[Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md) tracing**: Nests subagent results under the turn that started the subagent instead of recording them as a separate turn span.
  * **Hook errors**: `bt trace hook`, which agent plugins run for each event, exits nonzero when it can't capture the event, such as when tracing settings are invalid or the background tracing process is unreachable. See [Troubleshooting](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace.md#troubleshooting) for details.
  * **Private tracing files on Windows**: Tracing settings files, the tracing data directory, and the background tracing process's named pipe are accessible only to the current user and SYSTEM, matching the owner-only permissions on macOS and Linux. On every platform, `bt trace import` keeps its data directory private.
  * **No console windows on Windows**: Background tracing no longer flashes a console window when it runs helper commands such as `git`.

### Python SDK releases

* [v0.49.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.49.1) - [MCP content capture configuration](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/mcp-sdk.md#capture-content-python) moves from `auto_instrument()` to `MCPIntegration.setup(capture_content=False)` or the per-object wrappers. Pass `client=True` to `MCPIntegration.setup()` to configure clients as well as servers.

* [v0.49.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.49.0) - Added tracing for [MCP clients and servers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/mcp-sdk.md#auto-instrumentation-python), including standalone FastMCP, with automatic trace context propagation between clients and servers. Log uploads now batch nearby rows with a default 1-second delay in long-lived processes and no delay in detected serverless environments, configurable with [`BRAINTRUST_FLUSH_DELAY_MS`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/api-reference#environment-variables).

* [v0.48.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.48.1) - Added tracing for [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md#what-traced-python) beta tool runners and message threads. [Cohere](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/cohere.md#what-traced-python) rerank spans record `relevant_spans`, [Harbor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/harbor.md#what-received-python) omits scores for rewards that Harbor marks invalid, and [workflow evaluations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/api-reference.md#workflowtask-and-workflowscorer) retry a failed `submit` on the next `poll()`. Fixed Anthropic `messages.create(stream=True)` streams failing outside a `with` block and their spans not ending when closed early, [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-python) Decisions API generator inputs, and [Pydantic AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md#what-traced-python) reasoning token metrics from normalized usage. Anthropic streaming `time_to_first_token` now measures time to the first output content.

* [v0.48.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.48.0) - [LiteLLM](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/litellm.md#what-traced-python) traces System One calls (`litellm.systemone` and `litellm.asystemone`) as `decision` spans and logs media passed in `images`, `assets`, `audio`, or `videos` as attachments. [OpenRouter](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openrouter.md#what-traced-python) spans record `safeguard_results` and `alignment`, and [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md#what-traced-python) spans record the requested `speed`. [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md#what-traced-python) hook spans keep newer input fields such as `error` and `duration_ms`, and sub-agent runs on older bundled Claude Code CLIs create task spans. Fixed missing token metrics on [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-python) Responses API streams that end as incomplete or failed, and Claude Agent SDK orchestrator turns being nested under a running sub-agent. The SDK requires `certifi` v2026.10.19 or later, which distrusts certificates affected by the [eMudhra (emSign) certificate authority compromise](https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/), and the wheel no longer ships the SDK's test modules. **Breaking change**: [TypeSafe](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/typesafe.md#what-traced-python) decision spans record images as `<omitted>` unless [`BRAINTRUST_CAPTURE_ATTACHMENTS`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/api-reference#environment-variables) is set to `1` or `true`.

* [v0.47.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.47.0) - [TypeSafe](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/typesafe.md#what-traced-python) decision spans now capture box answers and images. Images in the state, instructions, criteria, and `assets` are logged as attachments, and when the request contains exactly one image, box answers record its location as `image_path`. Fixed [Pydantic AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md#what-traced-python) tool calls that are deferred for approval or external execution being logged as errors.

* [v0.46.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.46.1) - Base64 data URL images in [DSPy](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/dspy.md#what-traced-python) spans and [LiteLLM](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/litellm.md#what-traced-python) completion messages are now logged as attachments, and [Instructor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/instructor.md#what-traced-python) `create_partial` spans log only the final partial model as `output`. Fixed `error_score_handler` not running for every failed row in [`Eval()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/api-reference#eval) and being ignored by `EvalAsync()`, tags added through `hooks.tags` leaking into other trials, rows, or the dataset, and attachments and other nested values in logged dataclasses not being serialized. Also fixed [Gemini](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md#what-traced-python) Interactions API calls failing when `response_format` is a Pydantic model class, streamed Gemini code execution parts being logged without their content, [Pydantic AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md) spans dropping message fields, and [OpenAI Agents SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/openai-agents-sdk.md) tracing modifying the response's `metadata`. Reduced repeated project lookups and span serialization overhead.

* [v0.46.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.46.0) - [Span customizers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/redact-sensitive-data.md#opentelemetry-spans) now also run on OpenTelemetry spans, and a customizer that raises no longer drops the record. Added tracing for the [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-python) and [LiteLLM](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/litellm.md#what-traced-python) Decisions APIs as `decision` spans, the [OpenRouter](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openrouter.md#what-traced-python) GA Responses API, and [Google ADK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/google.md) workflow nodes and tool calls. [TypeSafe](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/typesafe.md#what-traced-python) spans now use the `decision` type. [Mistral](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/mistral.md#what-traced-python), [Pydantic AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md), and [Gemini](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md#what-traced-python) spans capture more request and response details. Fixed Google ADK model calls counting token usage twice, and OpenAI prompt cache write tokens being left out of cost and token totals. Added Python 3.15 support.

* [v0.45.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.45.0) - Added `cancel_event` (`threading.Event`) to [`Eval()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/api-reference#eval) and `EvalAsync()` to stop a running evaluation. Tasks can read it from `hooks.cancel_event`, and a `timeout` also sets it. Added [`WorkflowBatchTask` and `WorkflowBatchScorer`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/api-reference#workflowbatchtask-and-workflowbatchscorer) to submit many cases in each provider batch in [workflow evaluations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/workflow-evaluations.md). Workflow evaluation types can be imported from the top-level `braintrust` package, and [`process_submission_result()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/api-reference#workfloweval) no longer requires a `run_id` when you pass an `external_id`. [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md#what-traced-python) spans now record the `diagnostics` request parameter, plus `cache_miss_reason` and `cache_missed_input_tokens` in metadata when Anthropic reports a prompt cache miss. [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-python) Responses API spans now record `access_programs` in metadata, and Agents API sessions now trace computer use calls (with screenshots as attachments), browser authentication requests, and computer use approval items. Fixed duplicate spans when calling `wrap_openai()` after `auto_instrument()`, and [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md) tracing swallowing task cancellation, including `asyncio.wait_for()` timeouts, in `query()` and `receive_response()`.

* [v0.44.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.44.1) - Reduced the SDK's CPU overhead when queuing and sending logs, and log batches now use the full request size limit instead of half of it, so the SDK sends fewer requests. `span` in `with start_span(...) as span` now type-checks as `Span` under pyright and mypy, and `add_braintrust_span_processor()` parameters are fully typed. [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md#what-traced-python) MCP connector tool calls now appear as child tool spans named after the MCP tool, with the tool input (including in streamed responses), instead of generic `mcp` spans. Fixed streamed [Agno](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/agno.md#workflows-python) workflow output including step progress events, and [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-python) Agents API sessions not being traced when you collect results with `get_final_result()` or `with_result_collection()`.

* [v0.44.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.44.0) - [Span customizers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/redact-sensitive-data.md#span-customizers) now run on all native SDK spans, including spans you create with `traced()`, `start_span()`, and `logger.log()`, not only spans from integrations and wrappers. Dataset rows and feedback are still excluded. With `BRAINTRUST_OTEL_COMPAT` enabled, the SDK logs an error instead of installing customizers. Added `project_group_name` to [`projects.create()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/projects#create-a-project) to create a new project inside an existing project group. [Agno](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/agno.md) agent and team run spans record `cancellation_stage` in metadata (Agno v3.0.11 or later), [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md) run spans record `verbatim_prompts` when verbatim prompt mode is enabled (Claude Agent SDK v0.2.158 or later), and [AgentScope](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/agentscope.md) `TeamPipeline.reply_stream()` calls are now traced (AgentScope v2.0.9 or later). Fixed token metrics from [DSPy](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/dspy.md) LM history, STT usage metrics for [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md), audio duration usage for [Pydantic AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md), classifier usage metrics for [Pipecat](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pipecat.md), and iterable `tools` metadata for [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md). Integer environment variables with empty or non-integer values now fall back to their defaults instead of raising an error.

### TypeScript SDK releases

* [v3.42.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.42.0) - Added [MCP server tracing](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/mcp-sdk.md) for servers built with `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` v1 or `@modelcontextprotocol/server` v2, through auto-instrumentation or `wrapMCPServer()`. Tool and prompt arguments, results, and notification parameters are recorded by default. To keep the spans but omit this content, set [`mcp.captureContent`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/mcp-sdk#capture-content-typescript) to `false`. Notifications that the server sends, such as progress updates and log messages, are traced, and spans record the client and server names and versions from the `initialize` exchange. On HTTP transports without a session ID, spans after `initialize`, such as `notifications/initialized`, don't record `mcp.protocol.version`. Read the version from the `initialize` span instead. [Pi Coding Agent](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pi-coding-agent.md#what-traced-typescript) tool spans record `metadata.tool_approval`, and spans created while a tool runs nest under the tool span.
* [v3.41.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.41.0) - [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md#audio-attachments-typescript-auto) records the caller's and the agent's audio separately with `BRAINTRUST_CAPTURE_USER_AUDIO_ATTACHMENTS` and `BRAINTRUST_CAPTURE_AGENT_AUDIO_ATTACHMENTS`, and `session.close()` resolves after the recording finishes uploading. Install [`@braintrust/audio`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/related/audio) to reduce the size of uploaded recordings. Fixed tool tracing for [Eve](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/eve.md) 0.72 and later. Fixed missing finish reasons on streamed [Gemini](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md) responses that end without content. **Breaking change**: `BRAINTRUST_CAPTURE_ATTACHMENTS` no longer records LiveKit Agents audio. Set the two LiveKit variables instead.
* [@braintrust/audio v0.1.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/%40braintrust/audio%400.1.0) - Initial release. [`@braintrust/audio`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/related/audio) reduces the size of uploaded [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md#audio-attachments-typescript-auto) call recordings. Requires `braintrust` v3.41.0 or later and Node.js 20 or later. The package is in early development (alpha) until v1.0, so expect backwards-incompatible changes between releases.
* [v3.40.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.40.0) - Added Cloudflare [Workers AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/cloudflare.md#cloudflare-workers-ai-typescript) tracing for Clef and Clairic calls with `wrapWorkersAI()`. Long-lived Node.js processes now batch log rows for up to 1 second, configurable with [`BRAINTRUST_FLUSH_DELAY_MS`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/api-reference#environment-variables), so call [`flush()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/api-reference#flush) before `process.exit()` or in signal handlers. AI SDK tool spans log [`toModelOutput`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel#what-traced-typescript) results. [Pi Coding Agent](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pi-coding-agent.md#what-traced-typescript) LLM spans capture system prompts and tools, and auto-instrumentation supports Pi 1.x. Fixed double-counted Pi Coding Agent token totals by recording token metrics only on LLM spans, not on `AgentSession.prompt` task spans. [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md#what-traced-typescript-auto) traces include `livekit.<name>` lifecycle spans. Fixed auto-instrumentation with [TypeScript loaders](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/trace-llm-calls.md#auto-instrumentation) such as `@poppinss/ts-exec`.
* [v3.39.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.39.0) - Added [application logs](#typescript-application-logs). Added tracing for [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md#auto-instrumentation-typescript) v1.9.x pipeline sessions, AI SDK [batch requests](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel.md#trace-batch-requests-typescript) and [code mode](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel.md#trace-code-mode-typescript), and [Cloudflare Think](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cloudflare-think.md) up to v0.20. Fixed output and token usage for streamed AWS Bedrock calls to OpenAI models, merged streamed tool calls from OpenAI, OpenRouter, and Hugging Face, and cache tokens missing from [Pi Coding Agent](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pi-coding-agent.md) and [Flue](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/flue.md) `prompt_tokens`. Internal SDK errors now print only when [`BRAINTRUST_DEBUG_LOG_LEVEL`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/api-reference#environment-variables) is set, which replaces `DEBUG=@braintrust*`.
* [@braintrust/otel v1.1.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/%40braintrust/otel%401.1.0) - [Span customizers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/redact-sensitive-data.md#opentelemetry-spans) now also run on OpenTelemetry spans that `BraintrustSpanProcessor` and `BraintrustExporter` export, and in OpenTelemetry compatibility mode. Requires `braintrust` v3.38.0 or later.
* [v3.38.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.38.0) - Added tracing for [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-typescript) Decisions API calls and AI SDK [`experimental_decide()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/typesafe#manual-instrumentation-ai-sdk-typescript) calls, including AI SDK decision telemetry callbacks. [TypeSafe](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/typesafe.md#what-traced-typescript) spans capture image inputs, and decision spans capture inline media as attachments when `BRAINTRUST_CAPTURE_ATTACHMENTS` is enabled. A failed [span customizer](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/redact-sensitive-data.md#customizer-failures) now uploads only the record's protected fields and timestamps instead of dropping the record, and customizers run on OpenTelemetry spans with `@braintrust/otel` v1.1.0 or later. Empty `BRAINTRUST_API_URL` and `BRAINTRUST_PROXY_URL` values are ignored. Fixed tree shaking for browser and edge builds, and queued logins replacing active credentials. Rebuild bundles that use an [auto-instrumentation bundler plugin](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/trace-llm-calls.md#auto-instrumentation) after upgrading. TypeSafe spans use the `decision` span type instead of `question`. Base64 images in OpenAI Responses API inputs are recorded as `<omitted>` unless `BRAINTRUST_CAPTURE_ATTACHMENTS` is enabled.
* [v3.37.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.37.2) - Fixed `ReactNode` type conflicts in React 19 projects that use `braintrust/custom-views`. `@types/react` is now an optional peer dependency and is no longer installed for every consumer.
* [v3.37.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.37.1) - Fixed the `braintrust/vite` [auto-instrumentation plugin](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/trace-llm-calls.md#auto-instrumentation) crashing during Vite 8 dependency optimization.
* [v3.37.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.37.0) - Added `signal` to [`Eval()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/api-reference#eval) task hooks for cooperative cancellation. When an evaluation times out or its `signal` aborts, it aborts `hooks.signal`, stops starting new cases, and waits for running tasks and scorers to finish before it rejects, instead of rejecting right away and leaving tasks running in the background. A `timeout` of `0` now times out immediately. Added [`WorkflowBatchTask` and `WorkflowBatchScorer`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/workflow-evaluations) to submit many cases in each provider batch in workflow evaluations. Workflow evaluations also retry a failed `submit` on the next `poll()`, and `processSubmissionResult()` accepts an `externalId` without a `runId`. Added support for [Eve](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/eve.md) 0.62 and later, and Eve turns now nest under the caller's span when the caller propagates trace context. [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md) agent run spans now include sub-agent token usage across all models, `estimated_cost`, and `total_cost_usd` metadata. Fixed missing output and token metrics on [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md) Responses API streams that end with `response.incomplete` or `response.failed`, and the error is now logged for `response.failed`.
* [v3.36.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.36.0) - [Span customizers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/redact-sensitive-data.md#span-customizers) now run on all native SDK spans, including spans you create with `traced()`, `startSpan()`, and `logger.log()`, not only spans from instrumentation and wrappers. Dataset rows and feedback are still excluded. Span customizers aren't supported in OpenTelemetry compatibility mode. Eval and function parameters now accept Zod v4 schemas as well as Zod v3. Added `projectGroupName` to [`projects.create()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/projects#create-a-project) to create a new project inside an existing project group. Fixed empty `BRAINTRUST_NUM_RETRIES` and `BRAINTRUST_DEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE` environment variables being read as `0`, missing reasoning text from streamed [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md)-compatible responses that return `reasoning_content`, and missing model names on streamed Anthropic calls traced through [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md).

### Go SDK releases

* [v0.19.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.19.0) - Added the [`openapi` package](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/api-reference#api-client), a REST API client generated from the Braintrust OpenAPI specification that covers projects, experiments, datasets, prompts, functions, project automations, function invocation, and more. Added `prompt.LoadWithClient` and `eval.NewEvaluatorWithClient`, which take an `*openapi.Client`. The handwritten `api` package, `Client.API()`, `prompt.Load`, and `eval.NewEvaluator` are deprecated but keep working. Use `openapi` for new code. `Client.LoadPrompt` and `braintrust.NewEvaluator` aren't deprecated.
* [v0.18.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.18.0) - Added [workflow evaluations](#go-workflow-evaluations). Added `InsertEvents` to the [experiments API client](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/api-reference.md#api-client) to insert or merge experiment rows. Fixed [`bt eval --max-concurrency`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval#evaluator-concurrency) having no effect on Go evals, which ran evaluators one at a time.
* [v0.17.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.17.2) - [`bt eval --no-send-logs`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/advanced-evaluations#run-without-uploading-results) now runs Go evals locally without logging in, creating an experiment, or uploading spans, and the new [`NoSendLogs`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/api-reference#evaluations) option on `eval.Opts` and `eval.RunOpts` does the same in code. [Google ADK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/google.md#what-traced-go) model provider spans, such as Google GenAI `generate_content` spans, now nest under their `call_llm` span, including streaming calls. Fixed [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-go) Responses API spans not being marked as errors when a stream sends an `error` event or a response ends with a `failed` status. OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google GenAI spans are now marked as errors when the response can't be read or parsed, such as when a stream is cut off partway through.
* [v0.17.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.17.1) - When a [span customizer](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/redact-sensitive-data.md#when-a-customizer-fails) fails, the SDK now exports that span with its content stripped and `context.customizer_error` set to `true`, instead of dropping the whole export batch. Customizer failures no longer make `ForceFlush` return an error. [Google ADK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/google.md#what-traced-go) sub-agent spans now nest under the agent that called them, and [LangChainGo](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md#what-traced-go) agent action and finish spans now nest under the agent executor's chain span.
* [v0.17.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.17.0) - [Span customizers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/redact-sensitive-data.md#how-customizers-run) now run before attachment upload, so they can remove inline base64 data to keep files out of Braintrust. Customizer errors are no longer included in logs, since they can contain span data. Fixed partially converted attachments when the upload queue is full, and the missing `background` parameter in [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md) Responses API metadata.

### Java SDK releases

* [v0.3.26](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.26) - When a [span customizer](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/java/redact-sensitive-data.md#how-customizers-run) fails, the SDK now exports only that span's identity and timing instead of dropping the whole export batch. Non-inference calls, such as batches, files, and token counting, now produce plain `anthropic.http`, `openai.http`, or `google.http` spans instead of LLM spans. Fixed a `ClassCastException` that failed instrumented requests during JVM shutdown.

Added tracing for [Roast](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/roast.md) workflows (`roast-ai` v1.0.0 or later), with a span for each workflow run and each cog, and the LLM calls of `chat` cogs nested under their cog. Fixed `Braintrust::Trace.permalink` URLs for organization and project names that contain spaces or slashes.

### C# SDK releases

Streaming agent runs now keep their LLM and tool spans nested under the agent span. Fixed evals failing for service accounts with only project-level access, a regression in v0.2.12. **Breaking change**: `ChatClientBuilder.UseBraintrustTracing()` has been removed and `UseBraintrustFunctionTracing()` has moved from `ChatClientBuilder` to `AIAgentBuilder`. Replace `UseBraintrustTracing()` with `UseBraintrustLLMTracing()` on the `ChatClientBuilder` and `UseBraintrustFunctionTracing()` on the agent builder. See [Microsoft Agent Framework](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/microsoft-agent-framework.md) for details.

With `bt` v0.23.0 or later, [`bt trace doctor`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-doctor) reports earlier plugin versions as out of date. Run `bt trace update claude` to update.

Run `bt trace update codex` to update. With `bt` v0.27.2 or later, the command also trusts the updated hooks.
