### Google Antigravity tracing releases

* [v0.1.5](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.1.5-antigravity) - Tool spans start when the tool starts instead of being reconstructed afterward, and tracing failures can't block tool calls.

### @braintrust/pi-extension releases

* [v2.2.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.2.2-pi) - The extension sends events to `bt` in the background instead of waiting for each one, so tracing no longer slows down pi. With `bt` v0.28.0 or later, session spans record the running pi package name in `metadata.host_name`, and trace links appear as soon as the first turn's spans are delivered. In Oh My Pi, traces record the Oh My Pi version instead of the version of the pi package installed alongside the extension. This doesn't apply to Oh My Pi's standalone binary. Run `bt trace update pi` to update.
* [v2.2.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.2.1-pi) - The extension reads the background tracing process's socket path from `BRAINTRUST_DAEMON_SOCKET`, falling back to `BT_DAEMON_SOCKET`, matching the [`BRAINTRUST_` environment variable names](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate#v0-26-0) that `bt` uses starting in v0.26.0.
* [v2.2.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.2.0-pi) - Records the pi version on session spans for pi 0.99 and 1.0. With `bt` v0.22.0 or later, sessions traced by an extension earlier than v1.0.0 continue in their existing trace.

### @braintrust/trace-opencode releases

* [v2.1.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.1.1-opencode) - Adds new turns from a [resumed OpenCode session](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md#common-workflows) to its existing trace instead of starting a new trace each time OpenCode restarts. [Built-in Braintrust tools](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md#built-in-braintrust-tools) read the path to `bt` from `BRAINTRUST_BT_BIN`, falling back to the deprecated `BT_EXECUTABLE`, and no longer open a console window on Windows. Run `bt trace update opencode` to update.

### Cursor tracing releases

* [v0.5.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.5.1-cursor) - Internal maintenance update.
* [v0.5.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.5.0-cursor) - Subagent spans open when a subagent begins instead of when it finishes, so they record the subagent's task and duration, and they nest under the `Task` tool call that spawned the subagent. The new hooks always let the tool call or subagent proceed. Requires `bt` v0.27.0 or later. Run `bt trace update cursor` to update. See [Cursor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/cursor.md#what-braintrust-traces) for details.
* [v0.4.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.4.2-cursor) - On Windows, the hook launcher reads the path to `bt` from `BRAINTRUST_BT_BIN`, falling back to `BT_BIN`.
* [v0.4.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.4.1-cursor) - `bt trace enable cursor` adds capture hooks to Cursor's `hooks.json`, so sessions are traced even when Cursor doesn't load the local plugin. Requires `bt` v0.26.0 or later. See [Cursor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/cursor.md) for details.

### Improvements

* Sort, filter, group, and save views of the [Patterns list](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#browse-patterns) (public preview). On each pattern, set status, severity, quality, and categories with an optional reason, and follow changes and comments in [**Activity**](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#activity).

* Each [Patterns automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/enable.md#customize-severity-and-categories) (public preview) can have its own severity and category rubric. Automations without one use the project's rubric.

* Braintrust computes each measurable pattern's [**Affected traces** chart](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#affected-traces) and **Last seen** time (public preview) in the background, so pattern pages load faster and **Last seen** stays current even when Loop adds no new evidence.

* A [**Models**](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#models) breakdown on each measurable pattern (public preview) compares each model's share of the pattern's matching traces with its share of all traffic, so you can spot models where the behavior is overrepresented.

* [Loop automations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#create-a-loop-automation) can follow your organization's [Loop default provider](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#set-a-default-provider-for-loop). With **Auto** on, each run uses the current default and its recommended model and reasoning effort.

* You can now [copy or export a Loop thread](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/manage.md#copy-or-export-a-thread) as Markdown, including tool calls and their output, to share an investigation or hand it to a coding agent.

* [Loop automations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#review-past-runs) now surface failed Slack and webhook deliveries in their automation status.

* GPT-6.1 Sol is available in [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md#choose-a-provider-and-model) and scheduled automations, supports Max reasoning effort, and is the default for new threads.

* On desktop, the thread now opens in a panel beside the trace, so you can read the conversation and inspect spans side by side. See [Inspect a trace](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#inspect-a-trace) for details.

* [Custom preprocessors](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/enable.md#write-a-custom-preprocessor) run faster on traces with many spans. If a custom preprocessor throws an error on any span, the run fails instead of silently skipping that span.

* You can now edit simple point-and-click filters in the Basic filter editor with their values filled in, instead of only in SQL. See [Filter and search logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/filter.md#apply-a-filter) for details.

* [Dashboard chart export](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md#export-chart-data) now covers SQL charts and table charts, and the click menu on chart data points now includes an **Export data** option that downloads JSON or CSV.

* [Online scoring rules](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md) and [human review score configurations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md) now appear in the [audit log](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/audit-logs.md) as `project_score` events (created, updated, and deleted), including pause and resume status changes. Reordering human review scores isn't recorded.

* With [row-level audit logging](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/audit-logs.md#async-queries-and-applied-scorers) enabled, the audit log also records submitting and canceling [async queries](https://docs.braintrust.dev/api-reference/query.md#async-queries), fetching their results, and applying scorers to an experiment with **Apply scorers**.

* The [\*\* Logs\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/logs) chart now opens the chart action menu when you click a bar, and you can split it into series by any field from a trace's details. In the **Traces** row type, the chart counts traces instead of spans. See [Explore the logs chart](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/view-logs.md#explore-the-logs-chart) for details.

* On the [Patterns page](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md) (public preview), you can now investigate a finding with Loop inline, with the selected pattern attached as context.

* [Copy for agent](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#hand-it-to-a-coding-agent) no longer waits for trace details before copying a brief. The brief includes up to three prioritized trace IDs and CLI instructions for retrieving additional evidence.

* You can now inspect trace evidence beside a pattern on the [Patterns page](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#examples) (public preview).

* The [custom view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/custom-views.md) editor preserves your Loop conversation when you close and reopen the same view in the same browser.

* [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md) queues follow-up messages while it works, so you can continue the conversation without waiting for the current turn to finish.

* Loop can now help [troubleshoot scoring failures](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#scoring-failures) using the scorer and error context from project logs.

* The new [Thread view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md) preserves unrecognized input and output payloads alongside parsed messages.

* You can now [translate](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#translate-message-content) and copy LLM responses and user messages in the [new thread view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#use-the-new-thread-view), as in the original thread view.

* [Patterns Slack notifications](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/enable.md) now include individual alerts for new or reactivated patterns, alongside a digest of new and existing active patterns with frequency estimates and links to additional findings.

* Loop can now create [human review scores](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md#configure-review-scores) from a description, including categorical choices, sliders, and free-form inputs.

* Table charts that return raw rows of spans from project logs now offer **Open trace**, which opens the exact trace and span behind the clicked row instead of a sample of matching traces. See [Read a table](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/sql-charts.md#read-a-table) for details.

* Service tokens with an expiration can no longer create service tokens that outlive them. These requests return a 403 error that states the maximum allowed lifetime. See [Create service tokens](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#create-service-tokens) for details.

* [Patterns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns.md) now checks each finding's cause and suggested fix against relevant source code in a [connected repository](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/github.md) and requirements or contracts from an [external MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/mcp.md) before saving it, and distinguishes confirmed causes from hypotheses when a relevant source isn't available.

* On the [Patterns page](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md) (public preview), the pattern header shows the name of the automation or person who discovered it. When a project has more than one Patterns automation, the list shows that automation's name and you can filter the list to one of them. A pattern a person created shows their name in the list as well.

* Organization owners can now list every member's API keys with `GET /v1/organization/api_keys`, for example to audit keys programmatically. See [Manage API keys](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#manage-api-keys) for details.

* You can now push code functions and scorers from Python 3.14 on Braintrust-hosted and self-hosted AWS deployments. See the [supported Lambda runtimes](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/remote-evals.md#2-register-your-sandbox).

* You can now paste images from your clipboard directly into the [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md) composer.

* [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/manage.md#models-and-providers) can use project-level AI providers when running in a sandbox and recognizes supported models with gateway prefixes.

* The [Patterns Frequency chart](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#affected-traces) (public preview) now defaults to the share of all traces in your project. The list also uses overall frequency for its saved percentages and frequency ranking.

* [Patterns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#duplicate-patterns) (public preview) consolidates duplicate findings into one canonical pattern, preserving useful evidence and linking the closed duplicates to it.

* [Continue in Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#keep-investigating) (public preview) starts a new chat when a conversation is already active, keeping the pattern investigation separate from that conversation.

* The trace view labels TypeSafe [Jev](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/typesafe.md#inspect-jev-decisions) spans as **Decision** spans instead of **Question** spans, and **Filter by span type** lists them under **Decision**.

* The [SQL sandbox](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql.md#sql-sandbox) now supports split view, so you can run and compare two queries side by side.

* [SQL chart top lists](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/sql-charts.md#donut-charts) now support a **Donut** display mode that shows each group's share of the overall value.

* Dashboard filters now default to span filtering, so only matching spans contribute to each chart and charts load faster. You can switch to cross-trace filtering for fields on other spans in the trace, and existing saved dashboards keep cross-trace filtering. See [Filter and group data](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards.md#filter-and-group-data) for details.

* The [new thread view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#use-the-new-thread-view) (public preview) is now the default in the thread panel. You can switch back to the original thread view from the thread settings menu.

* [Dashboard charts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards.md) that fail to load now label recognized failures, such as an invalid query, a timeout, or missing access, and show a suggested next step and the error message when available.

* The [Patterns list](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#browse-patterns) (public preview) can filter by more than one automation, and a **Backfill** action asks Loop to assign a severity to patterns that don't have one.

* A **Traces linked to patterns** [preset chart](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md) ranks patterns on a dashboard by the number of traces attached to each as evidence.

* You can now duplicate dataset [custom views](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/custom-views.md#duplicate-views) to other datasets in your organization, so you can reuse a dataset view without rebuilding it.

* Members, service accounts, and permission groups with every permission on every row of a project group's permissions, including through organization-wide **All projects** grants, now count as Owners of that project group and can create service accounts and service tokens for it. See [Project group owners](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control/manage-permissions.md#project-group-owners) for details.

* The trace viewer no longer includes the public-preview Debugger. Saved Debugger links open the trace view. Use [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/capabilities.md#analyze-traces) to investigate individual traces.

* The [Coding agent insights dashboard](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/coding-agent-insights.md#group-the-table) (public preview) can now break down usage and cost by permission group or service account, so you can compare spending across teams and identify activity authenticated by service accounts.

* Dashboard charts now support [markers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md#add-markers), which highlight threshold values and ranges by severity, such as a latency target or an error rate limit.

* You can now resize the columns of [table charts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/sql-charts.md#read-a-table) on dashboards. Braintrust remembers your column widths for each chart in your browser.

* Patterns (public preview) improvements:
  * [Slack notifications](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/enable.md#slack-notifications) send one alert per new or reactivated pattern, including severity and categories, instead of an end-of-run digest.
  * [Pattern wording](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns.md#goals) refreshes when the writing guidance or finding changes, preserving the finding's meaning and evidence when only the guidance changes.
  * The pattern header shows when the behavior was last seen in a trace. Hover over the label for the exact timestamp.

* When you ask Loop to change a pattern's [corrected severity or categories](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#severity) (public preview), Loop now makes the change. Patterns automations still keep your corrections.

* The [`new_pattern` MCP tool](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp#tools) requires a `source_review` that records whether source code was reviewed before the pattern was saved. Pattern writes that report matching traces must include `last_seen`, and `first_seen` when the pattern doesn't have one.

* SQL queries now support [`is_uuid()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions#string-functions), which checks whether a string is a valid UUID, so you can exclude malformed IDs in `WHERE` clauses and aggregates.

* [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/manage.md#models-and-providers) can now use GPT models served through a Databricks AI provider, such as `databricks-gpt-6-1-sol`.

* Coding agents can now save dataset [custom views](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/custom-views.md) and reuse existing ones as templates with the [Braintrust MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md#run-evals-and-edit-datasets).

* Coding agents can now run a saved [Loop automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#run-an-automation-now) immediately with `run_scheduled_loop_job` on the [Braintrust MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md#manage-automations-and-alerts), the same as clicking **Run now**. The run sends results to the automation's configured destinations.

* The [Braintrust MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md#explore-your-data) can now resolve [attachment](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/attachments.md) references from query results into short-lived download URLs with `resolve_attachments`, so coding agents can retrieve the images and files attached to your logs, experiments, and datasets.

* On the [Patterns page](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#examples), the first example trace opens beside the pattern automatically, and you can step through the other examples without leaving the page. In the [new thread view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#use-the-new-thread-view), evidence appears as cards below the highlighted text, with controls to jump between examples.

* On desktop, you can now choose whether a trace shows its conversation and custom views, its spans, or both side by side. See [Choose a trace layout](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#choose-a-trace-layout) for details.

* [**Usage analytics**](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/monitor-usage.md#view-usage) now charts built-in model and Topics tokens together in a built-in inference chart, broken down by model or token kind. On every plan except Starter without on-demand usage, you can switch the charts to cost. On Enterprise plans, cost mode shows list-rate estimates for inference and hides the logs and scores charts.

* [Decision spans](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/trace-application-logic.md#log-bounding-boxes) now support `box` answers, which the trace view draws as bounding boxes over the input image.

* The span panel's **Details** tab now shows a span's input and output with its other fields, and the trace header shows the root span's scores. See [View as a hierarchy](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#view-as-a-hierarchy) for details.

* You can now pause, resume, or delete several [online scoring rules](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#pause-an-automation) at once instead of editing each rule.

* The [Account details page for Enterprise account billing](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing.md#view-account-billing) lists the account's invoices, each linking to its hosted invoice.

* \*\* Settings\*\* now has a separate page for each kind of automation and score, such as [online scoring rules](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md), [alerts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/alerts.md), and [human review scores](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md). Links to the former **Automations** and **Scoring** pages open **Loop** and **Human review**.

* The [`set_topics_automation` MCP tool](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp#tools) no longer replaces existing topic maps when you change facets. `facets` only adds facets, and the tool removes facets or topic maps only when you name them in `remove_facets` or `remove_topic_map_ids`. **Breaking change**: `facet_mode: "replace"` returns an error. Use `remove_facets` and `remove_topic_map_ids` instead. A new `unpin_topic_maps` tool removes topic map version pins without changing other settings.

* Prompts in experiments now use saved [project MCP credentials](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/prompts/create.md#add-to-a-project) when no request credentials are supplied, so runs can authenticate without a browser connection.

* The online scoring rule form now estimates the rule's weekly scorer runs from the last 7 days of matching logs, so you can choose a sampling rate before you save the rule. See [Estimate weekly scorer runs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#estimate-weekly-scorer-runs) for details.

* The online scoring rules table now shows how many spans or traces each rule scored in the last 24 hours, and the estimated LLM cost of its scorers. See [Monitor rule activity](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#monitor-rule-activity) for details.

* SQL `IS NULL` and `IS NOT NULL` filters on JSON fields such as `metadata.user_id` now run faster. `IS NULL` also treats arrays whose elements are all null or empty, such as `[null]` and `[{}]`, as null. See [Null checks on JSON fields](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/filtering.md#null-checks-on-json-fields) for details.

* `IS NULL` and `IS NOT NULL` filters on a path through an array, such as `input.messages.role IS NOT NULL`, now check every array element on all JSON fields, so you don't need to index a specific element. See [Null checks on JSON fields](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/filtering.md#null-checks-on-json-fields) for details.

* Scorer spans now include `span_attributes.scorer_id` when Braintrust runs the scorer, such as for online scoring rules and scores applied in the UI, so you can break down scorer tokens and LLM cost by scorer. See [Monitor rule activity](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#monitor-rule-activity) for details.

* SQL queries now support more [string](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#string-functions), [regular expression](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#regular-expression-functions), [numeric](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#numeric-functions), and [date part](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#date-and-time-functions) functions, such as `split_part`, `strpos`, `regexp_like`, `regexp_replace`, `trunc`, and `EXTRACT`. Filters that compare an extracted year to a whole number, such as `EXTRACT(YEAR FROM created) = 2026`, run as fast as an equivalent time-range filter.

* Time series charts on dashboards now support custom Y axis bounds and a log scale, so you can keep outliers from flattening the rest of a series. See [Chart editor options](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md#chart-editor-options) for details.

* Online scoring rules no longer score a trace or span again when you assign it for review or change its review status. Updates that only add or change scores also no longer restart the [idle timeout](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#configuration-parameters) for trace-scoped and group-scoped rules.

**September 2026**

### Apply scorers to experiments

You can now add scorers to an experiment after it runs. Braintrust scores the experiment's existing traces in the background without re-running the task, and continues scoring traces added to the experiment later. See [Apply scorers to all traces](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/interpret-results.md#apply-scorers-to-all-traces) for details.

### Dashboard notes

Add Markdown notes to the dashboards you create to explain what a dashboard tracks, link to runbooks, or annotate groups of charts. You can copy notes to other dashboards, the same as charts. See [Add notes](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md#add-notes) for details.

> [!NOTE]
>
> Dashboard notes are only available on [Pro and Enterprise plans](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#plans).

### TypeScript workflow evaluations

[Workflow evaluations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/workflow-evaluations.md), which run eval tasks and scorers through provider batch APIs, are now available in the TypeScript SDK (v3.33.0+), following their [Python release](#python-workflow-evaluations).

> [!WARNING]
>
> TypeScript workflow evaluations are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Span customizers

Span customizers let you modify spans inside your application before the SDK uploads them to Braintrust, for example to redact sensitive values, remove fields, or add tags and metadata. They're available in the TypeScript (v3.35.0+), Python (v0.43.0+), Go (v0.16.0+), and Java (v0.3.25+) SDKs. See [Redact before upload](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/data-management/protect-sensitive-data.md#redact-before-upload) for details.

### External MCP servers in Loop

Connect [external MCP servers to Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/mcp.md) so it can use their tools and data alongside your Braintrust traces. Save an API key or OAuth connection for the project to share access with Loop and scheduled automations.

> [!WARNING]
>
> External MCP connections in Loop are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Chart data point actions

Clicking a data point on a chart now opens a menu of actions for that point. Open matching traces in a side panel without leaving the page, explore the rows behind the point in the SQL sandbox, or filter the dashboard to the clicked series. See [Interact with chart data](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards.md#interact-with-chart-data) for details.

### Python workflow evaluations

Python evaluations (SDK v0.39.0+) can now run their tasks and scorers through provider batch APIs, which providers often offer at a discount. Start a run, let the provider work, and pick the run back up later by polling or by responding to a webhook. The process that resumes the run doesn't have to be the one that started it. When every case finishes, Braintrust records the results in a single experiment. See [Workflow evaluations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/workflow-evaluations.md) for details.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Python workflow evaluations are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Application logs

Send application logs to Braintrust and inspect them alongside your traces, with or without an LLM call. Emit messages or forward standard-library `logging` records with the Python SDK (v0.42.0+), or export OpenTelemetry logs from any language. Browse and filter logs by severity with the new  **Logs** row type, view each message in the trace that emitted it, or query logs with [SQL](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql.md). See [Send application logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/application-logs.md) for details.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Application logs are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Claude Code OpenTelemetry mapping

Braintrust now maps Claude Code's native OpenTelemetry traces and events to LLM and tool spans with token metrics, cost, and conversation messages. See [Trace Claude Code with OpenTelemetry](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md#trace-with-opentelemetry) for details.

### OpenInference tracing

Braintrust now maps [OpenInference traces](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/opentelemetry/attributes.md#openinference-attributes) to structured messages, tool calls, span types, token metrics, tags, and scores. Use OpenInference instrumentors for frameworks such as LangChain, [LlamaIndex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/llamaindex.md#opentelemetry-python), and DSPy with your OpenTelemetry pipeline.

### Transform coding-agent traces locally

Redact secrets, remove sensitive fields, or add tags and metadata to [coding-agent traces](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace.md) before they leave your machine. The `bt` CLI (v0.21.0+) runs JavaScript span plugins that you register with `bt trace enable`, `bt trace run`, or `bt trace import`. If a plugin fails, `bt` withholds the affected spans instead of sending untransformed data. See [Transform spans with JavaScript](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace.md#transform-spans-with-javascript) for details.

### Pattern frequency

[Patterns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#examine-a-pattern) shows how often a finding occurs among comparable traces or across your project, with a chart for the selected time range. Scheduled runs refresh measurements for existing active patterns.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Patterns is in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### AI provider setup and model controls

AI provider updates include a redesigned setup experience, new model access controls, and a fix to hosted request routing:

* **Simpler setup and testing:** A clearer setup form organizes credentials and advanced settings. The new **Models** tab lets you [browse models](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#browse-and-test-models) and copy a Gateway command to test one.
* **Model restrictions:** New [model restrictions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#restrict-available-models) work with [AI provider governance](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#permissions-and-access) to control which models each team can access. Organization providers support model selection on both Braintrust-hosted and self-hosted deployments. For project providers, the new **Selected models** option is available on Braintrust-hosted deployments.
* **Routing fix (behavior change):** On Braintrust-hosted deployments, project providers [now override](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#request-routing) organization providers only for models they serve. Eligible organization providers can serve other models using their own credentials and billing accounts. If you rely on project restrictions to limit model access or spending, review organization provider model selections and permissions.

### Usage analytics by project

The [**Usage analytics**](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/monitor-usage.md#view-usage) section of the billing page now breaks usage down by project, so you can see which projects drive your logs, scores, and Topics token spend and export the breakdown as a CSV. Project data is available from September 1, 2026.

### DeepSeek V4.1 Flash

DeepSeek V4.1 Flash is available as a [built-in model](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#available-models) in playgrounds, prompts, and scorers, with multimodal reasoning support and no AI provider setup required. You can also request `deepseek-v4.1-flash` through the Braintrust Gateway. Usage draws from your monthly [model credits](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#model-credits), shared with Topics.

### TypeSafe Jev

TypeSafe provides [Jev](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/typesafe.md), a model that returns a specific choice or numeric result instead of generating text. You define the question and its allowed answers before the call, so nothing downstream has to find a decision inside prose or malformed JSON.

You can now trace Jev calls from your application to inspect the context, question criteria, and selected answers together, and to identify ambiguous decisions. Requires [Python v0.41.0+](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.41.0) or [TypeScript v3.34.0+](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.34.0).

You can also use Jev as the judge in a [scorer or classifier](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/typesafe.md#llm-as-a-judge), either with a built-in model or your own TypeSafe key, to score outputs against predefined criteria or label logs with a fixed set of categories. It fits frequent, focused checks with a clear answer, and Braintrust records the confidence and per-choice probabilities behind each decision so you can inspect uncertain judgments.

### Loop thread sharing

Share [Loop threads](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/manage.md#share-a-thread) with project members to review an investigation or continue it together, with view-only or chat access. Loop responds to each message using the sender's permissions, and only that sender can approve or cancel the work their message starts.

### Trace Grok and Google Antigravity sessions

Understand how [Grok](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/grok.md) and [Google Antigravity](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/antigravity.md) coding sessions progress across prompts, model calls, and tool activity. The `bt` CLI (v0.19.3+) captures each session as a Braintrust trace without interrupting the coding agent if tracing fails.

### Observability templates

Reuse facets and Loop automations across projects instead of rebuilding your observability setup each time. The [`bt observability template`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/observability) commands in `bt` v0.19.0+ export a project's configuration as a portable JSON template and apply it to another project.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Observability templates are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Metric columnstore

Braintrust can now store trace-level metrics in a columnar format, so the queries behind the  **Logs** page read far less data on high-volume projects. Enable **Metric columnstore** in project settings alongside log search optimization, and Braintrust writes columnar artifacts during compaction, speeding up loading and sorting traces. Aggregation queries are unaffected and continue to follow [SQL best practices](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/best-practices.md). See [Speed up log filtering](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/projects.md#speed-up-log-filtering) for details.

### Faster log search

Brainstore now runs log searches on an asynchronous execution engine that streams results as segments finish scanning, instead of waiting for every segment to complete. This covers full-text and phrase search, along with trace searches that combine several [`ANY_SPAN()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure#single-span-filters) clauses, including negated ones. Searches over large, cold log volumes benefit most, and unsorted searches gain the most. Query syntax is unchanged and no configuration is required. See [Filter and search logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/filter.md) for search syntax.

### SQL `SELECT DISTINCT` for unique rows

`SELECT DISTINCT` on scalar columns now deduplicates SQL query results, returning one row per unique combination of values. See [SELECT DISTINCT](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#select-distinct) for supported usage and restrictions.

### Patterns

Braintrust now investigates your traces for you. [\*\* Patterns\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/patterns) runs Loop against your project on a schedule, searching for recurring problems and trends that no scorer is already measuring, such as a failure mode affecting one cohort of users or an LLM call whose cost has climbed. Each finding is saved with the traces that support it, and with a suggested fix when Loop has one to offer, so you start from evidence rather than a hunch. From a pattern you can create a scorer or classifier to track the behavior continuously, continue the investigation in Loop, or copy the finding as a prompt for your coding agent. See [Enable Patterns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/enable.md) to turn it on.

Patterns support is limited to two model families: GPT-6 Astra and GPT-5.6 (GPT-5.6 Sol, GPT-5.6 Terra, and GPT-5.6 Luna). Braintrust is working to add more models. Use Braintrust's native inference when possible, so scheduled discovery runs on supported models without provider setup.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Patterns is in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Debugger

> [!WARNING]
>
> The Debugger, including **Analyze trace**, was retired in October 2026. To investigate an individual trace, paste its permalink or ID into a [Loop thread](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/capabilities.md#analyze-traces).

A long agent trace can span dozens of LLM and tool calls, which makes reading it end to end impractical. The  **Debugger** reports the failure modes it considers likely for a single trace, citing the spans, tool calls, and model outputs behind each one.  **Analyze trace** answers the complementary question, grouping a run into **Work sections** that explain what it did rather than what went wrong.

### Loop works across your whole project

[\*\* Loop\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/loop) now runs in a Braintrust-managed runtime rather than your browser, so it takes on longer investigations and your threads persist for you to resume. It works across your logs, experiments, and datasets rather than only the page you opened it on, and it can create and edit the objects you'd otherwise build by hand, including prompts, scorers, datasets, facets, custom views, dashboards, and automations. Every write pauses for your approval unless you turn on auto-accept, and results open in a workspace beside the chat so you can review them without leaving the thread. See [What Loop can do](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/capabilities.md).

Loop support is limited to two built-in model families: GPT-6 Astra and GPT-5.6 (GPT-5.6 Sol, GPT-5.6 Terra, and GPT-5.6 Luna). Braintrust is working to add more models. Use Braintrust's native inference when possible, so teammates can start threads on supported models without configuring provider credentials.

### Loop automations

Loop can now do recurring work without someone starting a chat. A [Loop automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md) is a scheduled run with its own instruction, model, and write permissions, useful for reviewing cost or latency on a cadence, checking whether quality is degrading, or summarizing recent errors. Each run leaves a read-only thread you can  **Continue** to ask follow-up questions, and results can be sent to a Slack channel or webhook.

### Blind reviews

By default, human review now hides peer scores, comments, and aggregates for a row until a reviewer fills in their own scores, and reviewers open unscored rows with a blank form. Enable the **Blind human reviews** project setting to keep those results hidden for the whole review, so independence holds even after a reviewer finishes. Reviewers with the project `Update` permission are exempt and always see all reviews. See [Enable blind reviews](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md#enable-blind-reviews) for setup steps.

### Required scores for human review

Turn on **Require all scores** in \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Human review\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/review) to require assigned reviewers to complete every score visible to them. The review completes automatically after all assigned reviewers finish scoring, and manual completion is not available while the setting is on. See [Require all scores](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md#require-all-scores) for details.

### GLM-5.3 Flash

GLM-5.3 Flash joins GLM-5.2, Kimi K3, and DeepSeek V4 Flash 0731 as a built-in open-source model served by Braintrust, with no AI provider setup required. Select it under the **Braintrust** provider in playgrounds, prompts, and scorers, or request `glm-5.3-flash` through the Braintrust Gateway. GLM-5.3 Flash is a multimodal reasoning model. Usage draws from your monthly [model credits](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#model-credits), shared with Topics. See [Available models](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#available-models) for details.

### Dashboard sections

Group a dashboard's charts into named **sections** you can reorder, duplicate, and collapse to keep a long dashboard readable. Braintrust remembers each section's collapsed state in your browser, and duplicating a section keeps it. See [Organize charts into sections](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md#organize-charts-into-sections) for details.

> [!NOTE]
>
> Dashboard sections are only available on [Pro and Enterprise plans](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#plans).

### Project groups

Group projects into a named collection, then grant a permission group access to that collection once instead of repeating the same grant on every project. Every project in the group inherits the access, and a project you add later inherits it immediately. Create and manage them at \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Project groups\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/project-groups). See [Manage project groups](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control/manage-permissions.md#manage-project-groups) for details.

> [!NOTE]
>
> Project groups are only available on the [Enterprise plan](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#plans).

### bt CLI releases

* [v0.22.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.22.1)

  * Fixed separately started coding-agent sessions, such as two agents in the same terminal, merging into one trace.
  * Fixed trace events getting stuck while waiting to link to a parent session.
  * Fixed [`bt trace doctor`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-doctor) reporting authentication as ready when the background tracing process could not authenticate.

* [v0.22.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.22.0)

  * **Custom views CLI**: New [`bt custom-views`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/custom-views) commands let you bootstrap starter files, preview views locally against real trace and dataset data with hot reload, and push finished views to Braintrust. Requires `braintrust` SDK v3.33.0 or later.
  * **npm distribution**: `bt` is now published as [`@braintrust/bt`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@braintrust/bt) on npm, independent of the `braintrust` JavaScript SDK. Install with `npm install --save-dev @braintrust/bt` or globally with `npm install -g @braintrust/bt`.
  * **Python dataset pipeline fix**: The `scope: "trace"` setting in `DatasetPipeline` source definitions now works correctly in the Python runner, matching existing TypeScript behavior. Previously the scope was ignored and all Python transforms received span-scoped arguments.
  * **Tool errors**: Failed tool spans from [pi](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md), [Codex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md) MCP tools, [Grok](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/grok.md), and [Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md) record the agent's own error message, such as `Command exited with code 1`, instead of a generic one such as `Tool execution failed`.

* [v0.21.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.21.0)

  * **Span plugins**: Adds `--plugin` to [`bt trace enable`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-enable), [`bt trace run`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-run), and [`bt trace import`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-import) to [transform spans with JavaScript](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace.md#transform-spans-with-javascript) before they are sent. [`bt trace doctor`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-doctor) reports plugin failures.
  * **[Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md) tracing**: Records the permission mode on each turn span, and the client system prompt on the session span when Claude Code provides it.
  * **[Google Antigravity](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/antigravity.md) tracing**: `bt trace enable antigravity` and `bt trace update antigravity` support Windows.
  * **[pi](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) tracing**: Fixes `bt trace update pi`. `bt trace enable pi` installs the extension without a version range.
  * **Trace structure**: Prevents traces with more than one root span when an update arrives for a span whose first write was not saved. Codex, Google Antigravity, OpenCode, and pi no longer send empty traces for sessions that end without agent activity. Codex sessions imported below a parent span stay in the parent's trace.
  * **Background process upgrades**: When different `bt` versions send tracing events, an older `bt` reuses a newer background tracing process instead of restarting it.

* [v0.20.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.20.0)

  * **[Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md) tracing**: Stops marking successful tool calls as errors, fixes recovered tool outcomes, and no longer turns stale child transcripts into synthetic subagent spans.
  * **[Codex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md) tracing**: Preserves turn inputs and native timestamps, ignores empty error fields, and labels compaction and resume events correctly instead of as startup.
  * **[OpenCode](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md) and [pi](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) tracing**: Flush delivery in the background so a slow backend flush no longer blocks idle handling, shutdown, or the next input.
  * **[Antigravity](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/antigravity.md) and [Grok](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/grok.md) tracing**: Use each harness's native hook timeouts.
  * **Breaking change**: Removes the `--relabel-overlap` flag from [`bt topics config enable`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/topics#bt-topics-config-enable) and [`bt topics config set`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/topics#bt-topics-config-set). The flag was deprecated and ignored since data plane v2.15.0. See the [v0.20.0 migration instructions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate.md#v0-20-0).

* [v0.19.3](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.19.3)

  * **Browser links**: Adds [`--app-public-url`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/overview#global-flags) and [`BRAINTRUST_APP_PUBLIC_URL`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/overview#environment-variables) for deployments whose browser-facing app URL differs from the authentication endpoint.
  * **Claude Code tracing**: Prevents empty traces from session startup and idle notifications.
  * **Custom certificates**: Tracing and data uploads that use the Rust SDK can trust private or corporate certificate authorities through [`BRAINTRUST_CUSTOM_CA_BUNDLE`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/overview#environment-variables). Supply PEM certificate contents, not a file path.
  * **Eval discovery**: Automatic [`bt eval`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval#file-selection) discovery skips `vendor` directories.
  * **Go evals**: [`bt eval`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval#runtime-configuration) runs Go packages that use the Go SDK's `evalrunner` package. It accepts a package directory, discovers the package with `go list`, or runs a prebuilt binary passed with `--runner`. Go evals are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md).
  * **Grok tracing**: Adds CLI support for [tracing Grok sessions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/grok.md).
  * **pi tracing**: [`bt trace run pi`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi#common-workflows) now uses v2 of the tracing extension, matching persistent setup.
  * **Project commands**: `bt projects create`, `view`, and `delete` support [JSON output](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/projects.md) for automation. `view --json` returns project details without opening a browser.
  * **Trace metadata**: Google Antigravity, Grok, OpenCode, and pi session spans include the local username when available. Grok traces also include Git repository metadata.
  * **Trace tags**: Adds [session tags](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace.md#tag-traces) for Claude Code, Codex, Google Antigravity, OpenCode, and pi through `--tag` or `BRAINTRUST_TAGS`.
  * **Tracing removal**: [`bt trace disable`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-disable) attempts to remove the tracing configuration even if uninstalling the plugin fails.
  * **Tracing updates**: [`bt trace update`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-update) updates installed coding-agent integrations without rewriting their tracing settings. [`bt trace doctor`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-doctor) warns about outdated plugins and mismatched package version ranges.
  * **Breaking changes**: `bt trace enable opencode` and `bt trace enable pi` now install v2 of their respective tracing integrations. See the [v0.19.3 migration instructions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate.md#v0-19-3).

* [v0.19.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.19.2)

  * **Tracing performance**: Reduces the time tracing adds to coding-agent commands.
  * **Smaller traces**: Reduces repeated conversation history in model-call spans after the agent summarizes its context.
  * **Behavior change**: [`bt sync pull`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/sync) includes all matching experiment and dataset history unless you pass `--window` or set `BT_SYNC_WINDOW`. Project logs retain the default three-day window. See the [v0.19.2 migration instructions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate.md#v0-19-2).

* [v0.19.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.19.1)

  * **Google Antigravity tracing**: [`bt trace enable antigravity`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-enable) activates the plugin after installing it. [`bt trace import antigravity`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-import) imports existing conversations.
  * **Self-hosted tracing**: Fixes coding-agent tracing for self-hosted and hybrid deployments by resolving each organization's data-plane API URL.
  * **Trace recovery**: Prevents duplicate spans across supported coding agents when tracing resumes after an interruption.

* [v0.19.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.19.0)

  * **Observability templates**: Added public preview [`bt observability template`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/observability) commands to export and apply portable JSON configuration for facets and Loop automations.
  * **SQL**: [`bt sql`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/sql) now supports `FROM logs` for querying the selected project.
  * **Tracing setup**: [`bt trace enable`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-enable) is now the recommended setup command (`bt trace setup` remains an alias). [`bt trace disable`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-disable) removes an integration. [`bt trace doctor`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-doctor) diagnoses tracing configuration and authentication. Rerunning setup removes obsolete top-level destination and authentication fields from global OpenCode and pi configurations. Claude Code setup warns about obsolete `BRAINTRUST_CC_PROJECT` and `BRAINTRUST_CC_DEBUG` settings without removing `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY`.
  * **Session tracing**: Added initial Google Antigravity support. The `enable`, `run`, and `import` commands now accept custom root-span metadata through `--additional-metadata` or `BRAINTRUST_ADDITIONAL_METADATA`. Fixed tracing for non-interactive `bt trace run claude` invocations.
  * **Authentication and context**: Expanded [`bt status --all`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/status) output. Added [`bt profiles doctor`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/profiles) and [`bt profiles repair`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/profiles) for credential diagnostics and repair. Stable profile IDs preserve tracing references after renames. [`bt logout --all`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/logout) signs out of all saved profiles. Trace viewing and experiment comparisons using URLs now select the URL's organization when an explicit profile is passed, unless `--org` overrides it.
  * **Breaking changes**: Normal Claude Code and Codex sessions no longer read `TRACE_TO_BRAINTRUST` or `BRAINTRUST_ADDITIONAL_METADATA` from the environment. `bt trace run codex` rejects `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust` to prevent duplicate Braintrust hooks. [`bt login`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/login) requires confirmation before saving `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY`. See the [v0.19.0 migration instructions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate.md#v0-19-0).

### Python SDK releases

* [v0.43.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.43.0) - Added [span customizers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/redact-sensitive-data.md#span-customizers) (`SpanCustomizer`, `set_span_customizers()`, and the `span_customizers` argument to `auto_instrument()`) to modify or redact records from instrumented spans before upload. [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md) tracing captures refusal `stop_details`, `compaction` and `context_management` request parameters, and the `speed` and `fallback_credit` usage fields. Integration modules load lazily, which reduces import time. Fixed `OtelExporter` sending spans without authentication when the API key was resolved after construction with `opentelemetry-exporter-otlp-proto-http` 1.45, and fixed usage capture for the [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md) when partial messages are enabled.
* [v0.42.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.42.0) - Fixed remote scorers to correctly serialize attachment inputs, so scorers that receive attachments no longer fail. Fixed org context propagation for scorers added from the playground during remote evals. Preserved [Agno](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/agno.md) session IDs across resumed runs.
* [v0.41.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.41.1) - Improved span creation performance under heavy tracing.
* [v0.41.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.41.0) - Added [TypeSafe](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/typesafe.md) instrumentation for synchronous and asynchronous SDK calls. Added [Google Discovery Engine](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/google-discovery-engine.md) instrumentation for answer generation, conversational search, grounding checks, and ranking.
* [v0.40.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.40.0) - Adds OpenAI [Agents API](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#what-traced-python) instrumentation: `beta.agents.sessions.create(stream=True)` calls are now traced as `openai.agents.sessions.create` task spans, capturing agent and session metadata, tool call child spans, and token usage. Fixes remote eval reruns to preserve upsert IDs across reruns.
* [v0.39.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.39.0) - Added [workflow evaluations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/workflow-evaluations.md) in public preview, which submit tasks and scorers to asynchronous provider APIs and collect results through polling or webhooks. Added `has_error`, `metadata`, and `duration` fields to `SpanFilters` for `trace.get_spans()`, allowing trace-level scorers to filter spans by error state, metadata values, and execution time bounds. See [custom code scorers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/custom-code.md#score-traces) for details.
* [v0.38.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.38.0) - Scorer functions can now return `{"score": 0.5}` without a `name` field. The scorer function's name is used as the score key. Fixed [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md) extended-thinking spans to capture `completion_reasoning_tokens`. Fixed [Pydantic AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md) agent spans to include `instructions` and `system_prompt` in span input, tools in model-call metadata, and reasoning token counts for Anthropic and Google models.
* [v0.37.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.37.0) - Added invocation parameters (`temperature`, `max_tokens`, and similar model settings) to [Pydantic AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md) LLM span metadata when model settings are provided.
* [v0.36.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.36.0) - Added eval instrumentation for [Agno](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/agno.md): `AccuracyEval`, `AgentAsJudgeEval`, `ReliabilityEval`, `PerformanceEval`, and eval suites are now traced automatically via `auto_instrument()`. Eval suite runs open a Braintrust experiment automatically. Added standard verifier output upload to the [Harbor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/harbor.md) plugin (`test-stdout.txt`, `test-stderr.txt`, and `ctrf.json`) with `attachments` and `redact_patterns` configuration. Fixed API key propagation in `load_prompt`.

Added instrumentation for [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md) audio and image APIs, [Google GenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md) image generation, image editing, and video generation, and [Groq](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/groq.md) audio APIs (speech, transcription, and translation). Set `BRAINTRUST_CAPTURE_ATTACHMENTS` to `1` or `true` to upload files from OpenAI image and audio calls as attachments. Fixed token metrics for AI SDK middleware, nesting of child spans under [Pi Coding Agent](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pi-coding-agent.md) tool spans, capture of provider-managed tool calls and the provider name when using the [Vercel AI Gateway](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel.md), and dataset snapshots silently including deleted rows. **Breaking change**: Base64 image and file inputs to OpenAI Chat Completions calls are recorded as `<omitted>` unless `BRAINTRUST_CAPTURE_ATTACHMENTS` is set to `1` or `true`. Earlier versions uploaded them as attachments.

Improved `flush()` so events queued after a flush starts do not delay it. Fixed OpenAI Batch API instrumentation for `Uint8Array` and `Buffer` JSONL sources and the `/v1/embeddings` endpoint.

Added `SpanFilters` to `Trace.getSpans()`, so [trace-level scorers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/custom-code.md#score-traces) can filter spans by type, name, error state, metadata, and execution time. Added auto-instrumentation for the [LangGraph Platform SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/langgraph.md) (`@langchain/langgraph-sdk`), tracing `RunsClient.wait()` and `RunsClient.stream()` calls. Added `startOpenAIAgentsTrace`, `updateOpenAIAgentsTrace`, and `failOpenAIAgentsTrace` for tracing the [OpenAI Agents API](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#agents-api-typescript) (`openai.beta.agents.sessions`) from `openai` v7+. Added multimodal embedding support to the [Gemini](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md) integration for `gemini-embedding-2` models, capturing image inputs as Braintrust attachments. **Breaking change**: Workflow evaluations replace the experimental `defineDurableEval()` API from v3.28.0. `defineDurableEval()` and its `processBatchResult()` method, `BatchTask`, `BatchScorer`, `DurableEvalStore`, `DurableEvalMemoryStore`, and `DurableEvalRedisStore` are removed.

Added [`@google/generative-ai`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini#google-generative-ai-typescript) instrumentation (`wrapGoogleGenerativeAI`) for the legacy Google Generative AI SDK (v0.24.x). Added `input_audio` content type support in Mustache and Nunjucks prompt template rendering.
* [v3.31.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.31.0) - Test runner scorer functions can now return a score object without a `name` field, with `"score"` used as the key when `name` is omitted. [Vercel AI SDK v6](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel.md) tool call spans now include `toolCallId` in span metadata. Fixed Anthropic token metrics in LangChain spans and instrumentation hook injection for Next.js versions earlier than 15.
* [v3.30.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.30.0) - Added OpenAI Batch API instrumentation with `openaiFilesCreateTraced`, `openaiBatchesRetrieveTraced`, and `completeOpenAIBatchTrace` for tracing asynchronous OpenAI Batch requests. See [OpenAI Batch API](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#batch-api-typescript) for details.

### Go SDK releases

* [v0.16.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.16.0) - Adds [span customizers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/redact-sensitive-data.md): register ordered customizers with `braintrust.WithSpanCustomizers` to modify or redact every span before export. If a customizer fails, the SDK drops the whole export batch instead of sending unmodified spans. The OpenAI Responses API integration also captures `context_management` and `conversation` in request and response span metadata.
* [v0.15.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.15.0) - Adds tracing for the [A2A (Agent2Agent) protocol](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/a2a.md) (`trace/contrib/a2a`), covering client `SendMessage()` and `SendStreamingMessage()` calls and server request handling, with trace context propagated so client and server spans share one trace. Supports Orchestrion auto-instrumentation or manual setup with `InstrumentClient()` and `InstrumentServer()`. Captures `completion_reasoning_tokens` for Anthropic extended thinking, and preserves `server_tool_use` and `mcp_tool_use` blocks in streamed Anthropic output. Captures `previous_response_id` and `include` in OpenAI Responses API span metadata, so chained responses can be linked. Fixes streaming OpenAI chat completion spans to record response metadata, `logprobs`, and refusal text, and to keep each choice separate when `n` is greater than 1.
* [v0.14.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.14.0) - Adds tracing for the legacy OpenAI `/v1/completions` endpoint as a new `Completion` span. Captures Anthropic `context_management` config and response `applied_edits` together in span metadata. Adds `prompt_cache_key`, `safety_identifier`, `web_search_options`, `prediction`, and streaming citation `annotations` to OpenAI chat completion and responses spans. HTTP 4xx/5xx responses mark spans as errors.
* [v0.13.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.13.0) - Adds prompt support: load a Braintrust prompt in Go, render its variables locally, and call any LLM client with the result. Prompts can also drive eval parameters, and rendered calls link back to their prompt in Braintrust traces. See [Go API reference](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/api-reference.md#prompts) for details.

### Java SDK releases

* [v0.3.25](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.25) - Adds [span customizers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/java/redact-sensitive-data.md): implement `SpanCustomizer` and register it with `addSpanCustomizer()` on `BraintrustConfig.Builder` to modify or redact every span before export, including attachment data before upload. If a customizer fails, the SDK drops the whole export batch. **Breaking change**: `context.span_origin.instrumentation.name` now holds the name of the tracer that created the span, such as `openai`, `anthropic`, `aws-bedrock`, `genai`, `langchain4j`, or `springai` for integration spans, or your tracer's name for spans you create. Earlier versions always used `braintrust-java`, which still appears on eval and dev server spans. Update queries that filter on `braintrust-java`.
* [v0.3.24](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.24) - The S3 attachment uploader now recovers automatically after transient errors instead of shutting down. New `BraintrustConfig` options tune its behavior: `BRAINTRUST_ATTACHMENT_UPLOADER_QUEUE_SIZE` (default: `1024`), `BRAINTRUST_ATTACHMENT_UPLOADER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MILLIS` (default: `60000`), `BRAINTRUST_ATTACHMENT_UPLOADER_MAX_RETRIES` (default: `8`), and `BRAINTRUST_ATTACHMENT_UPLOADER_INITIAL_RETRY_DELAY_MILLIS` (default: `500`). See [environment variables](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/java/api-reference.md#environment-variables) for details.
* [v0.3.23](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.23) - Eval cases now run concurrently (default: 3 at a time). `Eval.start()` starts an eval in the background and returns an `EvalResult` as soon as the experiment exists, so you can surface the link before all cases finish. `Eval.run()` awaits completion as before. `EvalResult` gains lifecycle APIs: `getStatus()`, `isDone()`, `awaitCompletion()`, `getStartedAt()`, `getDuration()`, `getCasesExecuted()`, and `getAbortCause()`. Set concurrency with `.maxConcurrency(int)` on `Eval.Builder` or `Devserver.Builder`, or supply your own `Executor` with `.executor(Executor)`. Global defaults for concurrency and OTel batch tuning are available via `BRAINTRUST_DEFAULT_MAX_CONCURRENCY`, `BRAINTRUST_OTEL_MAX_QUEUE_SIZE`, `BRAINTRUST_OTEL_MAX_EXPORT_BATCH_SIZE`, and `BRAINTRUST_OTEL_EXPORT_INTERVAL_MILLIS`. **Breaking change**: `Task`, `Scorer`, and `Classifier` implementations must now be thread-safe. See [API reference](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/java/api-reference.md#evaluations) for details.

### Ruby SDK releases

* [v0.5.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-ruby/releases/tag/v0.5.0) - Added support for [RubyLLM](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/ruby-llm.md) 2.0. Improving eval flushing performance. Fixed `rails g braintrust:server` failing because the initializer template was missing from the published gem.
* [v0.4.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-ruby/releases/tag/v0.4.2) - Fixed a bug where the Playground dropped `origin`, `tags`, and `metadata` on inline dataset rows sent to a remote eval endpoint, forwarding only `input` and `expected`. The Playground uses `origin` to match streamed progress events back to its grid rows, so results appeared to never arrive.

### GitHub eval action releases

* [v2.1.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/eval-action/releases/tag/v2.1.0) - Added `report_scores` and `report_metrics` inputs to filter which scores and metrics appear in the PR comment. Each accepts a comma- or newline-separated list of names. Omitting either input includes all results in that category. The PR comment table now labels scores and metrics in separate sections within a single table. See [Run in CI/CD](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/run-in-ci.md) for details.

### trace-claude-code releases

* [v3.0.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-claude-plugin/releases/tag/v3.0.1) - Claude Code runs `bt` directly for each hook instead of through a Bash script, and stops sending hook events that tracing does not use, such as idle notifications. The plugin no longer installs `bt` when it is missing, so `bt` must be on the `PATH` of the environment that launches Claude Code. See [Claude Code tracing troubleshooting](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md#troubleshooting-tracing).
* [v3.0.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-claude-plugin/releases/tag/v3.0.0) - The `trace-claude-code` tracing integration is unchanged. **Breaking change**: The Braintrust Claude marketplace no longer distributes the MCP-specific `braintrust` plugin. Configure Braintrust MCP directly in [Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md#connect-to-braintrust-mcp), or use a remote connector in [Cowork](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-cowork.md#connect-to-braintrust-mcp).

### trace-codex releases

* [v2.0.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.0.1-codex) - Codex runs `bt` directly for each hook on every platform, so Windows no longer requires Bash. The plugin no longer installs `bt` when it is missing. After updating, trust the changed Braintrust hook through `/hooks`. See the [Codex integration guide](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md#troubleshooting-tracing).
* [v2.0.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.0.0-codex) - The `trace-codex` tracing integration is unchanged. **Breaking change**: The Braintrust Codex marketplace no longer distributes the MCP-specific `braintrust` plugin and its bundled routing skill. Configure Braintrust MCP directly in [Codex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md#connect-to-braintrust-mcp).

### trace-grok releases

* [v0.0.4](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.0.4-grok) - Runs `bt` directly instead of through a Bash script.
* [v0.0.3](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.0.3-grok) - Adds per-turn cache token counts, reasoning tokens, model-call count, API duration, and raw cost metrics when Grok reports them. Recovers the system prompt and first user message on the first model-call span when available.
* [v0.0.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.0.2-grok) - Updates the plugin README with authentication instructions and the per-session hook reload requirement. Tracing behavior is unchanged.
* [v0.0.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.0.1-grok) - Adds [Grok session tracing](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/grok.md), including model-call details, tool activity, and token usage for each turn. Requires `bt` v0.19.3 or later.

### Google Antigravity tracing releases

* [v0.1.3](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v0.1.3-antigravity) - Antigravity runs `bt` directly for each hook, so tracing no longer requires a Unix-compatible `sh`. With `bt` v0.21.0 or later, you can [set up Antigravity tracing](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/antigravity.md) on Windows.

### @braintrust/trace-opencode releases

* [v2.1.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.1.0-opencode) - Refreshes session summaries while the agent is idle. Fixes a delivery flushing issue where a slow flush could block idle handling and shutdown.
* [v2.0.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.0.1-opencode) - Internal maintenance update to OpenCode tracing.
* [v2.0.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.0.0-opencode) - Prevents duplicate traces when you use `bt trace run` with the tracing plugin already installed, and fixes the built-in tool for logging data to Braintrust. **Breaking changes**: The plugin no longer reads environment-variable overrides for tracing enablement, profile, organization, project, or metadata. Save these settings with `bt trace enable opencode`. Nested profile, organization, and project settings now take precedence over their legacy top-level equivalents. See [OpenCode migration instructions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md#migrate-from-an-older-plugin-version).

### @braintrust/pi-extension releases

* [v2.1.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.1.1-pi) - Reduces tracing overhead during streamed responses by sending only the first text or thinking update for each model call, which preserves time to first token. A slow tracing process no longer delays pi startup or event capture.
* [v2.1.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.1.0-pi) - Fixes a delivery flushing issue where a slow flush could block idle handling and shutdown.
