* [v2.0.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-coding-agent-plugins/releases/tag/v2.0.0-pi) - **Breaking change**: The extension no longer reads environment-variable overrides for tracing enablement, profile, organization, project, or metadata. Save these settings with `bt trace enable pi`. Nested profile, organization, and project settings now take precedence over their legacy top-level equivalents. See [pi migration instructions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md#migrate-from-an-older-extension-version).

### Improvements

* **Analyze trace** now shows guide sections as collapsible timeline groups, preserving span order, bars, and hierarchy.

* Loop and the [MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md#tools) now resolve Braintrust organization members, API keys, and service tokens by name, so you can ask which teammate or key produced a set of traces instead of reading raw IDs. See [Explore your data](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/capabilities.md#explore-your-data) for details.

* The [MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md#tools) now resolves and generates links to patterns, prompts, and scorers within their containing projects. Both tools accept `org_name` to disambiguate name lookups across organizations.

* Custom column names that conflict with built-in table fields are now rejected at creation time. See [Create custom columns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/view-logs.md#create-custom-columns) for details.

* GPT-6 Astra, a multimodal reasoning model, is now available in [Loop's model picker](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md#choose-a-provider-and-model), including scheduled automations.

* Long [Loop threads](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/manage.md#view-thread-history) now load faster by loading recent history first, with earlier messages available on demand.

* Loop automations that cannot run because model credits are depleted, the selected built-in model is unavailable, or built-in models are disabled for the organization now show as **Paused** in \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Automations\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/automations) with a warning indicator. Opening the automation shows a banner explaining the reason and links to the credit or AI provider settings. See [Pause an automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#pause-an-automation).

* You can now select GPT-6 Astra in [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md#choose-a-provider-and-model), including scheduled automations.

* [Loop automations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#create-a-loop-automation) now support timezone selection for custom cron schedules. New Pattern discovery automations run daily at 9:00 AM by default.

* You can now copy a shareable link to a dashboard chart that opens it in fullscreen mode, and each chart's move and copy actions now sit directly in its more options menu (). See [Move and copy charts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md#move-and-copy-charts).

* Large traces now download faster from the trace viewer, with a progress indicator showing how many spans have been fetched and the percentage complete.

* The [Braintrust MCP server](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md) now lists connected Slack workspaces and public channels with `list_slack_channels`, helping agents target the intended channel when configuring alerts and automations.

* The [MCP server's `search_patterns` tool](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp#tools) now supports cursor pagination for listings and text searches, so agents can retrieve more than 100 matching patterns.

* GPT-5.6 Luna now supports Max reasoning effort in [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md#choose-a-provider-and-model).

* You can now add filters through the search bar while viewing [live topic distributions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/review-insights.md#real-time-analysis), with autocomplete to help build filters.

* The [**Evaluators** tab](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#test-and-apply-evaluators) in Logs now includes scorers and classifiers from other projects in your organization.

* The [**Usage analytics**](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/monitor-usage.md#view-usage) section now includes a **CSV** export for the **Entire organization** scope, alongside the existing project-level export.

* GPT-6 Luna and GPT-6 Sol are now available in [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md#choose-a-provider-and-model) and scheduled Loop automations. Both are multimodal reasoning models that support Max reasoning effort.

* You can now create Scorer, Loop, and Topics automations from [\*\* Logs\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/logs) without navigating to project settings.

* You can now push code functions and scorers from Node.js 24, and they run in a matching `nodejs24.x` sandbox. See [Remote evals](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/remote-evals.md) for supported runtimes and async behavior requirements on Node.js 24.

* The [span detail panel](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#view-as-a-hierarchy) now shows a **Context** section for spans with a `context` field, such as the span origin recorded by SDK integrations.

* [Translating message content](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#translate-message-content) in the trace viewer is now faster. Translation uses a built-in model by default instead of the model selected in Loop.

* SQL queries and dashboard charts that group by three or more keys now use Brainstore's faster columnar aggregation when every key qualifies for the fast path, and the [SQL sandbox](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql.md#sql-sandbox) and Loop no longer reject them. For a time series chart, the time bucket counts toward the three. See [Aggregate efficiently](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/best-practices.md#aggregate-efficiently) for which keys qualify.

* [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md) now recognizes HTTP and HTTPS URLs as links in your messages. Typing URLs no longer opens command or mention menus.

* [Patterns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/enable.md#common-issues) now surfaces failed automation runs with error messages and direct links to their Loop threads, so you can investigate failures from the Patterns page.

* Clicking a span name in the trace panel now copies it to the clipboard instead of filtering the logs list. This works in every trace panel, not only in Logs. See [Copy a span name](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#copy-a-span-name) for details.

* The [\*\* Logs\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/logs) table now supports a **Dense** row height, so you can scan more rows at once. See [Change the table density](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/view-logs.md#change-the-table-density) for details.

* [Patterns Slack digests](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/enable.md) now include active patterns and patterns closed during the run, excluding patterns that remained closed.

* [Patterns evidence](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/patterns/review.md#examples) now previews up to three prioritized examples and shows saved frequency estimates when available. Copying a pattern for a coding agent includes all attached evidence and measurements.

* You can now open or create a Loop automation directly from the [\*\* Loop\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/loop) page. See [Create a Loop automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#create-a-loop-automation) for details.

* You can now retry failed [Loop automation runs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/automations.md#review-past-runs) to recover from transient failures without starting over or reconfiguring the automation.

* The search dropdown now shows recent SQL filters that start with the text you type, so you can reuse a previous filter without clearing the search field. See [Filter and search logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/filter.md) for details.

**August 2026**

### Dashboards

Monitoring views are now dashboards, with a dedicated page per dashboard and a project-scoped list to browse, search, and star them. Start from an empty dashboard, clone the built-in **Cost and quality** dashboard, or duplicate an existing one, then rename or delete it from the **Dashboard actions** menu. Chart, filter, and grouping changes save automatically. See [Dashboards](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards.md) for details.

### Permissions for organization AI providers

You can now use permissions to control who can access and manage organization-level AI providers. Learn more about [provider permissions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#permissions-and-access).

### Coding-agent plugins trace through bt

Tracing for [Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md), [Codex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md), [OpenCode](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md), and [pi](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) now runs through the [`bt` CLI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace). `bt trace setup` installs or updates the plugin, selects the Braintrust project, and writes the agent's tracing settings. [`bt trace run`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-run) and [`bt trace import`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace#bt-trace-import) cover one-off and saved-session workflows. **Breaking change**: `bt` now handles authentication and trace routing for these plugins. Older plugin-specific API key, project, tracing, and config-file settings must be migrated. See the [`bt` CLI migration guide](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate#v0-16-0) and the upgrade notes for [Claude Code](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/claude-code.md#migrate-from-an-older-plugin-version), [Codex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md#upgrade), [OpenCode](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md#upgrade), and [pi](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md#upgrade).

### MCP write tools

The Braintrust MCP server now exposes write tools, so coding agents can create and update Braintrust objects instead of only reading them. Agents can author prompts, scorers, and classifiers, configure the [Topics](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics.md) pipeline end to end, build dashboards, create alerts and scheduled jobs, run evals, and edit dataset rows. Write tools use the permissions of your authenticated Braintrust account, and several replace or remove objects, so configure your client to require confirmation before running one. See [Braintrust MCP](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md) for the full tool list.

### Kimi K3 and DeepSeek V4 Flash 0731

Kimi K3 and DeepSeek V4 Flash 0731 join GLM-5.2 as built-in open-source models served by Braintrust, with no AI provider setup required. Select them under the **Braintrust** provider in playgrounds, prompts, and scorers, or request `kimi-k3` and `deepseek-v4-flash-0731` through the Braintrust Gateway. Usage draws from your monthly [model credits](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#model-credits), shared with Topics. See [Available models](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#available-models) for details.

### AWS Lambda extension

The Braintrust Lambda Extension gives Python and TypeScript/JavaScript Lambda functions a local handoff path for traces, so the Braintrust SDK's `flush()` method spends less time in the request path. See [AWS Lambda](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/runtime-environments/aws-lambda.md) for setup, compatibility, and considerations.

> [!WARNING]
>
> The Braintrust Lambda extension is in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md) and can change before reaching general availability.

### Group scope for online scoring

Online scoring rules now support **Group** scope, allowing you to evaluate a set of related, multi-turn traces as a single unit rather than one trace at a time. Groups are based on a session key of your choice, and don't require you to change your logging. Group scores can be kept on the first trace in the group and updated as the conversation grows, or written to every trace with prior turns as context. See [Score production traces](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md) for details.

### Pause and resume automations

Log alerts, environment alerts, time window alerts, scheduled Loop jobs, and online scoring rules can now be paused and resumed instead of deleted, so you can stop one during an incident or a noisy deploy without losing its configuration. Each automation shows an **Active** or **Paused** status, along with when it last ran. Events and traces that arrive while an automation is paused aren't processed when it resumes. See [Pause an alert](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/alerts.md#pause-an-alert) and [Pause an automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#pause-an-automation) for details.

### Summary table layout for the experiments list

The experiments list now has a  **Summary table** layout that compares every experiment in a project at a glance, with each experiment as a column and scores and metrics as rows. An **All scores (avg)** row shows the mean of the non-pairwise scores recorded for each experiment. Group the table to replace experiment columns with group values and compare aggregated scores across groups. See [Compare across all experiments](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/compare-experiments.md#compare-across-all-experiments) for details.

### Annotated version history

Describe what changed as you save a new version of a prompt, parameter, or scorer, and the note stays pinned to that version in its history. Version history now also shows each version next to the one it replaced, so you can move directly between either side of a change. See [Version prompts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/prompts/versions.md) for details.

### Trace groups in datasets

A dataset row can now reference a group of traces instead of a single one, so a multi-turn session or a related set of logs becomes one example. Select several traces from your logs and add them as a group, up to 64 per row. The row renders each trace inline and flags any that are no longer available. See [Promote traces from logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets/create.md#promote-traces-from-logs) for details.

### Azure AI Gateway

Braintrust now supports **Azure AI Gateway** as an AI provider, so you can use one provider for all backends behind an Azure API Management endpoint. It supports models that use the OpenAI Chat Completions, OpenAI Responses, or Anthropic Messages API. See [Azure AI Gateway](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/azure-ai-gateway.md) for setup details.

### Query sidebar in the SQL sandbox

> [!NOTE]
>
> The SQL sandbox was renamed [**Query**](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql.md#sql-sandbox) in October 2026.

The SQL sandbox now organizes your queries in a collapsible sidebar, where you can search by name, reorder by dragging, or jump to any query from the command bar. Each query has an  menu to rename, duplicate, or delete it. Click  **Copy share link** to hand a query to a teammate as a URL, which opens in their sandbox without running. See [SQL sandbox](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql.md#sql-sandbox) for details.

### Edit a member's permission groups

You can now manage a member's permission groups directly from \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Members\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/team), rather than opening each group in turn to find where that person belongs. The dialog shows the groups they belong to directly, the ones they inherit through another group's membership, and the ones you can add them to. See [Manage group membership](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control/manage-permissions.md#manage-group-membership) for details.

### Preprocessors for LLM-as-a-judge scorers

Trace-level LLM-as-a-judge scorers can now select the preprocessor that turns the trace into `{{thread}}` and `{{preprocessed}}`. A new **Preprocessors** section in project settings lets you create, test, and manage the preprocessors that scorers, the Thread view, and Topics facets use. See [Choose a preprocessor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/llm-as-a-judge.md#choose-a-preprocessor) and [Manage preprocessors](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/projects.md#manage-preprocessors) for details.

### bt CLI releases

* [v0.18.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.18.0) - Added [`bt environments`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/environments) to manage deployment environments. Added prompt version listing and environment assignment to [`bt prompts`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/prompts), and environment-based version lookup to [`bt functions view`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/functions#bt-functions-view). Added [`--max-concurrency`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval#evaluator-concurrency) to limit how many evaluators run at once. [`bt scorers create`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/scorers) now validates model parameters before saving and returns actionable errors for invalid definitions. **Breaking change**: Removed the `--api-key` flag. Use `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY` or a saved profile instead. See the [v0.18.0 migration steps](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate.md#v0-18-0).
* [v0.17.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.17.0) - Added [`bt profiles`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/profiles) to list, rename, and delete saved login profiles. Added [`bt scorers create`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/scorers) to create prompt-based LLM scorers and classifiers from the CLI. `bt setup` is now deprecated in favor of the standalone setup wizard. Fixed `bt update` on Windows, fixed `bt init` project ID saving, and improved remote eval connection diagnostics.
* [v0.16.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.16.2) - Added automatic Braintrust instrumentation for JavaScript and TypeScript evals in [`bt eval`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval), so supported AI clients and instrumented libraries can emit spans before eval files load. Use `--no-auto-instrumentation` to opt out. This release also fixes `type: "model"` eval parameter serialization and improves `bt init` errors for unwritable `.bt` directories.
* [v0.16.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.16.1) - Improved [`bt trace`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace) so coding-agent tracing setup, per-run tracing, and session imports use the shared plugin runtime. `bt trace run` now waits briefly for trace events from the launched agent to flush before returning, making traced CI and scripted runs more reliable.
* [v0.16.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.16.0) - Added [`bt trace`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/trace) to install and configure supported coding-agent tracing plugins, trace individual agent runs, and import past sessions. `bt` now owns coding-agent tracing authentication through [`bt login`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/login), instead of each plugin reading its own credentials. Added [mise](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/quickstart.md#install) as a supported installation method. Improved [`bt view`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/view) to correctly parse experiment links passed to `--url`. **Breaking changes**: the `bt auth` commands are replaced by top-level commands: `bt auth login` → [`bt login`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/login), `bt auth logout` → [`bt logout`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/logout), `bt auth profiles` → [`bt status --all`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/status), and `bt auth refresh` → [`bt login --refresh`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/login). The `--fresh` flag is renamed to `--force` on [`bt sync pull`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/sync), [`bt sync push`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/sync), and [`bt datasets pipeline push`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/datasets#bt-datasets-pipeline). Profiles are also [decoupled from organizations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate.md#v0-16-0), so you log in once per account and select your organization with [`bt switch`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/switch). The `--api-key` flag is deprecated, hidden from help output, and will be removed soon; use `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY` or [`bt login`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/login) instead. See the [CLI migration guide](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/migrate.md) for migration steps.

### Python SDK releases

* [v0.35.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.35.0) - Added `dataset_id` parameter to [`init_dataset()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/python/api-reference#init_dataset) to initialize a dataset by its ID, taking precedence over `project`, `project_id`, and `name`. Fixed LiteLLM prompt caching metrics not being traced correctly.
* [v0.34.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.34.0) - Added [Hugging Face Transformers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/huggingface.md#setup-python) auto-instrumentation for local pipelines, including text generation, summarization, translation, feature extraction, and question answering. The [Harbor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/harbor.md) plugin now defaults the Braintrust project name to `Harbor`, so passing `project_name` is optional. Eval case fields are now forwarded to scorer functions. Fixed Mistral cached prompt token tracking. Fixed [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md) traces attributing query usage to the final LLM call and creating orphan spans for concurrently responding MCP tools.
* [v0.33.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.33.0) - Added [Vercel AI SDK for Python](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel.md) auto-instrumentation (enabled by default in `auto_instrument()`). Added [Cursor SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cursor-sdk.md) Python instrumentation for tracing agent runs, model turns, and tool calls. Added native [Harbor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/harbor.md) job plugin for syncing Harbor evaluation results to Braintrust.
* [v0.32.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.32.0) - Added [Pipecat](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pipecat.md) auto-instrumentation for tracing real-time voice AI pipelines, including LLM turns, STT, TTS, and tool calls. Added `invoke_async()` as an async counterpart to `invoke()`. **Breaking change**: [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md) audio attachments on `agent_speaking` spans are now disabled by default. Set `BRAINTRUST_CAPTURE_AGENT_AUDIO_ATTACHMENTS=true` to restore the previous behavior.
* [v0.31.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.31.1) - Corrected Google token usage metrics so [Google ADK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/google.md) and [Google GenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md) traces report tool-use, reasoning, and audio and image tokens under standard metrics that reconcile with Google's reported totals.

### TypeScript SDK releases

* [v3.29.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.29.0) - `braintrust eval` now applies auto-instrumentation, so supported AI clients and instrumented libraries can emit spans before eval files load. Added `datasetId` parameter to `initDataset()` to look up a dataset by ID without specifying a project. Added `apiKey` parameter to `loadPrompt()`. [Vercel AI SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel.md) `generateImage` calls are now traced as LLM spans with generated images stored as Braintrust attachments. [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md) thinking tokens and [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) reasoning tokens are now recorded as span metrics. Fixed `noTraceIO` being ignored in `wrapTraced` for plain functions.
* [v3.28.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.28.0) - Added [Voyage AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/voyageai.md) auto-instrumentation for embeddings, multimodal embeddings, reranking, and contextualized embeddings. Scorer functions in `Eval()` now receive the `id` and `tags` of each case. Fixed Claude Agent SDK subagent tool span parenting, and corrected per-call token and cost metrics so query-phase usage is no longer attributed to the final call.
* [v3.27.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.27.0) - Added [Ollama](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/ollama.md) and [`@cloudflare/think`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cloudflare-think) instrumentation, Anthropic beta sessions tracing (`anthropic.beta.sessions.turn` and `anthropic.beta.sessions.thread.turn`), [Flue](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/flue.md) v2 support, and a `vitest-evals` span input override via `meta.eval.input`.
* [v3.26.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.26.0) - Added auto-instrumentation for [Cloudflare Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cloudflare-agents.md), [Cloudflare AI Chat](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/cloudflare-ai-chat.md), and [Hugging Face Transformers.js](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/huggingface.md#transformers-typescript). Added system prompt capture for [Strands Agents SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/strands-agent.md) spans. Fixed token counting for Google GenAI and Google ADK.
* [`@braintrust/langchain-js` v0.2.5](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/%40braintrust%2Flangchain-js%400.2.5) - **Deprecated.** This is the final release of `@braintrust/langchain-js`. Import `BraintrustLangChainCallbackHandler` from `braintrust` instead. Fixed reasoning token recording as a metric on LangChain JS LLM spans. See the [LangChain integration guide](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) for migration details.

### Go SDK releases

* [v0.12.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.12.0) - Adds remote eval support and a new `braintrust.NewEval` API for defining reusable evaluations. Define an eval once with its task, scorers, and `ParameterSchema`, then call `Run` against any dataset for local runs or register it with `evalrunner.RegisterEval` so the Braintrust playground can trigger it from your own infrastructure. Parameters declared in `ParameterSchema` become configurable controls in the playground and reach the task via `hooks.Parameters`. Also adds `eval.TaskWithHooks` for tasks that need to read parameters, and `DatasetQueryOpts.ProjectID` and `ProjectName` for scoped dataset lookups. **Go remote evals are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md).** See [Remote evals](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/remote-evals.md) and [Go API reference](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/go/api-reference.md#evaluations) for details.
* [v0.11.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.11.1) - Aligned [CloudWeGo Eino](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/cloudwego-eino.md), [Google GenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md), and [Firebase Genkit](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/firebase-genkit.md) instrumentation with the Braintrust spec. Firebase Genkit gains `WithProvider` and `WithModel` options on `NewMiddleware` for explicit model attribution, plus traced tool wrappers `DefineTool`, `DefineToolWithInputSchema`, and `DefineMultipartTool`. Auto-instrumentation now replaces `genkit.DefineTool`, `genkit.DefineToolWithInputSchema`, and `genkit.DefineMultipartTool` calls with their traced equivalents. Updates Orchestrion to v1.12.1. **Breaking change (Google GenAI)**: Provider metadata changed from `"gemini"` to `"google"`. Update trace queries that filter on the previous provider value. **Breaking change (Eino)**: ChatModel span output is now an OpenAI-compatible choices array (`[{"index": 0, "finish_reason": "...", "message": {...}}]`) instead of a flat message map. Embedding input is now `{"inputs": [{"content": "..."}]}` and output is `{"count": N}`, removing `embedding_length` and renaming `embeddings_count`. Provider metadata is now lowercase (for example, `"openai"` instead of `"OpenAI"`). Update trace queries that rely on the previous formats.
* [v0.11.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.11.0) - Aligned [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md), [Bedrock Runtime](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock.md), and [Google ADK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/google.md) instrumentation with the Braintrust spec. Anthropic spans now capture `prompt_cache_creation_5m_tokens` and `prompt_cache_creation_1h_tokens` for TTL-specific prompt caching. Bedrock Runtime spans now capture audio and video content blocks, and `InvokeModelWithResponseStream` is now fully instrumented for Anthropic Claude models. Google ADK spans now include reasoning and cached token metrics. **Breaking change (Anthropic)**: Span metadata no longer includes `endpoint`. The output field is now a single message object instead of an array. Non-streaming spans no longer emit `time_to_first_token`. **Breaking change (Bedrock)**: Span metadata renames `stop_sequences` to `stop` and removes `additional_model_request_fields`. Image, document, and tool block shapes align with Bedrock's native wire format.

### Java SDK releases

* [v0.3.22](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.22) - [LangChain4j](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain4j.md) instrumentation now covers the OpenAI Responses API (`OpenAiResponsesChatModel`, `OpenAiResponsesStreamingChatModel`) for LangChain4j 1.14.0 or later. OpenAI and Anthropic LLM spans now capture vendor request IDs (`x-request-id` and `request-id` respectively) as span attributes. Fixes OpenAI audio transcription output capture, missing OpenAI and Anthropic generation parameters in span metadata, and Anthropic system prompts dropped from span input when using the prompt-caching array format. AWS Bedrock Converse spans now capture streaming tool-use and non-text content blocks, normalized reasoning (extended thinking) blocks, prompt caching metrics, and tool definitions.
* [v0.3.21](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.21) - Web search, file search, code interpreter, and MCP calls in the OpenAI Responses API, and `server_tool_use` blocks in the Anthropic Messages API now appear as child `tool` spans nested under the LLM span. Anthropic spans also capture `server_tool_use_*` metrics (for example, `server_tool_use_web_search_requests`). **Breaking change**: The Google GenAI provider metadata field changed from `"gemini"` to `"google"`. Update trace queries that filter on the previous value.

### Ruby SDK releases

* [v0.4.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-ruby/releases/tag/v0.4.1) - All spans now carry [`braintrust.context_json`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/opentelemetry/attributes#braintrust-attributes) with SDK name, version, instrumentation scope, and detected runtime environment (set `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_NAME` and `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_TYPE` to override). **Breaking change**: Eval span attribute names and formats now conform to the Braintrust spec used by other SDKs: `braintrust.expected` is renamed to `braintrust.expected_json`, `braintrust.input_json` and `braintrust.output_json` now store the raw value rather than a wrapper object, and on task failure `braintrust.output_json` is omitted instead of set to `null`. `braintrust.expected_json` is now also written to task spans in addition to eval spans. Update any SQL queries or span-attribute handling that relied on the old names or format.

### C# SDK releases

* [v0.2.12](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-dotnet/releases/tag/v0.2.12) - Added remote dataset support: call `FetchDatasetAsync<TInput, TOutput>("my-dataset")` on the `Braintrust` instance to pull eval cases directly from a Braintrust dataset, fetched a page at a time and linked back to their source records. `BraintrustOpenApiClient` is now the primary API client, replacing the deprecated `DefaultBraintrustApiClient`, `BraintrustApiClient`, and `IBraintrustApiClient`. Access it via `braintrust.OpenApiClient`. `Eval<TInput, TOutput>` now implements `IDisposable`, so use `using var eval = ...` to release its internal HTTP client. See [API reference](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/csharp/api-reference.md) for details.
* [v0.2.11](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-dotnet/releases/tag/v0.2.11) - Added `DefaultBraintrustApiClient.Api`, a generated client for the full Braintrust REST API. The client covers every REST endpoint and ships inside the existing `Braintrust.Sdk` package with no separate install. See [API client](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/csharp/api-reference.md#api-client) for details.

### Improvements

* The **Edit billing information** dialog now includes a **Billing address** section, so you can update your billing address without replacing the card on file. Click **Edit address** under **Billing address**. See [Update your billing address](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/faq.md#how-do-i-update-my-billing-address) for details.
* Custom views now support writing scores via `trace.update`, so you can capture numeric feedback without leaving the view. See [Add interactive controls](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/custom-views.md#add-interactive-controls) for details.
* The **Edit billing information** dialog now includes a **Purchase order** field (added to the memo on future invoices) and **Tax information** fields (country and tax ID type shown on invoices). See [Enable on-demand usage](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/change-plan.md#enable-on-demand-usage) for details.
* Starter and Pro plan subscribers can now redeem coupon codes directly from the billing page. Click **Redeem coupon** under **Current plan** on \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Billing\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/billing). See [Redeem a coupon](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/change-plan.md#redeem-a-coupon) for details.
* Starter plan users can now disable on-demand billing immediately from the **Edit billing information** dialog. The card stays on file and plan limits apply from that point forward. See [Disable on-demand usage](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/change-plan.md#disable-on-demand-usage) for details.
* Images that point to your local or private network, or that use an unsafe URL scheme, now require approval before loading, even in **Auto-load images** mode. See [Image rendering controls](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#control-image-rendering) for details.
* The Monitor chart editor now warns you when a measure, filter, or group-by is likely to make a chart slow, and asks you to confirm before saving a chart that still has warnings. See [Create custom charts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md) for details.
* The **Assigned to** filter in the **Basic** tab now lets you select permission groups in addition to individual members. See [Apply a filter](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/filter.md#apply-a-filter) for details.
* The SQL sandbox now shows a performance warning on `LIKE` and `ILIKE` operators and offers a **Use MATCH** quick fix to convert them to the indexed `MATCH` operator. See [Pattern matching](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#pattern-matching) for details.
* On the Starter plan, you can now use GLM-5.2 with your monthly [model credits](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#model-credits) without first enabling on-demand usage. GLM-5.2 becomes unavailable once your credits are exhausted, until you enable on-demand usage or upgrade to Pro. See [AI providers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#available-models) for details.
* You can now add rows to a dataset in bulk from [\*\* Review\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/review). Select the rows, then click  **Add to** and choose a dataset. See [Promote traces from logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets/create.md#promote-traces-from-logs) for details.
* Online scoring automations now support bulk rewind, so you can re-process several rules from a single start time instead of rewinding each one separately. See [Rewind an automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#rewind-an-automation) for details.
* The view API now accepts an optional `description` and returns an `updated_at` timestamp, set whenever a view is created or modified. Views not modified since the field was added report `updated_at` as `null`. See [Create view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/api-reference/views/create-view.md) for details.
* **Group by** and **Cluster by** now live in the row type selector in the toolbar, and traces are not grouped by default, so you opt into grouping when you want to review multi-turn conversations end to end. See [Group related traces](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/view-logs.md#group-related-traces) for details.
* You can now group related traces on the [\*\* Review\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/review) page when reviewing **Log spans**, so a conversation's traces stay together as you work through a review queue. See [Group related log traces](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review/manage-review-work.md#group-related-log-traces) for details.
* Combining a single `ANY_SPAN()` with bare filter conditions now applies every condition to the same span, which lets the query planner evaluate these queries more efficiently. A query that previously matched its conditions against different spans returns fewer rows until you wrap each condition in its own `ANY_SPAN()` call. See [Single span filters](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#single-span-filters) for details.
* You can now filter the logs list to a specific span name by clicking that span's name in the trace panel header, instead of building the filter by hand. See [Apply a filter](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/filter.md#apply-a-filter) for details.
* If you self-host Braintrust on AWS, you can now run [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md) in your own data plane by enabling the Loop runtime, an opt-in ECS service that executes each Loop session in an isolated MicroVM sandbox. It requires AWS Terraform module v6.5.2 or later and a [supported AWS region](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/self-hosting/configure/loop-runtime#supported-aws-regions). See [Loop runtime](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/self-hosting/configure/loop-runtime) for details.
* The **Basic** filter tab's value picker is now searchable, and you can apply a value that isn't in the list by typing it directly. See [Apply a filter](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/filter.md#apply-a-filter) for details.
* Hovering a row in the **List** view of a Topics facet card now reveals a  **View logs** link that opens [\*\* Logs\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/logs) filtered to that topic and every topic nested under it. See [Review insights](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/review-insights.md#snapshot-view) for details.
* Custom preprocessors whose output isn't a recognized message array now render in the thread layout as text labeled with the preprocessor's name, instead of failing. See [Write a custom preprocessor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/enable.md#write-a-custom-preprocessor) for details.
* Service accounts now show the project permissions they inherit from their permission groups, and you can now manage a service account's group membership from the Service tokens page. See [Use service accounts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control/manage-permissions.md#use-service-accounts) for details.
* Slack link previews now distinguish trace details by your Braintrust identity: if you open a shared trace's details without linking your account, you are prompted to connect it, and if you are linked but lack access to the trace's project, you see a restricted-access notice instead of a generic missing-trace error. See [Braintrust link previews in Slack](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#braintrust-link-previews-in-slack).

**July 2026**

### Pairwise scoring

When you compare two experiments in diff mode, you can now record which one produced the better output for each row. Braintrust aggregates these head-to-head judgments into a win rate, so you can run A-vs-B human evaluations without configuring a separate scoring function. See [Pairwise scoring](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/compare-experiments.md#pairwise-scoring) for details.

### Trace references in datasets

When you add a trace to a dataset from [\*\* Logs\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/logs), you can now choose  **Add full trace to dataset** to store a reference to the trace in the row's `input` instead of copying a single span. Braintrust renders these rows with a read-only **Trace** viewer that shows the referenced trace inline. Because the row stores a reference rather than a copy, it depends on the trace remaining available under your retention window. See [Promote traces from logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets/create.md#promote-traces-from-logs) for details.

### Interactive controls in custom views

Custom views can now write back to your data instead of only displaying it. Add buttons, inputs, and other controls that update span metadata and tags in trace views, or metadata, input, expected output, and tags in dataset views. This turns a custom view into a purpose-built annotation surface, so reviewers can capture positive or negative feedback, correct dataset inputs and expected outputs, and apply tags without leaving the view. See [Add interactive controls](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/custom-views.md#add-interactive-controls) for details.

### Slack notifications for review assignments

When the Slack integration is enabled, assignees who have linked their Slack account now receive a Slack direct message when spans or dataset rows are assigned to them for review, in addition to the existing email notification. The message previews the assigned items and links directly to them. See [Assign rows for review](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review/manage-review-work.md#assign-rows-for-review) for details.

### API key controls

Organizations have finer control over credential lifetime and creation. API keys and service tokens take an expiration date at creation, and organization owners can prevent members from creating new API keys without affecting keys that already exist. See [Manage API keys](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#manage-api-keys), [Disable API key creation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#disable-api-key-creation), and [Create service tokens](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#create-service-tokens) for details.

### Trace search improvements

Searching within a trace is faster and easier to navigate on large traces. **Full trace** searches default to whole-word matching, which is significantly faster than substring matching, and you can switch back with **Match whole words**. Navigation steps through results one occurrence at a time across span and field boundaries, with a per-field match count shown in the results panel. Clicking a result switches the span detail panel to the tab containing the matched field. See [Search within a trace](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#search-within-a-trace) for details.

### Search bar autocomplete

The search bar suggests completions as you type. **Suggestions** offers field name completions, and **Recent** lists your most recent text searches and SQL filters, so you can re-run a previous query without retyping it. When Loop is enabled, select  **Generate SQL with Loop** to open the SQL filter editor with your typed text as a prompt. Text search chips in the filter bar are editable, so you can revise a search term inline instead of clearing the filter and starting over. See [Filter and search logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/filter.md) for details.

### Extended data retention

On the Pro plan, you can now keep logs and playground data, including each row's activity history, queryable for longer, with up to 180-day retention, for an additional storage charge. See [Configure data retention](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/data-management/retention.md#extend-the-retention-window) and [Plans and limits](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#usage-limits) for details.

### GLM-5.2

Braintrust now offers [GLM-5.2](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#available-models) as a built-in model — no need to configure your own AI provider. In playgrounds, prompts, and scorers, it is available under the **Braintrust** model provider, and you can also call it through the Braintrust gateway. Usage draws from your monthly [credit](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#model-credits), shared with Topics. For details, see [AI providers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#available-models).

### Skip non-applicable cases in LLM-as-a-judge scorers

LLM-as-a-judge scorers now support an **Allow skip** toggle. When enabled, the judge can return an implicit **Skip** choice that scores as `null`, so non-applicable test cases are excluded from score aggregates instead of lowering them. See [LLM-as-a-judge](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/llm-as-a-judge.md#skip-non-applicable-cases) for details.

### Slack link previews

When the Slack integration is enabled, Braintrust expands links to logs, traces, and experiments that you share in Slack, so your team gets context on a resource without opening it. Private resource details are only shown to Slack users who have linked their Slack account to a Braintrust account with access to the resource. See [Braintrust link previews in Slack](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#braintrust-link-previews-in-slack) for details.

### Query SQL data sources by name

Most SQL table functions now accept object names in addition to IDs, so you can write queries like `experiment('my-experiment')` or `project_logs('my-project')` without first looking up a UUID. When the same name exists in multiple projects, disambiguate with a two-element array (`experiment(['my-project', 'my-experiment'])`). See [Data sources](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#data-sources) for the supported functions.

### Python SDK releases

* [v0.31.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.31.0) - The dev server now forwards dataset version and environment selection to remote evals. Fixed remote eval parameters without a `description` serializing `"description": null`, which failed playground validation and hid every eval on the endpoint. Fixed [Google ADK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/google.md) stream cleanup being logged as errors during subagent delegation. Fixed [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) async runs inheriting ended spans. Fixed span metadata handling in [Pydantic AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md) and [Strands](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/strands-agent.md) integrations. Fixed error logging in the [Temporal](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/temporal.md) integration.
* [v0.30.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.30.1) - Fixed async streaming for the [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md) integration: on async clients using `with_streaming_response`, `parse()` now returns an awaitable as expected. **Breaking change**: The OpenAI and [OpenRouter](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openrouter.md) integrations now allowlist span metadata, so internal request fields that were previously captured are no longer recorded. For example, OpenAI no longer records `extra_headers`. Update any queries, saved views, or automations that read these fields.
* [v0.30.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.30.0) - [LiteLLM](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/litellm.md) now resolves the actual underlying provider name (e.g., `"openai"`, `"anthropic"`) in span metadata instead of always reporting `"litellm"`. [LlamaIndex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/llamaindex.md) LLM calls are now typed as task spans instead of LLM spans, avoiding duplicate LLM spans when a provider integration is also active. [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md), [Instructor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/instructor.md), [Google GenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md), and LlamaIndex integrations now include `model` and `provider` fields in span metadata. Remote eval scorers that return multiple scores are now recorded correctly instead of erroring. **Breaking change**: Several integrations changed what appears in span metadata. Tool definitions are now recorded in `metadata.tools` for Google GenAI and LangChain spans (previously in `input` for Google GenAI, and under the span's invocation parameters in `metadata` for LangChain). The LangChain, [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md), and Google GenAI integrations now allowlist span metadata, so internal fields that were previously captured are no longer recorded; for example, LiveKit Agents no longer includes the full conversation context (`chat_ctx`). Update any queries, saved views, or automations that read these fields from their old locations.
* [v0.29.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.29.0) - Spans now carry a per-integration source identifier in `context.span_origin.instrumentation.name` (e.g., `openai-auto`, `anthropic-auto`), so you can filter and query traces by the SDK integration that created each span. Improved span data quality for [CrewAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/crew-ai.md) (provider metadata, tools routed to metadata), [DSPy](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/dspy.md) (aligned span shape, removed nested LLM spans), [Agno](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/agno.md) (tools routed to metadata, media attachments materialized, exceptions propagated), [Bedrock Runtime](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock.md) (metrics, tools, and attachments), [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md) (provider metadata, hook field allowlist), [ADK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/google.md) (provider, tools, errors in LLM span metadata), and [AgentScope](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/agentscope.md). **Breaking change**: For CrewAI and Agno spans, tool definitions are now recorded in `metadata.tools` instead of `input`. Update any queries or saved views that read tool schemas from `input`.
* [v0.28.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.28.0) - Added span origin provenance. Spans now automatically record the execution environment (CI provider, cloud platform, or custom `PYTHON_ENV`), overridable via `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_TYPE` and `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_NAME`. Fixed [LiteLLM](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/litellm.md) span metadata to use an allowlist, preventing internal LiteLLM fields from leaking into spans. Fixed custom `PYTHON_ENV` values being dropped from span origin.
* [v0.27.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.27.0) - Added tracing for anthropic `messages.parse` structured output calls, capturing `parsed_output` alongside standard message fields. Fixed function invocation errors on EU and self-hosted deployments.

### TypeScript SDK releases

* [v3.25.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.25.0) - Expanded [Pi Coding Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pi-coding-agent.md) support to v0.81.x. Added [`@openrouter/sdk`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openrouter) v1 compatibility. Fixed eval tasks hanging until timeout when tasks complete early. Fixed [AI SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel.md) `HarnessAgent` trace context propagation across suspended turns.
* [v3.24.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.24.0) - Added tracing for the [Vercel AI SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel.md) `HarnessAgent` class (`@ai-sdk/harness` v1.x), including `generate`, `stream`, `continueGenerate`, and `continueStream` turns. Fixed [Eve](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/eve.md) LLM reasoning capture across workflow resumes and [LangChain JS](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) total token metrics.
* [v3.23.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.23.1) - Cleaned up Claude Agent SDK span metadata to reduce trace noise.
* [v3.23.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.23.0) - Added [LangSmith](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langsmith.md#tracing-typescript) auto-instrumentation and wrapper functions (`wrapLangSmithTraceable`, `wrapLangSmithRunTrees`, `wrapLangSmithClient`) for tracing LangSmith runs in Braintrust. Eve turns are now tracked as individual traces, and the Eve session ID is recorded on all spans. Strands agents now log inline document, image, and video content as Braintrust attachments rather than embedded bytes. Fixed Mastra `time_to_first_token` capture for streaming responses.
* [v3.22.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.22.0) - Added `braintrustEveHook` and `braintrustEveInstrumentation` for tracing [Eve](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/eve.md) agent sessions, turns, LLM steps, tool calls, and subagent interactions. Fixed Mastra `logFeedback` routing: the Braintrust row ID now matches the Mastra span ID so feedback attaches to the correct span instead of landing as a stray row.
* [v3.21.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust%403.21.0) - Added `injectTraceContext` and `extractTraceContextFromHeaders` for [W3C trace-context propagation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/api-reference.md#injecttracecontext) across HTTP service boundaries, compatible with OpenTelemetry and other W3C-compliant systems. Added [`agentAssertionScorer`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/api-reference#agentassertionscorer), a trace scorer that evaluates agents through declarative assertions on tool calls and outputs. Added tracing for the Vercel AI SDK [`WorkflowAgent`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel#trace-agents-typescript) class. The SDK now forwards the [`bt eval --sample N`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval) flag to the dataset loader, fetching only the sampled rows. Fixed AI SDK total token metrics synthesis, concurrent model-instance span parenting, AI SDK v7 parent span lifecycle, and AI SDK tool spans capturing the full message history in their input.

### Java SDK releases

* [v0.3.20](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.20) - Fixes streaming span output across OpenAI, LangChain4j, and Spring AI (OpenAI and Anthropic backends): tool-call argument fragments are now merged into the reconstructed span output instead of being dropped. Streaming Anthropic responses in Spring AI now also preserve `tool_use` blocks and `thinking` blocks. OpenAI Chat Completions spans now capture `completion_reasoning_tokens` from `completion_tokens_details` for reasoning models such as o1 and o3.
* [v0.3.19](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.19) - Adds [Spring AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/spring-ai.md) 2.x instrumentation for `OpenAiChatModel` and `AnthropicChatModel`, available through manual wrapping with `BraintrustSpringAI.wrap()` or auto-instrumentation. Async OpenAI and Anthropic clients are now supported, both when wrapped manually and auto-instrumented. Async and streaming calls now nest correctly under the span that started them.
* [v0.3.18](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.18) - Adds span origin provenance. Spans now automatically record the execution environment (CI provider or cloud platform), overridable via `BraintrustConfig.Builder.environment(...)`, `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_TYPE`, or `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_NAME`.
* [v0.3.17](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.17) - Eval spans record `input` and `output` as raw values (e.g., `"hello"` rather than `{"input": "hello"}`), and the task span carries its own `input`, `output`, and `expected`. **Breaking change**: if you read these span attributes directly, `braintrust.input_json` and `braintrust.output_json` now contain the raw value, `braintrust.expected` is renamed to `braintrust.expected_json`, and `braintrust.output_json` is absent when a task throws.
* [v0.3.16](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.16) - Adds typed deserialization for datasets, remote evals, and custom scorers. `braintrust.fetchDataset(...)` and `RemoteEval.builder(...)` now take input and output `Class` arguments (or converter functions) so fetched cases deserialize into your own types instead of raw maps, and `braintrust.fetchScorer(...)` gains matching converter overloads. Custom scorer arguments now serialize with `BraintrustJsonMapper` (snake\_case field names, honoring `@JsonIgnore`) by default. OpenAI spans now capture `prompt_cached_tokens` from both the Chat Completions and Responses APIs. The untyped `fetchDataset` and no-arg `RemoteEval.builder()` overloads are deprecated.
* [v0.3.15](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.15) - The dev server now supports [remote eval](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/remote-evals.md) experiment snapshot runs triggered from the Braintrust UI, creating a distinct experiment for each run. To support this, added the `Eval.Builder.ensureNew(boolean)` option, which forces `Eval.run()` to always create a new experiment even when one with the same name already exists (the backend deduplicates the name). `EvalResult` now also exposes `getExperimentId()` and `getExperimentName()` alongside `getExperimentUrl()`, with the URL now reflecting the backend-resolved experiment name.

### @braintrust/trace-opencode releases

See the [OpenCode integration guide](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md) for details.

### trace-codex releases

* [v0.1.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-codex-plugin/releases/tag/trace-codex-v0.1.0) - Session spans now capture Git attribution metadata (`git_origin_url` with credentials redacted, `git_branch`, and `git_commit_sha`). When Codex reads a `SKILL.md` file, the tool span is renamed to `skill: <skill-name>` and carries `skill_name` and `skill_path` metadata. Prompts with explicit skill requests also record `loaded_skill_names` and `loaded_skills` on the turn span, and the skill span gains `skill_load_trigger: "explicit"`. Added `parentSpanId` and `rootSpanId` configuration to attach a Codex session under an existing Braintrust span. All spans now carry span origin provenance identifying them as `braintrust.plugin.codex`, with the execution environment auto-detected from standard CI and platform environment variables and overridable via `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_TYPE` and `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_NAME`. See the [Codex integration guide](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md) for details.

All spans now carry span origin provenance identifying them as `braintrust.plugin.pi`, with the execution environment auto-detected from standard CI and platform environment variables and overridable via `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_TYPE` and `BRAINTRUST_ENVIRONMENT_NAME`. See the [pi integration guide](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) for details.

When pi reads a `SKILL.md` file, the tool span is renamed to `skill: <skill-name>` and carries `skill_name` and `skill_path` metadata. Prompts with explicit `/skill:<name>` requests also record `loaded_skill_names` and `loaded_skills` on the turn span, and the skill span gains `skill_load_trigger: "explicit"`. See [Run in CI/CD](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/run-in-ci.md) for details.

### Improvements

* Spans ingested via OpenTelemetry now automatically include a `context.span_origin` field that records the span's provenance, including the SDK name, SDK version, and instrumentation scope. See [OpenTelemetry integration](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/opentelemetry/attributes.md#braintrust-attributes) for details.
* Loop now uses project-level AI provider secrets, so models configured as project-level providers are available in Loop sessions opened within that project. See [Configure AI providers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#project-providers) for details.
* You can now delete an AI provider directly while editing it. See [Delete a provider](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#delete-a-provider) for details.
* GPT-5.6 Sol, Terra, and Luna are now available in [Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop.md), and GPT-5.6 Sol is the recommended default when OpenAI is configured as an organization AI provider.
* You can now disable frontend Loop logging in \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Loop\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/loop). See [Control Loop logging](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#control-loop-logging) for more info.
* The tag filter dropdown on the experiments table now surfaces tags inferred from recent logged data alongside configured project tags, without requiring explicit registration.
* Span errors now appear on both the **Messages** and **Details** tabs of the span detail panel, so errors are visible alongside the input and output that produced them. See [Inspect a trace](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#inspect-a-trace) for details.
* Classifier results now appear as individual, filterable, and sortable columns in the experiments and logs tables and in playground results, with one column per classifier. In experiments and playgrounds, you can also group rows by a classifier's label. See [Classifiers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md#classifiers) for details.
* \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Permission groups\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/groups) now paginates (50 groups per page) and includes a search box to filter by name or description. See [Manage permission groups](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control/manage-permissions.md) for details.
* The prompt editor now supports Anthropic cache control on individual messages and content blocks when using Anthropic or AWS Bedrock (Converse API) models, with TTL options of 5 minutes or 1 hour. See [Create prompts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/prompts/create.md) for details.
* The prompt editor now allows the `temperature` parameter for GPT-5.1 and later models when reasoning effort is set to `none`. Raising the reasoning effort removes the `temperature` setting, matching OpenAI's parameter support. See [Create prompts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/prompts/create.md) for details.
* You can link your Slack and Braintrust accounts on demand by running `/braintrust link` in any Slack channel, or from the prompt shown when you open the Slack App Home. If your Braintrust and Slack email addresses differ, the authorization page offers a **Switch account** option. See [Braintrust link previews in Slack](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#braintrust-link-previews-in-slack) for details.
* You can now copy your organization ID or name directly from the organization switcher dropdown. See [Find your organization ID](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#find-your-organization-id) for details.
* You can now delete a human review score directly from its edit panel, both in settings and while reviewing inline. See [Set up human review](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md#create-edit-and-delete-scores-inline) for details.
* While reviewing experiment spans, you can now enable diff mode and select a comparison experiment to compare traces inline as you score. See [Score traces and datasets](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review/score-traces.md#change-the-trace-layout) for details.
* The **Invoices** dropdown on the \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Billing\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/billing) page now includes card validation fee receipts for pay-as-you-go users alongside regular usage invoices. See [Get an invoice or receipt](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/faq.md#how-do-i-get-an-invoice-or-receipt) for details.
* When **Reconcile with previous version** is enabled for a Topics facet, preserved topics now receive updated descriptions based on their current content rather than keeping the description from first creation. See [Tune clustering settings](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/manage.md#tune-clustering-settings) for details.
* Pausing and resuming Topics automations from the UI now requires organization owner permissions. See [Pause/resume automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/manage.md#pause-resume-automation) for details.
* The `sql_query` MCP tool now handles large results gracefully: results exceeding 1 MB are uploaded to object storage and returned as a signed `overflow_url` instead of inline data, preventing context overflow. Set `return_url: true` to request a URL even for results below the threshold. See [Braintrust MCP](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/mcp.md#tools) for details.
* Custom facet exclusion patterns and JSON schema `pattern`/`patternProperties` values are now validated for safety when you save them. Patterns that use lookahead, lookbehind, or backreferences are rejected with a clear error. See [Custom facets](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/custom-facets.md#create-a-facet) and [Define schemas](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets/manage.md#define-schemas) for details.
* The **Live tail** time range in [\*\* Logs\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/logs) is now a distinct option separate from the **1 hour** fixed-window range. Live mode reliably delivers new rows, highlights newly arrived rows with a flash animation, and pauses polling when the browser tab is hidden. See [View your logs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/view-logs.md#browse-logs) for details.
* Experiment comparison diffs now let you choose the diff direction explicitly, so you can control which experiment is the baseline. Your last-used direction is saved for future comparisons. See [Use diff mode](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/compare-experiments.md#use-diff-mode) for details.
* Custom columns are now available when reviewing **Log spans** on the [\*\* Review\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/review) page, so you can surface metadata, scores, or nested values alongside review work. See [Create custom columns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review/manage-review-work.md#create-custom-columns) for details.
* The **Assigned to** column is now a built-in column in the logs, experiment, and dataset tables, making reviewer assignments visible at a glance without a custom column workaround. See [Assign rows for review](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review/manage-review-work.md#assign-rows-for-review) for details.
* Overriding code or graph (workflow) functions that have stored env secrets now requires `update` permission. Read-only callers that attempt to pass overrides to such functions receive an access denied error. See [Override function data per call](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/functions.md#override-function-data-per-call) for details.
* Monitor time series charts can now use `metrics.start` (event start time) instead of `created` (ingestion time) as the time dimension, which improves chart accuracy for projects that batch-ingest historical spans. See [Configure Monitor chart time dimension](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/projects.md#configure-monitor-chart-time-dimension) for details.
* Custom views now support grouped sessions. When a view is open on a session grouped from multiple traces, the `trace` object's spans, field fetches, and updates operate across every trace in the session, up to a limit of 1,000 traces. See [Custom views](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/custom-views.md) for details.
* `openai.gpt-5.3` and later models on AWS Bedrock now route through Bedrock's Responses API instead of Chat Completions, so they work correctly through the Braintrust gateway. See [AWS Bedrock](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock.md) for details.
* A new [cookbook on supervising agent behavior with behavior specs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/cookbook/recipes/AgentBehavior.md) covers trajectory-level evals: defining expected agent behaviors in a spec and grading each one with an LLM judge.
* The **Organization AI providers** and **Project AI providers** tables are now visible in read-only mode to members without permission to edit them, so they can see which providers are configured. Redacted key previews remain visible only to members who can edit each section. See [Configure AI providers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md#permissions-and-access) for details.
* Project and organization automation configuration changes are now captured in the [audit log](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/audit-logs.md#events), with create, update, and delete events for each. Webhook action URLs are redacted.
* Applied scorers and classifiers on the \*\* Evaluators\*\* tab now show their recorded rationale inline, so you can see why a score or label was assigned without opening the span that produced it. See [Test and apply evaluators](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#test-and-apply-evaluators) for details.

**June 2026**

### Improved SDK documentation

Braintrust's documentation now has a dedicated [SDKs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks.md) tab, with sections for TypeScript, Python, Go, Java, Ruby, and C#. Each language has a quickstart, an install-and-instrument guide, and a curated API reference. See [SDKs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks.md) to get started.

### Audit logging

Braintrust now records administrative actions across your organization, such as creating projects, changing settings, granting permissions, managing members, and creating API keys. View recent activity in  **Settings** >  **Audit log** with time range, filter, and column controls, or query the full history with [SQL](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql.md) from the SQL sandbox, the [`bt sql`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/sql) CLI, or the API. Organizations with strict data access requirements can also enable auditing of data reads. See [Audit logging](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/audit-logs.md) for details.

> [!NOTE]
>
> Audit logging is only available on the [Enterprise plan](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#plans).

### Gateway provider failover

Gateway requests can now set `x-bt-fallback-providers` to retry the same request against fallback provider credentials when the primary provider returns a retryable provider error. Responses include headers showing which provider completed the request and, when failover succeeds, the original and final `model/provider` pairs. See [Enable provider failover](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/gateway.md#enable-provider-failover) for details.

### Trace redesign

Selecting a span now opens its detail panel to the **Messages** tab, focused on the highest-signal content first. When [topics](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics.md) are enabled, the matched topics and facet summaries appear at the top, followed by the span's input and output messages, tool calls, and annotations. Metadata, metrics, scores, and raw data remain available in the other tabs. See [Examine traces](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#inspect-a-trace) for details.

### Score visibility for human review

Human review scores now support an optional visibility setting. Restrict a score to specific members or permission groups so only relevant reviewers see it during review, which keeps the review experience focused for large teams, or leave it unset to keep the score visible to everyone. See [Restrict score visibility](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md#restrict-score-visibility) for details.

### Conditional human review scores

Human review scores now support filter conditions that control when a score appears in the review panel. Set a **Show when** condition on a score to surface it only when its filter expression evaluates to true for the span being reviewed — for example, show a detailed rubric only when a triage score is below a threshold, or a correction score only when the expected output matches a specific category. Conditions use SQL syntax and can reference other scores, expected values, and metadata. See [Show scores conditionally](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md#show-scores-conditionally) for details.

### Prompt and scorer versions in playground tasks

When you add a saved prompt or scorer as a playground task, you can now build the task from a specific historical version instead of always getting the latest, making it easier to compare versions side by side and reproduce past results. See [Add tasks](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/playgrounds.md#add-tasks) for details.

### Dataset sampling in playgrounds

Playground runs now support a sample rate control that evaluates a random subset of dataset rows, so you can iterate faster on large datasets before promoting a configuration to an experiment. See [Sample rate](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/playgrounds.md#sample-rate) for details.

### Rewind online scoring automations

When you update a scorer or need to correct scoring results, you can now rewind an online scoring automation to re-process traces from a specific timestamp forward. This is useful for re-evaluating recent traces after scorer updates, fixing interrupted scoring runs, or addressing scoring errors. See [Rewind an automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#rewind-an-automation) for details.

### Classifiers

Classifiers are scorer-like evaluators that return a categorical label instead of a numeric score, so you can categorize by a dimension such as sentiment, issue type, or policy category. They run wherever scorers do, across eval cases in experiments and on production traces via online scoring. Classifications surface alongside scores and appear as columns you can sort and filter in experiment and trace tables. In logs, you can also promote a category's traces into a dataset, turning production patterns into evaluation test cases. See [Classifiers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md#classifiers) for details.

### Multi-user human review

Multiple reviewers can now score the same span independently without overwriting each other's work. Braintrust stores each reviewer's scores separately and automatically averages them on the parent span, with a breakdown of your own reviews, all reviews of the span, and all spans in the trace. For score types that can't be averaged, such as free-form text and categorical scores that write to expected output, you can choose which reviewer's value becomes the parent span's value. See [Add human feedback](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md) for details.

### Programmatic service token creation

Organization owners can now create service tokens through the API. Call [`POST /v1/service_token`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/api-reference/servicetokens/create-service-token) and authenticate with a service token that has organization-owner permissions, or include `token_name` when creating a service account through [`PATCH /v1/organization/members`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/api-reference/organizations/modify-organization-membership). API key creation remains UI-only, and user API keys cannot be used to create service tokens. See [Create service tokens](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#create-service-tokens) for the supported flows.

### Run remote evals and sandboxes as experiments

[Remote evals and sandboxes](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/remote-evals.md) can now run directly as experiments, not just from a playground. This lets you capture immutable, tracked results from custom agent and non-prompt code, comparable to any other experiment in your project, without going through the playground iteration loop first.

### Workload identity federation for Azure

Connect Azure AI Foundry and Azure OpenAI to Braintrust using Microsoft Entra Workload Identity, which exchanges Braintrust-signed OIDC tokens for Microsoft Entra access tokens instead of storing long-lived Azure credentials in Braintrust. Available for Braintrust-hosted organizations through organization-level AI providers when the Braintrust Gateway is enabled. Project-level Azure providers continue to use API key or Entra API authentication.
