See [Azure AI Foundry](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/azure.md#configure-the-integration) for setup instructions.

### Workload identity federation for OpenAI

Connect OpenAI to Braintrust using workload identity federation, which exchanges Braintrust-signed JWTs for OpenAI access tokens instead of storing long-lived OpenAI API keys in Braintrust. Available for Braintrust-hosted organizations through organization-level AI providers when the Braintrust Gateway is enabled. Project-level OpenAI providers continue to use API key authentication. See [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md#add-openai-as-an-ai-provider) for setup instructions.

### Shingled search optimization

Brainstore can now index multi-word shingles in bloom filters, so phrase and multi-word `search()` queries eliminate more log segments before scanning. Enable **Shingled search optimization** alongside log search optimization in project settings to speed up full-text search on high-volume logs. See [Speed up log filtering](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/projects.md#speed-up-log-filtering) for details.

### Trace Codex CLI sessions

The [trace-codex plugin](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-codex-plugin) (v0.0.2) traces [Codex](https://openai.com/codex) CLI sessions to Braintrust as session, turn, model call, and tool spans. Install the plugin, set `TRACE_TO_BRAINTRUST=true`, and Codex sessions appear in your Braintrust logs. See [Codex](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/codex.md) for setup and CI instructions.

### Startup program

Qualifying early-stage startups can apply for 6–12 months of free Pro plan access. To be eligible, you must be a new Braintrust customer at Series A or earlier and have raised at least \$100K in funding. Visit the [startups page](https://www.braintrust.dev/startups) to apply.

See [Plans and limits](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#plans) for plan details.

### bt CLI releases

* [v0.15.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.15.0) - Added [`bt experiments compare`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/experiments#bt-experiments-compare) to compare summary metrics for up to eight experiments side by side, by name or from a Braintrust comparison URL. Added [`bt view thread`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/view#bt-view-thread) to print a trace's collapsed LLM conversation transcript and [`bt view waterfall`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/view#bt-view-waterfall) (alias `timeline`) to render per-span timing, token, cost, and cache metrics. `bt eval --sample N` now propagates the sample rate into the SDK runtime, so evals that load a Braintrust dataset fetch only the sampled rows. Added `--web` to open datasets, prompts, projects, and experiments in the browser.
* [v0.14.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.14.0) - Fixed a bug where synchronous Python `Eval()` task functions ran sequentially even with `max_concurrency` set, because they were wrapped as async. Sync tasks now run in parallel across threads. Async tasks are unaffected.
* [v0.13.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.13.0) - Added [`bt functions view --version`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/functions#bt-functions-view) to inspect a specific historical version of a function. Improved the error message shown when a login session has expired, prompting you to re-authenticate with `bt auth login`.
* [v0.12.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.12.0) - Added [`bt datasets snapshots`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/datasets#bt-datasets-snapshots) to create, list, restore, and delete dataset snapshots from the CLI, with a preview of the rows affected before restoring. Added [`bt topics btmap`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/topics#bt-topics-btmap) to download the raw topic map artifact for a facet's topic map. Improved JSON parsing error messages to include more context about what failed and where.
* [v0.11.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.11.1) - Fixed the npm package build so the Windows binary is extracted to the correct path, completing npm distribution support across all platforms.
* [v0.11.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.11.0) - `bt` is now available through npm as an optional dependency of the [`braintrust` JavaScript SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/sdks/typescript/versions/latest) (v3.17.0 or later). After installing `braintrust`, run the CLI with `npx bt` or `pnpm exec bt`. See [Install with npm](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/quickstart.md#install).
* [v0.10.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.10.1) - Fixed the global `--json` flag, which previously had no effect, to format command output as JSON. Fixed serialization of typed parameter schemas for remote evals.
* [v0.10.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.10.0) - Added [`bt datasets pipeline`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets/pipelines) for transforming project logs into dataset rows, with one-shot (`run`) or staged (`pull`, `transform`, `push`) execution. Supports TypeScript and Python pipelines declared with `DatasetPipeline(...)`. `bt functions push` (Python) now discovers and pushes parameter definitions alongside functions and prompts. Windows binaries are now code-signed, removing SmartScreen "unknown publisher" warnings. Fixed `ERR_CLOSED_SERVER` errors when running evals via `vite-node`.

### Python SDK releases

* [v0.26.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.26.0) - Added `load_prompt_async()` for non-blocking prompt loading. Added [pydantic-ai 2.0](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md) compatibility. The SDK now honors the [`bt eval --sample N`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval) flag when loading a Braintrust dataset, fetching only the sampled rows instead of the full dataset. **Breaking change**: span and trace IDs now default to the OpenTelemetry-compatible format instead of UUIDs. If you store or parse Braintrust span or trace IDs outside the SDK and rely on the UUID format, set `BRAINTRUST_LEGACY_IDS=true` to keep it. Distributed tracing between Python services on v0.3.0 or later is unaffected, but if you use distributed tracing between Python and TypeScript services, either set `BRAINTRUST_LEGACY_IDS=true` on the Python service or enable compatibility mode on the TypeScript service to keep them interoperable.
* [v0.25.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.25.0) - Added an [AWS Bedrock](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock.md) integration that automatically traces `boto3` Bedrock Runtime `converse` and `invoke_model` calls, including their streaming variants, through `braintrust.auto_instrument()`. Added `project.classifiers.create()` for pushing [classifier](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md#classifiers) functions to Braintrust. The [AgentScope](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/agentscope.md) integration now supports AgentScope 2.x, and the [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md) integration captures inline moderation metadata. Fixed `base_experiment_id` propagation to experiment summaries, double-counting of [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md) prompt cache tokens in [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) traces, and null handling in remote eval prompt parameter defaults.
* [v0.24.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.24.0) - `invoke()` now accepts an `overrides` parameter that deep-merges configuration into the resolved [function](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/functions.md#invoke-functions) data server-side for facet, code, global, and remote eval functions (no effect on prompt functions). The SDK now discovers `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY` from a `.env.braintrust` file, searching upward from the working directory, so you can set credentials per project without exporting shell environment variables. Organization-level [git metadata](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#set-git-metadata-logging) settings are now honored when creating experiments. Fixed [Google GenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md) streaming spans to attach to the correct parent, and stopped the [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md) integration from emitting empty function tool spans.

### TypeScript SDK releases

* [v3.20.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.20.0) - Added `braintrustFlueInstrumentation()` for cleaner [Flue v1 manual instrumentation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/flue.md), so Flue v1 apps can register Braintrust with Flue's `instrument()` API. Flue v0.8.x manual instrumentation continues to use `braintrustFlueObserver`.
* [v3.19.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.19.0) - Added support for tracing [`@anthropic-ai/bedrock-sdk`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic#anthropic-bedrock-typescript), [`@aws-sdk/client-bedrock-runtime`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock#tracing-typescript), and [`@strands-agents/sdk`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/strands-agent#setup-typescript). The SDK now exposes a reporter for the [vitest-evals library](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vitest.md#report-vitest-evals-runs-to-braintrust). Added support for [Flue v1](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/flue.md) via manual instrumentation, removing the previously erroring automatic instrumentation. Fixed Anthropic system-message ordering and dataset-backed eval origin tracking for copied dataset rows.
* [v3.18.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.18.0) - Added support for tracing [AI SDK v7](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/vercel.md#manual-instrumentation-telemetry-registration-typescript) and the [Pi Coding Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pi-coding-agent.md). Expanded support for tracing [Google GenAI Interactions API](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/gemini.md#what-traced-typescript). Explicit `origin` metadata can now be passed into evals. `bt functions push` can upload project-scoped TypeScript [classifiers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md#classifiers). Fixed `BraintrustSpanProcessor` support for OpenTelemetry SDK v1 spans, Flue and Claude Agent SDK span nesting, AI SDK v6 time to first token metrics, and eval summaries for explicit base experiments.
* [v3.17.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.17.0) - The [`bt` CLI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/quickstart) is now available as an optional dependency of the `braintrust` npm package, so JavaScript SDK users can pin `bt` to their SDK version and run it via `pnpm exec bt` without a separate install. Platform binaries are delivered through optional dependencies, with a postinstall download as a fallback when optional dependencies are skipped. Added `BT_BINARY_PATH` to override the resolved binary and `BT_SKIP_DOWNLOAD=1` to skip the postinstall download.
* [v3.16.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.16.0) - Exported `LocalTrace`, the `Trace` object used by [trace-level scorers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/custom-code.md#score-traces), so you can construct one directly. `invoke()` now accepts an `overrides` parameter that deep-merges configuration into the resolved function data server-side for facet, code, global, and remote eval functions (no effect on prompt functions). Fixed `BraintrustStream` decoding of multi-byte UTF-8 characters split across chunk boundaries. The `DatasetPipelineRow` type no longer includes an `output` field.
* [v3.15.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.15.0) - Updated [Flue](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/flue.md) instrumentation to use the `@flue/runtime` 0.8.0 observe hooks, with new span names and metadata keys. Requires `@flue/runtime` 0.8.0 or later. **Breaking change**: removed the `wrapFlueContext` and `wrapFlueSession` exports. Migrate to the observe hooks API. Auto-instrumentation continues to work without changes.

### Go SDK releases

* [v0.9.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.9.1) - Fixed Anthropic message tracing when calls are routed through the Braintrust Gateway or another proxy that adds a path prefix to the `/v1/messages` endpoint.
* [v0.9.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.9.0) - Added `TrialCount` support to the eval runner: set `Opts.TrialCount` to run each eval case [multiple times](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/advanced-evaluations.md#run-trials), override it [per-case](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/advanced-evaluations.md#override-trial-count-per-case) with `Case.TrialCount`, and read the zero-based trial index in task hooks via `TaskHooks.TrialIndex`.
* [v0.8.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.8.0) - Added [classifier support](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md#classifiers) through the `Classifiers` option on the eval runner, with `eval.NewClassifier` and the `eval.Classification` type. The SDK now discovers `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY` from a `.braintrust.json` file, searching upward from the working directory when no key is set through the environment or the `WithAPIKey` option. `WithAPIKey` now ignores blank or whitespace-only values, so environment and file-based fallback remain in effect.
* [v0.7.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.7.2) - Base64-encoded AI attachments (images, PDFs, and other binary data) in span inputs and outputs are now automatically uploaded as separate Braintrust attachment objects, reducing span payload sizes and the chance of exceeding OpenTelemetry span size limits. Supported for OpenAI, Anthropic, Google Gemini, and AWS Bedrock. Enabled by default. Disable with `BRAINTRUST_AUTO_CONVERT_AI_ATTACHMENTS=false` or the `WithAutoConvertAIAttachments(false)` option.

### Java SDK releases

* [v0.3.14](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.14) - Fixed `DatasetCase.metadata()` returning an empty map for dataset rows fetched from Braintrust, such as a hosted dataset used in a [remote eval](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/remote-evals.md). Case-level metadata stored on those rows is now populated as expected.
* [v0.3.13](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.13) - Fixed `BrainstoreTrace.getLLMConversationThread()` returning an empty conversation inside [trace-level scorers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/custom-code.md#score-traces). The method now reconstructs the conversation thread even when the trace's root span has not yet been ingested, which is the case during eval scoring. Also fixed remote evals so eval metadata omits the `parameters` field entirely when an eval defines no parameters.
* [v0.3.12](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.12) - Added [classifier support](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md#classifiers) in `Eval()` through the `classifiers` parameter. The SDK now also discovers `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY` from a `.env.braintrust` file, searching upward from the working directory.

### Ruby SDK releases

* [v0.4.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-ruby/releases/tag/v0.4.0) - Added [classifier support](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md#classifiers) through the `classifiers:` keyword on `Braintrust::Eval.run` and `Braintrust::Eval::Evaluator`, with block-based (`Braintrust::Classifier.new`) and class-based (`include Braintrust::Classifier`) classifiers. The SDK also discovers `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY` from a `.braintrust.json` file, searching upward from the working directory when no key is set through the environment or an explicit `api_key:` argument.

### C# SDK releases

* [v0.2.8](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-dotnet/releases/tag/v0.2.8) - Added [classifier support](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md#classifiers) for categorical evaluation: implement `IClassifier<TInput, TOutput>`, use `FunctionClassifier<TInput, TOutput>` for inline single- or multi-label classifiers, or `ITracedClassifier<TInput, TOutput>` to inspect trace spans, and register them with `.Classifiers(...)` on the eval builder. The OpenAI and Anthropic integrations now tag LLM spans with their span type and capture request parameters such as model, temperature, max tokens, and tools as span metadata. The SDK now discovers `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY` from a `.env.braintrust` file, searching upward from the working directory, and defers API key validation until first use instead of at construction.

### @braintrust/trace-opencode releases

* [v0.0.9](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-opencode-plugin/releases/tag/v0.0.9) - LLM spans now record cache token usage in [OpenCode](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/opencode.md) traces. `prompt_tokens` follows Braintrust's inclusive convention (input + cache read + cache write), and `prompt_cached_tokens` and `prompt_cache_creation_tokens` are surfaced as separate metrics.

### @braintrust/pi-extension releases

* [v0.8.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-pi-extension/releases/tag/pi-extension-v0.8.0) - Root spans are now named after the pi session name (falling back to the repository or directory name) and carry new `pi_version`, `pi_mode`, and `session_name` metadata for richer filtering and search. New **Branch Summary** spans trace branch summarization while navigating a session's history tree, capturing the entries being condensed and the resulting summary. **Compaction** spans now record `compaction_reason`, `will_retry`, `tokens_before`, and `estimated_tokens_after`. **Breaking change**: the compatibility shim for pi versions before 0.65.0 has been removed, so `session_switch` and `session_fork` events are no longer traced on pi versions before 0.65.0. See the [pi integration guide](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) for details.
* [v0.7.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-pi-extension/releases/tag/pi-extension-v0.7.0) - Turn spans now capture input event metadata (`input_source`, `input_streaming_behavior`, `input_image_count`, `raw_input`) when pi fires an input event before a turn starts. Fixed ephemeral session key scoping to include the working directory, preventing session state conflicts when running pi in multiple directories simultaneously.

### Improvements

* User impersonation now requires the impersonated user to belong to at least one organization. See [Impersonate users](https://docs.braintrust.dev/api-reference.md#impersonate-users) for the updated requirements.

* Conditional human review scores now support **Trace** and **Subspan** condition scopes in addition to span-level conditions, so a score can show when any span in the trace, or any child span, matches its expression. See [Show scores conditionally](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md#show-scores-conditionally) for details.

* Gateway requests can now set `x-bt-project-name` to specify a project by name instead of ID. The Gateway resolves the project automatically and logs the request to that project's logs without requiring `x-bt-parent`. See [Use project-level AI providers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/gateway.md#use-project-level-ai-providers) for details.

* Timeline view now properly renders post-hoc scoring spans (such as AutoEval results) inline. By default, scorer spans are hidden to focus on the root execution timeline. Select  and toggle **Include score spans in timeline** to view scoring duration alongside your trace execution. See [View as a timeline](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#view-as-a-timeline) for details.

* Large text and object diffs can now be displayed in full by clicking **Show more** when comparing prompt versions, improving visibility for long prompts and complex data structures.

* When Loop is enabled, the SQL filter editor for [log alerts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/alerts.md#create-a-log-alert) now supports  **Generate**, so you can write the filter from a natural-language description instead of by hand.

* Added shareable links to specific saved [custom views](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/custom-views.md). Selecting a saved trace or dataset view now records it in the URL, so copying the link opens that exact view.

* The [custom view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/custom-views.md) empty state now includes a **Create default view** button that loads a pre-built template rendering your trace or dataset fields as formatted JSON, so you can start with a working view without writing a Loop prompt.

* The [\*\* Permission groups\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/groups) dialog now lets you set organization-level, all-projects, and project-specific permissions inline while creating a group, and you can create a new permission group directly from the member assignment picker. See [Access control](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control.md) for details.

* Time series charts on the [Monitor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards.md) page now support a **Target time interval** option (Auto, Week, Day, or Hour) for explicit control over time bucketing, with smoother automatic bucket sizes and query ranges aligned to full intervals.

* Git diff content is now opt-in. Braintrust no longer logs the **Diff** field of git metadata unless your organization explicitly enables it at [\*\* Settings\*\* > \*\* Logging\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/logging). Other git metadata fields are still collected by default. See [Set git metadata logging](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#set-git-metadata-logging) for details.

* Enabling [Topics](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics.md) now requires organization owner permissions. Members without owner access see a prompt in the [Enable Topics](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/enable.md) dialog to contact an org owner.

* You can now tune topic clustering settings, including sample size, hierarchy threshold, reconciliation, clustering algorithm, and dimension reduction, directly from the Topics page in addition to the [`bt` CLI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/topics). See [Tune clustering settings](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/manage.md#tune-clustering-settings) for details.

* Trace-level scorers and prompts now support a [`{{thread_with_system}}`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/llm-as-a-judge#score-traces) reserved variable that renders the full conversation including system messages. Scorer `{{thread}}` continues to omit system messages so judge rubrics aren't polluted by your application's system prompt.

* When creating an organization, the data plane region is now pre-selected based on your location: EU for users in the EU, EEA, UK, and Switzerland; US otherwise. You can still change it before creating the organization. See [Create an organization](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#create-an-organization) for details.

* Log alerts with a webhook action now require a successful test before saving, so a stale test result can't authorize a changed webhook target. See [Set up alerts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/alerts.md).

* Automation webhook URLs are now validated when you save an alert. Malformed and non-http(s) URLs are rejected, and delivery to private or internal network addresses is blocked. See [Set up alerts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/alerts.md).

* [Spend alerts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/monitor-usage.md#set-up-spend-alerts) now support Slack channel notifications in addition to email. Select **Billing email**, **Slack channel**, or both when configuring your alert thresholds.

* When you enter a US billing address while upgrading to Pro, Braintrust now verifies it against address records to ensure accurate tax calculation.

* Webhook alert URLs must now use `http` or `https`, and Braintrust blocks delivery to private, internal, or otherwise reserved network addresses to protect against server-side request forgery (SSRF). See [Webhook payloads](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/alerts.md#webhook-payloads) for details.

* MCP server URLs must now use `http` or `https` to protect against server-side request forgery (SSRF). See [Add MCP servers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/prompts/create.md#add-mcp-servers) for details.

* Bedrock custom models can now use the **OpenAI** format, routing through Bedrock's OpenAI-compatible Chat Completions endpoint, including streaming. See [AWS Bedrock](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock.md#connect-bedrock-to-braintrust) for details.

* Bedrock custom model format auto-detection now correctly handles geo inference profile IDs such as `eu.anthropic.claude-sonnet-4-6`: Anthropic profiles use the **Anthropic** format and all other providers use **Converse**, preventing routing errors when configuring geo-prefixed models in Loop and the playground. The Loop model list now shows geo qualifiers — **Bedrock (EU)**, **Bedrock (US)**, **Bedrock (Global)** — so you can distinguish geo profiles of the same model. See [AWS Bedrock](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock.md#connect-bedrock-to-braintrust) for details.

* Human review score cards now show a per-score review count with a breakdown of which reviewers match the base value and which diverge, and the review-span dropdown includes a **Base span** option to jump back to the parent span's scores. See [Review with multiple reviewers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review/multiple-reviewers.md) for details.

* The [Gateway](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/gateway.md) now supports Google Gemini's native `embedContent` and `batchEmbedContents` endpoints, so you can route Gemini embedding requests, including multimodal text and image content, through the Gateway with logging and billing. See [Generate embeddings](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/gateway.md#generate-embeddings) for details.

* The [\*\* Experiments\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/experiments) list now shows an **Estimated cost** column alongside scores and token metrics, making it easier to compare total cost across experiment runs at a glance.

* Custom columns in [\*\* Logs\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/logs) now accept any valid non-aggregate SQL expression. In addition to simple field references, you can use functions (`concat`, `coalesce`, `lower`), `CASE` expressions, arithmetic operators, and string patterns (`LIKE`, `ILIKE`). See [Create custom columns](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/view-logs.md#create-custom-columns) for details.

* The `len()` SQL function now returns `1` for scalar string, number, and boolean values instead of `null`. This preserves filter and sort behavior when row materialization collapses a single-element array into a scalar. See [SQL functions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#sql-functions) for details.

* &#x20;**Loop** and [\*\* Playgrounds\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/playgrounds) now display actionable error messages when AI provider requests fail, with inline recovery actions such as editing your [\*\* Models\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/models/organization) settings for invalid API key errors, starting a new session for context limit errors in Loop, or retrying for network errors.

* [\*\* Topics\*\*](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics.md) is now accessible from the command palette (⌘K / Ctrl+K) for projects with Topics enabled. Settings shortcuts such as API keys and AI providers now appear above the project list in the command palette switcher.

* The **Thread** () layout now includes an  options menu with a **Refresh** action to reload thread data. See [View as a conversation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#view-as-a-conversation) for details.

* Trace action buttons are now available when expanding a trace from the [\*\* Review\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/review) page, matching the actions available in logs and experiments. See [Score traces and datasets](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review/score-traces.md#start-reviewing) for details.

* [Monitor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/monitor.md) total-cost charts now use the [`estimated_cost()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions#sql-functions) SQL function, which accounts for cache token pricing and fixes the negative cost values that could appear for some providers. Split-by-token-type cost views are unaffected.

* The **Basic** filter on the [\*\* Logs\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/logs) and [\*\* Monitor\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/monitor) pages now includes a dedicated **Model** option that auto-detects the metadata field storing the model name (including OpenTelemetry's `gen_ai.request.model`) and lists available models with their trace counts, per-model cost, latency, and token usage.

* Free-form human review scores can now write reviewer input to the `expected` field instead of `metadata`, configurable per score. See [Configure review scores](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/human-review.md#configure-review-scores) for details.

* Disabling built-in models now automatically pauses your topic automations instead of letting them fail silently. Re-enabling built-in models does not automatically resume them. Resume each automation manually from the [Topics](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/manage.md#pause-resume-automation) page.

* The Thread view preprocessor picker can now generate a custom preprocessor with a coding agent, which inspects your real trace data and writes the preprocessor for you. See [Write a custom preprocessor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/enable.md#write-a-custom-preprocessor) for details.

* OpenTelemetry ingestion for [Pydantic AI v2](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/pydantic-ai.md) now correctly classifies `invoke_agent` wrapper spans as task spans instead of LLM spans, fixing inflated LLM call counts. Tool definitions, multimodal image inputs (`uri` and `blob` parts), and detailed token metrics (reasoning, cache, and audio) are also parsed from Pydantic AI v2 span attributes.

* Scorers, prompts, tools, and workflows from other projects now appear in playground and automation dropdowns for users with project-level read access, without requiring organization-wide read. This supports a shared scorer library, where you grant read access to a central scorer project. See [Use scorers from another project](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/score-online.md#use-scorers-from-another-project) for details.

* Creating, editing, and renaming [Monitor dashboards](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/create.md#create-a-dashboard) now requires only the project-level **Projects > Update** permission, not organization-level permission. Setting an organization-wide default view still requires the organization-level **Update** permission.

**May 2026**

### Topics pricing

[Topics](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics.md) now uses monthly included credits across all plans: \$10/month on Starter and \$249/month on Pro as a launch promotion for the first 3 months, reverting to \$100/month after. Overage charges apply once credits are used up, at \$0.06/MTok input and \$0.40/MTok output, uniform across plans. Credits do not roll over month-to-month. [Custom facets](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/custom-facets.md) are now available on all plans. See [Model credits](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#model-credits) and [Billing FAQ](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/billing/faq.md) for details.

### Cached tokens view for traces

The  **Timeline** trace view adds a cached tokens view that scales LLM span bars by cached read tokens and breaks down input token usage by uncached input, cached read, and cache write, so you can assess prompt caching effectiveness across a trace at a glance. See [View as a timeline](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#view-as-a-timeline) for details.

### Topics for sessions and multi-turn conversations

Topics automations can now classify groups of related traces, like sessions or multi-turn conversations, as a single unit instead of each trace independently. Pick a grouping key like `metadata.session_id`, set the interval and trace cap that defines each group, and choose whether the classification is written on the chronologically first trace in the group or on every trace with prior traces as context. See [Group traces into conversations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/manage.md#group-traces-into-conversations) for details.

### Workload identity federation for Anthropic

Connect Anthropic to Braintrust using workload identity federation, which exchanges Braintrust-signed JWTs for Anthropic access tokens instead of storing long-lived Anthropic API keys in Braintrust. Available for Braintrust-hosted organizations through organization-level AI providers. Project-level Anthropic providers continue to use API key authentication. See [Add Anthropic as an AI provider](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md#add-anthropic-as-an-ai-provider) for setup instructions.

### Workload identity federation for Vertex AI

Connect Google Vertex AI to Braintrust using workload identity federation, which exchanges Braintrust-signed OIDC tokens for Google access tokens instead of storing long-lived Google credentials in Braintrust. Available for Braintrust-hosted organizations through organization-level AI providers when the Braintrust Gateway is enabled. Project-level Vertex AI providers continue to use access token or service account key authentication. See [Google Vertex AI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/google.md#configure-the-integration) for setup instructions.

### Query patterns in the Infra dashboard

For self-hosted deployments, the \*\* Infra dashboard\*\* now includes **UI queries** and **API queries** tables that group slow query shapes by object type, filter fields, source, and predicate types like `ILIKE`, `match()`, and inequalities. Each row shows count and p50/p95/p99 latency, helping you identify which query shapes are driving latency and where to focus indexing or query refactors. See [Infra dashboard](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/self-hosting#infra-dashboard) for details.

### Project-scoped default views

Set a default view at the project level so everyone viewing a project lands on the same starting view, without affecting other projects or requiring organization-level permissions. Project admins (project-level **Update** permission) can now set defaults on Logs, Experiments, Datasets, Review, and Monitor pages, sitting between personal and organization defaults in the lookup order. See [Set default table views](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/view-logs.md#set-default-table-views) for details.

### Dataset filters for experiments

Scope an [experiment run](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/run-in-ui.md#create-from-scratch) to a subset of a dataset instead of every record. Filters from a [filtered dataset view](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets/manage.md#filter-records) carry over when you create an experiment, so the run targets the same records.

### Dataset versioning in Loop

[Loop](https://docs.braintrust.dev/loop/capabilities.md#curate-datasets) can now save, list, and restore dataset snapshots and tag versions with environments. Checkpoint a dataset before a batch of edits, roll back to a saved version, or promote a snapshot to `production` without leaving the chat. Available when Loop is opened from a dataset page.

### Comparison grade in experiment comparisons

Experiment comparisons now label each experiment as improvement, regression, tradeoff, or tie relative to the base experiment, so you can tell at a glance whether a run is a clear win, a clear loss, or a mix of gains and tradeoffs. The grade appears as a row in \*\* Summary table\*\* layout and in column headers in \*\* Grid\*\* layout, including playground task columns. See [Compare experiments](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/compare-experiments.md#assess-overall-impact) for details.

### Assume role authentication for Amazon Bedrock

Connect Amazon Bedrock to Braintrust using AWS STS `AssumeRole` with an IAM role in your AWS account, instead of storing long-lived AWS access keys in Braintrust. Available for Braintrust-hosted organizations. See [Connect Bedrock to Braintrust](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock.md#connect-bedrock-to-braintrust) for setup instructions.

### Self-hosted data plane v2.1.1

Data plane v2.1.1 is now the recommended target for self-hosted v2.x upgrades. Use Terraform AWS module v5.2.1 for AWS deployments, or Helm chart 6.2.1 for GCP and Azure deployments. Helm chart 6.2.1 disables code function execution by default. See [Upgrade to data plane v2.x](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/self-hosting/upgrade/v2) and [Self-hosting releases](https://docs.braintrust.dev/data-plane-changelog) for details.

### Secret previews and rotation tracking

AI provider and env variable settings show a redacted preview of each saved value (for example, `abc...xyz`) and a **Last updated** timestamp that tracks when the secret value itself was last changed, along with the user who made the change. The same fields are also returned by the public API for `/v1/env_var` and `/v1/ai_secret`. See [Configure AI providers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md) and [Set environment variables](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#set-environment-variables) for details.

### API key and service token creation restricted to the UI

> [!WARNING]
>
> **Update (June 2026):** Service token creation through the API has since been re-enabled for organization owners. See [Programmatic service token creation](#programmatic-service-token-creation).
>
> **Breaking change**: API keys and service tokens can now be created only from the Braintrust UI. The REST API no longer supports creating either, and the `POST /v1/api_key`, `POST /v1/service_token`, and `PUT /v1/service_token` endpoints have been removed from the public API. The [`PATCH /v1/organization/members`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/api-reference/organizations/modify-organization-membership) endpoint can still create a service account, but its service token must be created by an organization owner via the Braintrust UI. See [Manage API keys](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#manage-api-keys) and [Create service tokens](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#create-service-tokens) for the supported flows.

### Auto-instrumentation for Java

Braintrust now supports [auto-instrumentation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/trace-llm-calls.md#java) for Java, enabling zero-code tracing via the `braintrust-java-agent` JAR. Attach it at JVM startup to automatically trace OpenAI, Anthropic, Spring AI, LangChain4j, and Google GenAI calls. The agent also supports running alongside the Datadog Java agent.

### SQL `IF` and `COUNT_IF` for conditional expressions

SQL queries now support `IF(condition, then_value, else_value)` as a shorter alternative to `CASE WHEN ... THEN ... ELSE ... END` for two-branch conditions, and `COUNT_IF(condition)` as a shorter alternative to `count(CASE WHEN condition THEN 1 ELSE NULL END)` for counting rows that match a predicate. See [Conditional expressions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#conditional-expressions) for syntax and examples.

### SQL `TABLESAMPLE` for random sampling

SQL queries now support `TABLESAMPLE` (with the shorter `SAMPLE` alias) to randomly sample rows from a table without scanning every row. Use `TABLESAMPLE n PERCENT` to sample a percentage of rows or `TABLESAMPLE n ROWS` to sample a fixed count. An optional `SEED` clause makes results deterministic across runs. See [SAMPLE](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#sample) for syntax and examples.

### SQL `ROLLUP` and `GROUPING SETS` for multi-level aggregation

SQL queries now support `ROLLUP` and `GROUPING SETS` for multi-level aggregation in a single query. `ROLLUP` produces subtotals and a grand total alongside individual groups, while `GROUPING SETS` lets you specify exactly which grouping combinations to compute. The `GROUPING()` function labels which dimensions are rolled up in each row, so you can distinguish detail rows from subtotals. See [ROLLUP and GROUPING SETS](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#rollup-and-grouping-sets) for details.

### SQL `SETTINGS` clause for per-query options

SQL queries now support a trailing `SETTINGS` clause for passing per-query hints to the Brainstore execution engine. The initial options are `max_bloom_terms`, which controls when bloom-filter segment elimination is skipped for filters with many disjunctive terms, and `preview_length`, which truncates preview fields (`input`, `output`, `expected`, `error`, `metadata`) to a chosen character length. See [SETTINGS](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#settings) for syntax and the full list of options.

### bt CLI releases

* [v0.9.2](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.9.2) - [`bt setup`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/setup) now opens a browser to sign in or sign up and then prompts in the terminal to pick an organization and project, replacing the previous API-key paste flow. When multiple [auth profiles](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/overview.md#troubleshooting) are saved and no profile is selected by flag, env var, or org match, `bt` now prompts you to pick one interactively instead of failing with an ambiguous-profile error.
* [v0.9.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.9.1) - `bt` now warns once daily when any [AI provider](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md) key is older than six months. `bt view trace` text output now suggests `--json` for fetching the full trace payload in one command. `bt sync push` reads input data more efficiently via byte-based buffering. Fixed `--verbose` defaulting to true for `bt sync pull`. Fixed `bt datasets view` row fetching.
* [v0.9.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.9.0) - Added `bt datasets` for creating, listing, viewing, updating (aliases: `add`, `refresh`), and deleting remote datasets from the CLI. Supports file, stdin, and inline JSON input. Fixed `--limit` being ignored when paginating `bt view` results. Fixed unicode handling in `bt sync pull`. `bt setup` now combines project creation and selection into a single interactive step.
* [v0.8.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.8.0) - Added `--matrix-param` to `bt eval` to specify multiple values for one or more parameters and run one experiment per combination. Added `bt topics config delete` to permanently remove a Topics automation. Added Copilot and Qwen support to `bt setup`, with skills installed at `.copilot/skills/braintrust/SKILL.md` and `.qwen/skills/braintrust/SKILL.md` respectively. Copilot MCP is configured via `copilot mcp add`. `bt setup` default mode is now ephemeral. Pass `--skills` to install agent skills or `--mcp` to configure MCP. Agent skills are no longer installed automatically by `bt setup`. Run `bt setup skills` to opt in. Added a post-success verification prompt to `bt setup`.

### Python SDK releases

* [v0.23.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.23.0) - New [Hugging Face](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/huggingface.md) Hub integration that traces `InferenceClient` calls (chat completion, text generation, feature extraction, and sentence similarity). [Instructor](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/instructor.md) structured-output calls are now traced as `task` spans. **Breaking change**: when your org hasn't configured a \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Logging\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/logging) policy, `Eval()` and `init()` no longer log a default set of [git metadata](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#set-git-metadata-logging) fields. Configure a policy in the UI, or pass a `git_metadata_settings` object specifying the fields to log.
* [v0.22.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.22.1) - You can now pass [Pydantic](https://docs.pydantic.dev/) models (or any object that serializes to a dict) directly as `metadata` when logging, instead of converting to a dict first. [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) spans now break out Anthropic prompt cache creation tokens by TTL (`prompt_cache_creation_5m_tokens`, `prompt_cache_creation_1h_tokens`), so you can attribute prompt-cache spend to each pricing tier. Failed [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md) runs now surface their error on the trace instead of being silently dropped, so you can see why a run failed without leaving Braintrust.
* [v0.22.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.22.0) - [LiteLLM](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/litellm.md) async moderation calls (`amoderation()`) are now traced, like the existing sync `moderation()`. Fixed inflated token counts in [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) traces for OpenAI-style providers using prompt caching, where cached input tokens were double-counted. [Custom scorers](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md) that return `list[Score]` are no longer rejected by static type checkers. [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md) traces now capture end-of-utterance detection delay, so you can measure voice-turn responsiveness.
* [v0.21.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.21.0) - Added [LiveKit Agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/livekit-agents.md) integration for tracing real-time voice AI pipelines, including LLM turns, STT, TTS, audio output, and function tool calls.
* [v0.20.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.20.0) - [AutoGen](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/autogen.md) agents running inside teams (such as `RoundRobinGroupChat`) are now traced individually as child spans of the team. [LiteLLM](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/litellm.md) `text_completion()` and `atext_completion()` calls are now traced as `Completion` spans. Default [git metadata](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#set-git-metadata-logging) collection for `Eval()`, `EvalAsync()`, and `init()` now uses a fixed 8-field set (`commit`, `branch`, `tag`, `dirty`, `author_name`, `author_email`, `commit_message`, `commit_time`) and excludes `git_diff`. **Breaking change**: orgs that relied on automatic diff collection must enable `git_diff` under [\*\* Settings\*\* > \*\* Logging\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/logging). Fixed [Autoevals](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/autoevals.md) scorers type error issue. [Cohere](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/cohere.md) v2 streaming responses now include citations in span output, and `chat.stream()` closes its span correctly when used as a context manager. [Mistral](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/mistral.md) `beta.conversations` tool calls are now traced. The background logger no longer retries on HTTP 413. The [Strands](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/strands-agent.md) integration correctly closes spans when running alongside a no-op OpenTelemetry provider. Anthropic cache tokens captured via [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) are now folded into `prompt_tokens` and `total_tokens`. Span metadata for [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md) calls no longer includes entries for parameters you didn't pass.
* [v0.19.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-python/releases/tag/py-sdk-v0.19.0) - Auto-instrumentation now covers [Temporal](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/temporal.md); call `braintrust.auto_instrument()` to trace workflows and activities. The integration moved to `braintrust.integrations.temporal`, and the old `braintrust.contrib.temporal` import is deprecated. [OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openai.md) streaming audio transcriptions are now traced. [Cohere](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/cohere.md) and [Mistral](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/mistral.md) tool calls now generate child tool spans. Fixed a [remote evals](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/remote-evals.md) bug where the Python SDK's `braintrust.devserver` module routed experiments to the wrong project when an `/eval` request omitted `project_id`. **Breaking change**: [Anthropic](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md#python) per-TTL prompt cache write counts moved from span metadata to span metrics and were renamed: `cache_creation_ephemeral_5m_input_tokens` → `prompt_cache_creation_5m_tokens` and `cache_creation_ephemeral_1h_input_tokens` → `prompt_cache_creation_1h_tokens`. Update any references to the old names (for example, in SQL queries, saved filters, alerts, automations, or dashboards).

### TypeScript SDK releases

* [v3.14.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.14.0) - Added auto-instrumentation for [Mastra](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/mastra.md), tracing agents, workflows, steps, tool calls, and LLM calls with no code changes. Added the `BRAINTRUST_CACHE_LOCATION` [environment variable](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/advanced-tracing.md#tune-performance) to select the prompt and parameter cache mode (`mixed`, `memory`, `disk`, or `none`).
* [v3.13.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.13.0) - The Node.js SDK now searches parent directories for a `.env.braintrust` file containing `BRAINTRUST_API_KEY` at login. Added the experimental [`DatasetPipeline`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets/pipelines) API for transforming project logs into dataset rows.
* [v3.12.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.12.0) - Added auto-instrumentation for the [OpenAI Agents SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/openai-agents-sdk.md) (`@openai/agents`), [LangChain](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/langchain.md) and [LangGraph](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/langgraph.md), and Flue (`@flue/runtime`). Added `wrapNextjsConfigWithBraintrust`, imported from `braintrust/next`, as the canonical Next.js setup helper, which selects the webpack plugin or Turbopack loader based on the active build. Renamed the bundler plugin exports to `braintrustVitePlugin`, `braintrustWebpackPlugin`, `braintrustEsbuildPlugin`, and `braintrustRollupPlugin` (the previous names are deprecated). Added the `braintrust/apply-auto-instrumentation` entrypoint so CommonJS and TypeScript-to-CommonJS projects can enable auto-instrumentation without the `--import` ESM hook. Traces can now span multiple projects. **Behavior change**: git metadata is no longer collected by default when your org has no [git metadata logging](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#set-git-metadata-logging) policy. Hardened `mergeDicts` against prototype pollution and added exponential backoff to request retries.
* [v3.11.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.11.0) - Added auto-instrumentation for `@github/copilot-sdk`, `@openai/codex-sdk`, and [Firebase Genkit](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/firebase-genkit.md). Instrumented [Mistral](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/mistral.md) classification and moderation APIs, and added `projectId` support to `wrapVitest`. Captured reasoning content for [OpenRouter](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/openrouter.md) and [Groq](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/groq.md) reasoning models. Fixed duplicate LLM spans when multiple SDK instances load in one process, and corrected Google ADK agent naming.
* [v3.10.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-javascript/releases/tag/braintrust@3.10.0) - Added auto-instrumentation for [Groq](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/groq.md) (`groq-sdk`) and Cursor (`@cursor/sdk`). Added dataset versioning support so experiments can pin a [dataset](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets.md) to a specific version, and experiment metadata now records the dataset filters used for a run. [Cohere](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/cohere.md) now captures extended thinking content and a `reasoning_tokens` metric. Expanded [Google ADK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/google.md) auto-instrumentation to cover `@google/adk@1.0.0`. Fixed [Mistral](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/mistral.md) reasoning capture, [Hugging Face](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/huggingface.md) streamed tool calls, [Claude Agent SDK](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/agent-frameworks/claude-agent-sdk.md) built-in tool nesting, and bundler plugin export maps.

### Go SDK releases

* [v0.7.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-go/releases/tag/v0.7.0) - Upgraded the OpenTelemetry modules, gzipped OpenTelemetry span exports, and expanded OpenAI metadata capture to include request status, token usage, and details for incomplete or failed responses. **Breaking change**: requires Go 1.25.0 or later.

### Java SDK releases

* [v0.3.10](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.10) - Automatic base64 attachment conversion (introduced in v0.3.9) now recognizes the Anthropic, Google Gemini, and AWS Bedrock Converse message formats in addition to OpenAI-style data URIs, so multimodal traces from those providers render as previewable [attachments](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/attachments.md).
* [v0.3.9](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.9) - OpenTelemetry span and log payloads are now gzip-compressed before export by default, reducing network bandwidth with no caller-side changes. Braintrust span size limits still apply to the uncompressed payload. Disable span compression with `BRAINTRUST_COMPRESS_OTEL_PAYLOAD=false` or `BraintrustConfig.builder().compressOtelPayload(false)`. Base64-encoded AI attachments (images, PDFs, and other binary data) in span inputs and outputs are now also automatically uploaded as separate Braintrust attachment objects, reducing span payload sizes and the chance of exceeding OpenTelemetry span size limits. Enabled by default. Disable with `BRAINTRUST_AUTO_CONVERT_AI_ATTACHMENTS=false` or `.autoConvertAIAttachments(false)`.
* [v0.3.8](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.8) - Added [trace-level scoring](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/custom-code.md#score-traces): implement `TracedScorer<INPUT, OUTPUT>` instead of `Scorer` to score on intermediate LLM calls, tool invocations, and other spans produced during a task. The `Eval` framework dispatches to `score(TaskResult, BrainstoreTrace)`, where `BrainstoreTrace` gives cached access to task spans via `getSpans()` and `getSpans(type)` and a reconstructed conversation via `getLLMConversationThread()`. Existing `Scorer` implementations are unaffected. Opening a cursor on an empty Braintrust-hosted dataset no longer throws.
* [v0.3.7](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.7) - [Auto-instrumentation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/trace-llm-calls.md#java) now requires `com.openai:openai-java` 2.15.0 or later. Streaming calls to the OpenAI Responses API (`client.responses().createStreaming(...)`) now capture `output`, token usage, and `time_to_first_token`.
* [v0.3.6](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.6) - Internal publishing fix. No user-facing changes.
* [v0.3.5](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-java/releases/tag/v0.3.5) - [Anthropic instrumentation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/anthropic.md#java) now captures per-TTL prompt cache metrics (`prompt_cache_creation_5m_tokens`, `prompt_cache_creation_1h_tokens`) in place of the aggregate `prompt_cache_creation_tokens`, and tracks cache reads as `prompt_cached_tokens`. `BraintrustConfig.sslContext()` is now applied to the API HTTP client, fixing API calls in custom-CA environments. `BraintrustApiClient` is deprecated in favor of `Braintrust.openApiClient()` and `BraintrustOpenApiClient` (generated from the OpenAPI spec).

### C# SDK releases

* [v0.2.7](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-sdk-dotnet/releases/tag/v0.2.7) - Added [Azure OpenAI](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/azure.md) instrumentation in a new `Braintrust.Sdk.AzureOpenAI` package, and upgraded the OpenTelemetry dependencies.

### Improvements

* [AI provider](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/ai-providers.md) keys that have not been rotated in over six months now display a warning indicator on the secrets manager page, surfacing the recommendation to rotate secrets periodically.

* New [SQL best practices](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/best-practices.md) page covering query shapes, `ANY_SPAN()` filtering, subfield indexing, and performance warnings.

* Score filters on `summary` shape queries now evaluate at the span level for better performance. This is correct for the common case where scorers run once per span. To filter on the averaged score across all spans in a trace, use `HAVING avg(scores.foo)`. See [Data shapes](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#data-shapes) for details.

* The [Baseten](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/baseten.md) provider now accepts an optional **API base URL** so you can point Braintrust at a custom Baseten deployment endpoint, such as a dedicated deployment for a Gemma model.

* New models available through the Braintrust Gateway: GPT-5.5 Pro (`gpt-5.5-pro`) via OpenAI and Azure, and Claude 4.7 Opus (`claude-opus-4-7`) via Anthropic. See [Supported models](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/supported-models.md) for the full list.

* SQL queries now support `substring(text, start, length)` for extracting substrings. Positions are 1-based, and a start value of 0 or less is clipped to the first character. See [SQL functions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#sql-functions) for the full function reference.

* `date_trunc` now accepts positive-integer multiples of `second(s)`, `minute(s)`, `hour(s)`, and `day(s)` (for example, `'15 minutes'` or `'2 hours'`) for finer-grained time bucketing in `SELECT` and `GROUP BY`. The existing single-unit intervals continue to work, including `'week'`, `'month'`, and `'year'`, which don't accept multiples because they have variable durations. See [SQL functions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#sql-functions) for details.

* SQL queries now support `estimated_cost_breakdown()` and `estimated_cost_component(name)` for per-component cost analysis. Use these to separate prompt, cached prompt, cache creation, and completion costs. See [SQL functions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#sql-functions) for available component names.

* Topics automations now let you select which Braintrust-served `brain-facet-*` model generates facet summaries, so you can pin a specific version or stay on `brain-facet-latest`. Configure under **Advanced > Facet model** in [automation settings](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/manage.md#adjust-automation).

* You can now [pause and resume the Topics automation](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/topics/manage.md#pause-resume-automation) to stop and restart log processing without losing your place. When resumed, the automation picks up from where it left off rather than reprocessing.

* Tightened validation for [custom query export automations](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/data-management/export.md#custom-sql-query): the `project_logs(...)` source must take a single string-literal project ID matching the automation's own project. Cross-project queries are now rejected.

* The [SQL sandbox](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql.md) editor now autocompletes column names inferred from the `FROM` source (including nested fields), suggests the most common observed values when typing a filter comparison like `WHERE metadata.env =`, and completes `date_trunc` intervals and the `SETTINGS` clause.

* The trace viewer now honors an explicit `metrics.estimated_cost` logged on any span, including parent spans. A parent's displayed cost is the sum of child span costs plus the parent's own logged cost, bringing the trace UI into alignment with the [`estimated_cost()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions#sql-functions) SQL function. Previously, an explicit `estimated_cost` was only preserved on leaf spans.

* When [testing a scorer](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/write-scorers.md#test-with-logs) with **Logs** as the source, the  **Run** section now shows the full trace (tree and span detail) for each matching root span instead of a single JSON row, so you can drill into spans before running the scorer.

* The [\*\* Billing\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/billing) page now shows a projected upcoming invoice broken down by Logs and Scores, so you can see on-demand usage spend in context before the invoice cycle closes.

* Inline base64 payloads in [AWS Bedrock](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/ai-providers/bedrock.md) Converse `image`, `video`, `audio`, and `document` content blocks are now automatically extracted into [Braintrust attachments](https://docs.braintrust.dev/instrument/attachments.md) at ingest, so multimodal Converse traces render as previewable attachments without any SDK changes.

* The [Braintrust Gateway](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/gateway.md) now returns an `x-bt-error-origin` response header on error responses, with a value of `braintrust` for Gateway errors or the provider name (for example, `openai`) for upstream provider errors, so callers can distinguish Gateway failures from provider failures.

* Timestamp columns (`created`, `start`, `end`, and any timestamp field) in tables can now be switched between absolute and friendly (relative) formats. Open the column header menu and select **Show friendly dates** or **Show absolute dates**. The choice is saved per column and per page.

* Organization owners can now turn off automatic direct ownership grants for newly created objects with the **Create direct ownership grants** toggle in \*\* Settings\*\* > [\*\* Permission groups\*\*](https://www.braintrust.dev/app/~/configuration/org/groups), and optionally clear existing direct owner grants. See [Access control](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/access-control.md#how-permissions-work) for details.

**April 2026**

### Translate message content in traces

You can now translate any message or string field in a trace without leaving the UI. This is useful when debugging multilingual agents or reviewing customer conversations in unfamiliar languages. Choose from English, Spanish, French, German, Japanese, and Chinese, or type any language name. See [Translate message content](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#translate-message-content) for details.

### Custom views for dataset rows

Custom views, which let you build tailored interfaces for traces using Loop, are now also available for dataset rows. Use them to create annotation interfaces, side-by-side input and expected comparisons, or any visualization that helps your team review and label records more effectively. See [Create custom views](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/custom-views.md) for details.

### Dataset snapshots

Save named checkpoints of a dataset to mark stable states, compare before-and-after changes, and restore to a previous version if needed. See [Save and restore snapshots](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets/manage.md#save-and-restore-snapshots) for details.

> [!NOTE]
>
> Snapshots are only available on [Pro and Enterprise plans](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md#plans).

### Dataset environments

Datasets can now be assigned to [environments](https://docs.braintrust.dev/deploy/environments.md), alongside prompts and parameters. Pin a specific dataset version to production to ensure evals always run against a known-good baseline, and promote dataset versions through dev, staging, and production the same way you promote prompts. [Environment alerts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/alerts.md#create-an-environment-alert) fire for dataset environment changes as well, and webhook payloads now include an `object_type` field (`"prompt"` or `"dataset"`) so handlers can branch on the type of change.

### Monitor dashboard import and export

> [!NOTE]
>
> The Monitor page was renamed [**Dashboards**](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards.md) in August 2026.

Monitor dashboards can now be exported and imported across projects, making it easy to reuse dashboards without recreating them from scratch. Charts also support exporting their configuration or underlying data as CSV or JSON. See [Duplicate dashboards across projects](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/create.md#copy-a-dashboard-to-another-project) and [Manage custom charts](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/dashboards/build-charts.md#move-and-copy-charts) for details.

### Cloud storage export

Cloud storage export now supports start-from and rewind controls and a Hive-partitioned file layout. See [Export to cloud storage](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/data-management/export.md) for details.

### Tag filtering for experiment runs

Tags you apply to experiments are now available as a filter in the dataset **Runs** panel, making it easy to compare runs across a specific model version, prompt variant, or release candidate. See [Filter experiment runs](https://docs.braintrust.dev/annotate/datasets/track-performance.md#filter-experiment-runs) for details.

### Token distribution overview for traces

The  **Timeline** trace view now includes a token distribution overview above the timeline bars. It breaks down LLM span token usage by type — uncached input, cached read, cache write, and output — and shows cache hit rate per span, making it easy to spot where caching is and isn't working. The overview adjusts to the current **Scale by** selection. See [View as a timeline](https://docs.braintrust.dev/observe/examine-traces.md#view-as-a-timeline) for details.

### Summary table layout

The experiments table now includes a  **Summary table** layout that shows scores and metrics as rows with experiments as columns, making it easy to compare multiple experiments at a glance. Export results as a PDF to share with stakeholders who don't have Braintrust access. See [Compare experiments](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/compare-experiments.md#assess-overall-impact) for details.

### Infra dashboard for self-hosted deployments

Organization owners and admins can now view infrastructure metrics for their self-hosted deployment directly in the Braintrust UI. The infra dashboard shows processing throughput, CPU and memory usage, object storage latency, realtime lag, and status checks. See [Monitor your infrastructure](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/self-hosting#monitoring) for details.

### Log indexing and full-text search

You can now enable log search optimization to accelerate full-text queries across all fields, or add subfield indexes for fields you filter on frequently — such as `metadata.user_id` or `input.query`. Use the new [`search()`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure#full-text-search) SQL function to query all text fields in a single expression with automatic bloom filter acceleration when log search optimization is on. Braintrust suggests candidate fields based on your data and can backfill up to 3 days of history.

See [Speed up log filtering](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/projects.md#speed-up-log-filtering) and [Full-text search](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#full-text-search) for details.

### Data plane region selection

When creating a new Braintrust organization, you can now choose to host its [data plane](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#data-plane-region) in the EU or the US. After creating an organization, you cannot change its region. For details, see [Create an organization](https://docs.braintrust.dev/admin/organizations.md#create-an-organization).

### `estimated_cost()` SQL function

SQL now includes an `estimated_cost()` scalar function that returns the estimated cost of a span in US dollars. It resolves to the pre-computed `metrics.estimated_cost` value when available, and falls back to computing cost from token metrics and registered model pricing when it isn't. Unlike the raw `metrics.estimated_cost` field, `estimated_cost()` works across spans, traces, and summary shapes, and can be used inside aggregates like `sum(estimated_cost())`. See [SQL functions](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/functions.md#sql-functions) for details.

### SQL subqueries

SQL queries now support subqueries in the `FROM` clause. Write `FROM (<inner query>) AS <alias>` to use the result of one query as the data source for another. The alias is required. The most common pattern is aggregating in the inner query and filtering on the aggregated result in the outer query. Subqueries can be nested to multiple levels. See [Subqueries](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/sql/query-structure.md#subqueries) for syntax, constraints, and examples.

### Sandboxes for agent evals

Use sandboxes when your eval runs custom agent code that can't be expressed as a playground prompt. Unlike remote evals — which require running a dev server — sandboxes let you push your eval once and then run it from the playground on demand. Supports AWS Lambda (Python and TypeScript) and Modal (TypeScript, custom container). See [Test complex agents](https://docs.braintrust.dev/evaluate/remote-evals.md) for details.

> [!WARNING]
>
> Sandboxes are in [public preview](https://docs.braintrust.dev/feature-lifecycle.md). The API, configuration, and behavior are likely to change before general availability. Requires a [Pro or Enterprise plan](https://docs.braintrust.dev/plans-and-limits.md).

### pi integration

Braintrust now integrates with [pi](https://pi.dev), a minimal terminal coding harness. Install the [`@braintrust/pi-extension`](https://github.com/braintrustdata/braintrust-pi-extension) package to automatically trace pi sessions, turns, LLM calls, and tool executions to Braintrust. The extension also supports attaching pi sessions under existing parent traces for end-to-end workflow observability. See the [pi integration guide](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/developer-tools/pi.md) for setup instructions.

### OTel attribute deduplication

OTel ingestion previously stored each recognized OTel attribute twice on a span: once as the raw attribute on `metadata` (such as `gen_ai.input.messages`, `ai.prompt.messages`, or `llm.input_messages`), and again in the structured field Braintrust mapped it into (such as `input`, `output`, or `metrics`). Braintrust now keeps only the structured copy. This reduces cost for OTel-heavy workloads.

For Braintrust-hosted organizations, this change is enabled by default. You can preserve raw attributes on a specific span by setting the `braintrust.otel.preserve_attributes` attribute to `true` on it. For details, see [Why are some attributes missing from metadata?](https://docs.braintrust.dev/integrations/sdk-integrations/opentelemetry/attributes.md#why-are-some-attributes-missing-from-metadata)

> [!WARNING]
>
> **Breaking change**: this change can break SQL queries, automations, or dashboards that read raw OTel attributes (like `gen_ai.input.messages`) directly from `metadata`. Update them to read from the structured field instead (`input`, `output`, `metrics`, etc.). If you need raw attributes preserved on specific spans, set the `braintrust.otel.preserve_attributes` attribute to `true` on those spans in your OTel instrumentation.

### `bt` CLI releases

* [v0.7.1](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.7.1) - Added [`--ca-cert <PATH>`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/overview#global-flags) global flag (env: `BRAINTRUST_CA_CERT`) for custom PEM CA bundles, useful for self-hosted deployments with private TLS certificates. The Braintrust SDK (`braintrust`, `autoevals`, `@braintrust/*`) is now [bundled into function archives](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/functions.md) by default for both JS and Python, so deployed functions are self-contained. Added `--disable-reconciliation` flag to [`bt topics config topic-map set`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/topics) to force fresh topic map generation without referencing the previously saved report.
* [v0.7.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.7.0) - Added [`--param key=value`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval) flag to `bt eval` for passing runtime parameter values into evaluators that declare a parameters schema. Multi-evaluator commands filter params per-evaluator so unrecognized keys are silently dropped. [`bt setup`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/setup) now supports `gemini` as a coding agent target, symlinking `.gemini/skills` to `.agents/skills/braintrust/SKILL.md`. `bt setup` now displays the created API key after setup completes. Minimum Node.js version for TypeScript evals updated to 18.19.0+ or 20.6.0+ (Bun 1.0+ and Deno with Node compat also supported).
* [v0.6.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.6.0) - Added [`--first N` and `--sample N [--sample-seed S]`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/eval) flags to `bt eval` for running a subset of eval data as a non-final smoke run. Summary output now includes `runMode`, `isFinal`, and `runLabel` fields. **Breaking change:** `bt` no longer automatically loads `.env` files. Set environment variables explicitly before running `bt` commands. [`bt setup`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/setup) now handles authentication and API key creation interactively, simplifying first-time onboarding. Improved `bt setup mcp` experience for MCP server configuration.
* [v0.5.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.5.0) - Added [`bt topics`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/topics) command group for managing Topics automations, including `status`, `poke`, `rewind`, `open`, and `config` subcommands. Auth resolution order updated: explicit `--profile` now takes top priority, followed by `--api-key`/`BRAINTRUST_API_KEY`, then `BRAINTRUST_PROFILE` as a distinct step. `bt setup` now detects non-interactive environments (CI, agents) and falls back gracefully when no TTY is available. `bt functions push` now rejects Python bundle paths containing whitespace characters.
* [v0.4.0](https://github.com/braintrustdata/bt/releases/tag/v0.4.0) - Added [`bt functions push`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/functions) for uploading TypeScript and Python function definitions to Braintrust, with esbuild bundling for TypeScript and source collection for Python. Added [`bt functions pull`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/functions) for downloading function definitions from Braintrust to local files. Enhanced [`bt setup instrument`](https://docs.braintrust.dev/reference/cli/setup) with a full agentic SDK installation workflow — language detection, exact version pinning, LLM client instrumentation, app verification, and Braintrust permalink output — supporting Python, TypeScript, Go, Java, Ruby, and C#. The eval runner's SSE `start` event now uses camelCase keys (`projectName`, `experimentName`, `projectId`, `experimentId`, `projectUrl`, `experimentUrl`); the previous snake\_case keys are no longer emitted. Added `parent` field support in the eval request payload for attaching eval results to an existing trace span.

Eval trace structure also changed: each scorer now runs in its own named child span under the eval span, with scorer-specific `span_attributes` and full task context attached. Failed eval tasks now keep `input`, `expected`, `metadata`, and `origin` on the eval span, and omit `output_json` instead of writing `null`. This is a breaking change for teams that inspect raw eval span structure.
